# Dra Thal Gyur — English translation

## Chapter 1

<a id="dtg-000001"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000001 -->
༅།

[^G-U00001]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000002"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000002 -->
Here is the text named the Jewel-Producing Tantra of the Great All-Penetrating Word. [N-001](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-001)

[^G-U00002]

Earlier notes: [N-001](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-001).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000003"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000003 -->
༄༅།

[^G-U00003]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000004"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000004 -->
Here is the Tantra of the Great All-Penetrating Word, Which Brings Forth Jewels. [N-T01](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t01)

[^G-U00004]

Earlier notes: [N-T01](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t01).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000005"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000005 -->
gaṃgarbamatagā. [N-002](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-002) In the language of India:
Ratnakaraśabdamahāprasaṅgatantra nāma—‘The Jewel-Producing Tantra of the Great All-Penetrating Word.’ [N-001](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-001)

[^G-U00005][^G-U00006]

Earlier notes: [N-002](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-002), [N-001](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-001).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000006"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000006 -->
In the language of Tibet:
The Tantra of the Great All-Penetrating Word, Which Brings Forth Jewels.

Earlier notes: [N-T01](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t01).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000007"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000007 -->
To the Blessed One, the great naturally arisen, the supreme teacher,
glorious Samantabhadra, I pay homage.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000008"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000008 -->
[Provisional source caption: Three lineages … beginning … [first line partly unread]. I bow to the teacher Samantabhadra, Father–Mother.]

[^G-A2000-C01-S08]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000009"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000009 -->
[Source heading: Setting out the uncommon introductory setting.] [N-016](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-016)

Earlier notes: [N-016](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-016).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002661"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002661 -->
Thus did I hear at one time:
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002662"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002662 -->
At the beginning of cyclic existence and transcendence of sorrow,
from the naturally arisen and unmade,
there appeared an aggregate of gathered elements:
earth, water, fire, and wind, the four elements;
at the center, wind-mind, causes and conditions.

[^G-U00014]

Earlier notes: [N-003](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-003).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000011"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000011 -->
The immeasurable palace, gathered and generated,
is not contrived by naturally arisen awareness. [N-003](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-003)

Earlier notes: [N-003](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-003).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000012"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000012 -->
Where delusion is entirely pure,
the retinue of methods in which great bliss is gathered—
embodiments and primordial knowing, discerning knowing, and wind—
is indivisible, with no diverse objects of focus.

Earlier notes: [N-003](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-003).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000013"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000013 -->
The teaching of great bliss through the methods of channels and winds
is explained in accordance with each one's experience. [N-003](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-003)

Earlier notes: [N-003](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-003).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000014"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000014 -->
There is no past, future, or present;
there is no division at all in the indivisible.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000015"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000015 -->
All is naturally arising primordial knowing.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000016"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000016 -->
[Source heading: Setting out the common introductory setting.] [N-016](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-016)

[^G-U00029]

Earlier notes: [N-016](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-016).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002663"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002663 -->
Thus did I hear at one time:

[^G-U00030]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002664"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002664 -->
Endowed with Joy, Heaped with Merit—
the earliest realm of beings. [N-004](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-004)

Earlier notes: [N-004](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-004).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000018"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000018 -->
Earth and water, fire and wind—
the four great elements did not appear.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000019"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000019 -->
There was no form assembled from flesh and blood
in that realm of greatly pure self-appearance.

[^G-U00035]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000020"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000020 -->
In the utterly beautiful and expanded
full number of a lotus's stamens,
at the central site endowed with a thousand [stamens], [N-004](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-004)
with all qualities complete,
a form embodiment ripened by four conditions—
a miraculously born body—was assumed
by the teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,
who emanated in the beautiful form of an eight-year-old.

[^G-U00039]

Earlier notes: [N-004](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-004).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000021"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000021 -->
From the three gazes, the teacher's embodiment
appeared with inconceivable light.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000022"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000022 -->
The activity of emanation first arose.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000023"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000023 -->
In the initial setting for the arising of the tantra,
the seeds of the five were held. [N-005](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-005)

[^G-U00048][^G-U00049]

Earlier notes: [N-005](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-005), [N-T15](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000024"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000024 -->
As for the miraculously born beings to be trained,
white in color and free of fleshly form,
with stainless embodiments of unimpeded penetration,
their coarse afflictions were self-purified;
they were not lying dormant,
and the two kinds of manifest circulation were absent. [N-005](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-005)

Earlier notes: [N-005](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-005).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000025"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000025 -->
The five faculties were self-clear;
their distinguishing marks subsided within.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000026"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000026 -->
Coming and going dependent on breath were exhausted.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000027"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000027 -->
Having taken up abiding in the open sky,
they turned about and rose, back and forth.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000028"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000028 -->
Their walking, standing, and lying down,
their movements of stirring and running,
were nowhere other than in the open sky itself.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000029"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000029 -->
Light arose from themselves and appeared;
the light of the sun, moon, and so forth did not appear.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000030"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000030 -->
Unclothed, with a gentle manner,
beautiful and graceful,
at ease, with consciousness unimpaired,
as though abiding in the first meditative absorption,
they were free of manifest doing and agency.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000031"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000031 -->
Like the transformative agency of formless karma,
they did not appear through constituents together with deterioration. [N-006](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-006)

Earlier notes: [N-006](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-006).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000032"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000032 -->
In particular, from the lotus of the core,
the forms, one thousand and one in number,
like rainbows and illusions,
were the form embodiments of the buddhas.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000033"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000033 -->
Distinct among them was the teacher's form,
with immeasurable patterned rays of light.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000034"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000034 -->
I, the divine youth Joy-Maker,
had assumed a body complete in mastery.

[^G-U00080]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000035"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000035 -->
In the abode of the light rays of the teacher's speech,
I found bliss through intrinsic nature. [N-T05](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t05)

Earlier notes: [N-T05](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t05).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000036"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000036 -->
Then, through the blessing of his activity,
sixty-four stars of light
appeared as an array of forms in the open sky,
without having been made by anyone.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000037"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000037 -->
In particular, the seventeen utmost refinements, [N-007](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-007)
for the completion of the sacred teaching—
the actual teaching of embodiment, speech, and awakened mind, [N-T04](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04)
together with vajras and embodied forms—
had not been made by anyone.

Earlier notes: [N-007](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-007), [N-T04](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000038"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000038 -->
As self-appearance from the primordial outset,
in accordance with each one's karma and fortune,
they appeared in the very golden core,
not simultaneously to everyone.

[^G-U00092][^G-U00093]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000039"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000039 -->
Thus, like the heart, they are secret.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000040"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000040 -->
Since the ultimate supreme spiritual accomplishments
do not involve exertion and effort,
whoever encounters this is certain to be liberated.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000041"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000041 -->
Thus the Great Perfection is free from doing and effort. [N-T08](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t08)

Earlier notes: [N-T08](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t08).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000042"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000042 -->
It does not arise from other exertion.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000043"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000043 -->
Buddhas beyond expression
have paid homage from the primordial outset
to the dharma embodiment, in which it was certainly transmitted.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000044"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000044 -->
To make it appear to those with the appropriate karma,
the seed of emanation arises.

[^G-U00105]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000045"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000045 -->
From that actual teaching,
this magical display of reflected forms,
which no one can express,
[was proclaimed] from the open sky, undivided from space: [N-008](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-008)
even before all phrases,
from still above the ‘nine, two, and four,’ [N-008](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-008)
it was proclaimed in the melody of Great Brahmā.

Earlier notes: [N-008](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-008).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000046"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000046 -->
Then, from the space of the nature of phenomena,
through the speech of the Great All-Pervader,
through blessing, it was displayed as its own sound. [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

Earlier notes: [N-008](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-008), [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000047"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000047 -->
Then, from space free of directions,
from the melodious sound of the kalaviṅka,
this very king of tantras,
which gathers the quintessence of all teachings,
even before all phrases,
was drawn from three hundred and sixty
sounds and spoken by the teacher.

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000048"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000048 -->
Then, the sounds of the individual elements,
from the agencies of heat and cold—
eight thousand individual melodious sounds—
appeared blessed by the teacher.

[^G-U00126]

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000049"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000049 -->
In particular, in the speech that distinguishes,
the Ground from which words and phrases arise [N-T09](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t09)
was taught in sixty limbs by the teacher,
who spoke to the gathered retinue of self-appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-T09](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t09).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000050"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000050 -->
What was the enlightened intent of this?

Earlier notes: [N-009](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-009).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000051"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000051 -->
The manner of speaking had three enlightened intents:
antidote, condensation, and the meaning for the faculties. [N-009](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-009)

Earlier notes: [N-009](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-009).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000052"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000052 -->
The phrases were condensed and made to appear.

Earlier notes: [N-009](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-009).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000053"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000053 -->
No other phrases expressing their subject matter
are spoken by the elements of the three times.

Earlier notes: [N-009](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-009).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000054"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000054 -->
Words and phrases were definitively condensed into names;
even the kalaviṅka's sounds,
for drawing in, engaging, and being pleasing,
were condensed from twelve clarifiers of word and meaning
into four or five expressive phrases. [N-010](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-010)

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03), [N-010](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-010).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000055"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000055 -->
Definitively condensed into the teacher's speech,
there were a thousand and five in Brahmā's melody,
a hundred and three definite expressive forms,
definitively condensed into thirty lengtheners.

Earlier notes: [N-010](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-010).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000056"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000056 -->
There were sixteen clarifiers among them,
sixty-two limbs of ‘realization-clearing,’
and forty-two limbs free from conceptual elaborations. [N-010](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-010)

Earlier notes: [N-010](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-010).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000057"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000057 -->
The two classes specially condensed
are the foundation of words and phrases.

Earlier notes: [N-010](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-010).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000058"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000058 -->
What is the enlightened intent of that?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000059"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000059 -->
Through each one's karmic magic,
the roots of training—‘fourteen, two’— [N-011](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-011)
those skilled in designs
bring forth emanations and so on
and know the meanings of constant display. [N-011](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-011)

[^G-U00156]

Earlier notes: [N-011](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-011).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000060"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000060 -->
In the magical display of what was thus spoken,
even before all tantras,
from the root words and phrases of the All-Penetrating,
all certainly arose and proliferated as teaching.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000061"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000061 -->
The sources of the sacred teaching were established;
by its being spoken, cyclic existence is made to subside.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000062"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000062 -->
Its qualities are beyond thought.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000063"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000063 -->
At the beginning of the emanated teaching,
it increased through the arising of this.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000064"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000064 -->
At the outset, when all bodies were miraculously born,
in the age of immeasurable lifespans,
for miraculously born beings,
intrinsic nature was undivided and perfectly pure.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000065"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000065 -->
All was expressed as indivisible in essence;
words and phrases were bliss-awareness.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000066"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000066 -->
The dharma embodiment too was without cause;
the complete enjoyment embodiment spread out, all-pervading; [N-T07](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t07)
the Ground for the emanation embodiment's elaboration was complete.

Earlier notes: [N-T07](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t07), [N-T09](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t09).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000067"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000067 -->
Without division, they entered balanced union,
and in the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature
abided together in a single mandala.

Earlier notes: [N-T08](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t08).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000068"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000068 -->
Thus they were indivisible, of a single condition.

[^G-U00179]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000069"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000069 -->
Then, one with this and not separate, [N-012](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-012)
from among the retinue whose intrinsic nature was pure,
the Lord of Gods made this request:
‘Ema! O, O, Blessed One!

[^G-U00180]

Earlier notes: [N-012](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-012).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000070"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000070 -->
This sacred appearance,
this inconceivable gathering—
the intrinsic nature of its phrases is thus:
What is the enlightened intent of the four elements? [N-013](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-013)

[^G-U00184][^G-U00187]

Earlier notes: [N-013](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-013).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000071"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000071 -->
How are all the meanings of the limbs pursued?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000072"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000072 -->
What is the meaning of its enlightened intent?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000073"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000073 -->
How extensive is the expression of words?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000074"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000074 -->
How many sites of penetration are there?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000075"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000075 -->
What are transformation and its agents? [N-013](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-013) [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20)

[^G-U00192]

Earlier notes: [N-013](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-013), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000076"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000076 -->
What clearly expresses the chapters?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000077"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000077 -->
Who desires this perfection?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000078"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000078 -->
What is the meaning of the arising of the introductory setting?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000079"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000079 -->
How many remaining teachings are there?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000080"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000080 -->
From what does the cause of destruction arise?

[^G-U00197]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000081"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000081 -->
After destruction, from where does abiding arise?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000082"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000082 -->
Who maintains it in the interval?

Earlier notes: [N-T15](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000083"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000083 -->
Finally, into what does this very thing turn?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000084"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000084 -->
What are the body and its limbs like?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000085"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000085 -->
What do the elements desire?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000086"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000098 -->

<a id="dtg-000104"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000104 -->

<a id="dtg-000105"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000105 -->

<a id="dtg-000106"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000106 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000107"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000107 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000108"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000108 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000109"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000109 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000110"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000110 -->
By what is the activity of relocation carried out?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000111"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000111 -->
What distinguishes the aware from matter?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000112"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000112 -->
What method reverses the key points themselves?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000113"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000113 -->
By what is the enlightened intent of the tenet system established?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000114"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000114 -->
What is the enlightened intent concerning the ultimate?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000115"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000115 -->
How many stages of accomplishment are there?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000116"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000116 -->
How do the natural states abide?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000117"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000117 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000118"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000118 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000119"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000119 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000120"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000120 -->
How are the outer elemental signs discerned?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000121"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000121 -->
What are the junctures of time and teaching?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000122"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000122 -->
What is the rite of conferring empowerment?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000123"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000123 -->
What are the sacred pledges and vows like?

Earlier notes: [N-T50](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t50), [N-T51](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t51).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000124"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000124 -->
What subject matter is taught through phrases?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000125"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000125 -->
Beyond phrases, by what are elaborations cut?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000126"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000126 -->
How is this applied to the key point of direct perception?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000127"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000127 -->
By what are cyclic existence and transcendence of sorrow separated in the secret preliminary? [N-T11](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t11)

Earlier notes: [N-T11](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t11).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000128"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000128 -->
How many aspects of activity are there in retaining wind?

Earlier notes: [N-T15](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000129"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000129 -->
How many stages are there in training to hold ordinary mind? [N-T15](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15)

Earlier notes: [N-T15](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000130"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000130 -->
What are the outer, inner, and secret signs of death?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000131"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000131 -->
By what is viewing brought to its endpoint?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000132"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000132 -->
What are the life-pillars of cultivation?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000133"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000133 -->
How is activity applied?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000134"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000134 -->
How extensive is the site of attaining the result?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000135"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000135 -->
By what is the flowing stream of cyclic existence cut?

[^G-U00252]

Earlier notes: [N-T17](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000136"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000136 -->
What is the taking of nourishment from quintessence? [N-T16](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16)

Earlier notes: [N-T16](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000137"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000137 -->
By what is clothing accomplished?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000138"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000138 -->
How is the flow of the elements cut? [N-T17](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17)

Earlier notes: [N-T17](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000139"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000139 -->
By what are the tastes of the four balanced?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000140"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000140 -->
How do qualities arise in the treasury?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000141"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000141 -->
By what are the noble ones' miraculous powers accomplished?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000142"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000142 -->
How should one enjoy the treasures?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000143"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000143 -->
By what are the five Grounds brought under control?

Earlier notes: [N-T09](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t09).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000144"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000144 -->
I have not previously known these things.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000145"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000145 -->
Lord of appearance, speak to me!
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000146"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000146 -->
By clearly distinguishing their sequence for me,
in the times to come,
all who have faith in and aspire to this
will come to have conviction in this Great Perfection.

Earlier notes: [N-T08](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t08).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000147"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000147 -->
The simple, free from doing, will find great bliss. [N-019](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-019)

Earlier notes: [N-019](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-019).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000148"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000148 -->
When cyclic existence is exhausted to its very end,
they will find the entrance to the path of transcendence of sorrow.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000149"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000149 -->
Therefore, whatever I have asked,
clearly distinguish its sequence and speak!’
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000150"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000150 -->
Then, the all-pervading lord, the hero, [N-020](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-020)
though words and phrases were not proclaimed as names,
in space whose intrinsic nature is uncontrived,
without being written or arranged,
possessed a melody with sixty limbs.

[^G-U00272]

Earlier notes: [N-020](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-020).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000151"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000151 -->
Like a mere unwritten design,
to the faculties endowed with luster,
it certainly appeared in direct perception.

Earlier notes: [N-020](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-020).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000152"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000152 -->
Also in the sound of Great Brahmā:
‘Indra, sovereign of the gods, listen!

Earlier notes: [N-020](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-020).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000153"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000153 -->
Having thoroughly disciplined body, speech, and mental faculty, [N-021](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-021)
abandon wrong activity and thinking.

Earlier notes: [N-021](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-021).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000154"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000154 -->
Having purified the faculties and so forth,
listen with ordinary mind from which stains have been abandoned.

Earlier notes: [N-021](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-021).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000155"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000155 -->
Ema! How wonderful and astonishing!
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000156"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000156 -->
This, not spoken by the buddhas,
surpasses other tantras.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000157"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000157 -->
So that there may be no return to abiding in the three realms,
this most secret, definitive command,
for increasing the core of the teaching,
is the very highest summit of the vehicles.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000158"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000158 -->
It gathers, through meaning, the mirror of the teachings;
it is the commentary on the command, the heart of the pith instructions,
the great perfection whose intrinsic nature is secret,
the heart-commentary, the eye of transmission,
the most secret key, the inner depth of the view,
the training ground of activity, the key point of cultivation,
the cutter of the succession of wombs, the sweeper of the lower destinies. [N-022](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-022)

Earlier notes: [N-022](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-022), [N-T49](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t49), [N-T23](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t23), [N-T17](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000159"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000159 -->
I will explain the supreme secret tantra;
retinue who also hold the vajra, listen!

Earlier notes: [N-022](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-022).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000160"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000160 -->
In the phrases I elaborate,
there is not a particle of the dust of delusion.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000161"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000161 -->
The teachings taught from this point onward
are certain to be accomplished and to bring forth meaning;
without doubt they yield the result.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000162"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000162 -->
Therefore I teach the core of the teaching.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000163"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000163 -->
O, O, Joy-Maker—take hold of this!’
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000164"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000164 -->
As the phrases of the questions were clearly distinguished,
from the All-Pervader's voice,
emanations of letters such as these,
in space whose intrinsic nature is uncontrived,
unarranged phrases, naturally arisen,
appeared in the manner of questions and replies.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002665"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002665 -->
Accomplished through the settling of the basic condition, [N-023](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-023)
they appeared thus in the Ground from which phrases arise:

[^G-U00316]

Earlier notes: [N-023](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-023), [N-T09](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t09).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002666"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002666 -->
[Source annotation at U00317; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]
[Source annotation at U00318; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U00317][^G-U00318]

Earlier notes: [N-024](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-024).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000166"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000166 -->
[Source heading: First reply.]

[^G-SCAN-CH1-LAYER-00318]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000167"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000167 -->
The purposes of the elements are as follows:
Water gathers the pure extract
and performs the activity of separating the residue
in each individual body.

[^G-U00319]

Earlier notes: [N-T65](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t65).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000168"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000168 -->
Recognize its purpose, measure, and number.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000169"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000169 -->
Earth supports the foundation
and performs the activities of forming and establishing.

Earlier notes: [N-T15](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000170"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000170 -->
Through ripening, it generates channels and so forth.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000171"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000171 -->
Know its purpose in the conventional sphere.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000172"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000172 -->
Fire clears away the elements, [N-025](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-025)
and performs the activities of ripening and clarifying.

[^G-U00328]

Earlier notes: [N-025](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-025).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000173"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000173 -->
By blazing and spreading, it generates the eyes and so forth.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000174"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000174 -->
It makes one know its purpose in the forms of warmth.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000175"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000175 -->
Wind supports and lifts,
and performs the activities of stirring and swaying.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000176"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000176 -->
By vibrating and fluttering, it sustains the body.

Earlier notes: [N-T15](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000177"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000177 -->
Know its purpose in the abiding of ordinary mind.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000178"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000178 -->
The great mandala of these four elements
acts as the cause of the formation of the body and so forth.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000179"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000179 -->
In the interval, it brings about abiding and maintenance;
at the end, it performs all the activities of destruction.

Earlier notes: [N-T15](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000180"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000180 -->
The great elements, having a single cause,
appear as the masters of diverse functions.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000181"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000181 -->
Thus their purposes, for self and other,
are skilled in the meaning of completing what is to be done.

[^G-U00343]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000182"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000182 -->
[Source heading: Second reply.]

[^G-U00344]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000183"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000183 -->
The meaning of the limbs is as follows:
The clear sequence of phrases expressing
the knowing of the buddhas
is explained through the presentation of the title and through the text,
so that conceptual minds of the gradual and simultaneous types
may realize it. [N-T18](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t18)

[^G-U00345]

Earlier notes: [N-T18](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t18).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000184"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000184 -->
The text is taught in two aspects:
the establishment of the body itself,
and its extensive division through the meanings of the limbs.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000185"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000185 -->
The body is the two aspects of the introductory setting.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000186"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000186 -->
In order to surpass others in particular,
it is applied to the uncommon body.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000187"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000187 -->
In order to accord in approach with other vehicles,
the common introductory setting
is taught to inspire conviction
in the compiler's own retinue.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000188"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000188 -->
Arising from the Ground of training as that which trains,
it makes the teaching abide.

Earlier notes: [N-T09](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t09).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000189"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000189 -->
The limbs are examples and the meanings of phrases.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000190"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000190 -->
Through the six chapters of the text itself,
the meanings of the phrases, just as they abide,
are shown like opening the interior of a body
and revealing its solid and hollow organs and so forth.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000191"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000191 -->
The meanings of the phrases have two aspects,
spoken as questions and replies.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000192"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000192 -->
The replies are explained in two ways: briefly and extensively.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000193"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000193 -->
To suit the inclinations of the individuals who are to be trained,
diverse elaborations are the meaning.

[^G-U00372]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000194"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000194 -->
[Source heading: Third reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000195"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000195 -->
Ema! In the definite arising of the tantras,
there are two aspects of enlightened intent.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000196"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000196 -->
For those whose conceptual mind concerns awareness as self-appearance, [N-026](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026)
the meaning of the tantra's title is distinguished.

Earlier notes: [N-026](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000197"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000197 -->
Through the three—sign, word, and meaning—
direct perception of the nature of phenomena is made the path.

Earlier notes: [N-026](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000198"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000198 -->
Once the flow of samsara in the three realms has been cut,
because there is no turning back again and again,
one knows the defining characteristic of exhaustion.

Earlier notes: [N-T17](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000199"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000199 -->
For conceptual minds that focus on objects,
the texts of the continua are distinguished.

Earlier notes: [N-026](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000200"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000200 -->
The definitive crucial point corresponding to each inclination
is applied to each one individually.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000201"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000201 -->
If it is fully realized without separation,
one does not abide in samsara.

Earlier notes: [N-026](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000202"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000202 -->
People who delight in elaboration itself,
having trained in diverse methods,
also take pride in their respective accomplishments. [N-026](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026)

Earlier notes: [N-026](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000203"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000203 -->
The pith instructions on the meaning of the crucial points of enlightened intent
should be known in their respective contexts.

Earlier notes: [N-026](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026), [N-T49](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t49).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000204"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000204 -->
[Source heading: Fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000205"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000205 -->
Through this very king of continua,
the varieties of its definitive title
are taught; as for the pith instructions on the meaning of perfection,
the phrases alone are examined and distinguished with certainty.

Earlier notes: [N-T49](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t49).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000206"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000206 -->
The syllables, too, in accord with their meanings,
are brought to the face of meaning in this way:
This manner of expressing sound [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)
has not been explained in other continua.

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000207"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000207 -->
Ema! How greatly wonderful!
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000208"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000208 -->
I will elaborate it; listen to me.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000209"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000209 -->
The occasions of sound have five aspects:
Brahmā, the All-Pervader, the kalaviṅka,
the sounds of the elements, and the teacher's speech.

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000210"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000210 -->
Through these five, in the phenomena that arise,
one engages freely in great play.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000211"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000211 -->
For the five sites of Brahmā,
there are two hundred for each,
and one acting as a further site for each.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000212"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000212 -->
Since there are a thousand and five further sites,
merely by knowing this
one is brought into contact with the Blessed One.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000213"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000213 -->
As for the All-Pervader, from further sites,
by two and five and ‘six, three,’ [N-027](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-027)
the cause of the activity of parting the side-locks—
when the grouping with its marks is complete,
there are a hundred for each of these. [N-027](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-027)

Earlier notes: [N-027](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-027).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000214"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000214 -->
From the aspects of the magic that definitely distinguishes,
if one knows how to express each of those numbers,
higher knowing is accomplished in one's continuum.

Earlier notes: [N-027](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-027), [N-T19](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t19).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000215"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000215 -->
Through the melodious sounds of the kalaviṅka,
there are a hundred and twenty for the body,
and likewise for speech and ordinary mind—
the same number as above is spoken in definite expression.

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000216"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000216 -->
When the worldly gods,
likewise the gods beyond the world,
and the ḍākinīs gather,
if one knows the distinctions of their sounds,
one knows their languages.

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000217"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000217 -->
When one definitely engages in the six kinds of higher knowing, [N-T19](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t19)
one knows the meanings of the Bliss-Gone One's commands.

Earlier notes: [N-T19](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t19).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000218"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000218 -->
Through the number of verses,
one is definitively connected with the individual sites.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000219"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000219 -->
Then, in the sound of water too,
eloquent speech is inconceivable.

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000220"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000220 -->
For the skilled, the rite is complete.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000221"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000221 -->
As for the word and meaning of the elements,
through the hero and the hero-goer, [N-028](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-028)
in each of the four—earth, water, fire, and wind—
there are two apiece.

Earlier notes: [N-028](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-028).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000222"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000222 -->
Since the neutral one is empty,
from the distinction between male and female
there are the causal agencies of heat and cold.

Earlier notes: [N-028](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-028).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000223"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000223 -->
There are two thousand for each;
if a yogin knows how to express
the sounds of expression and designation,
then in the languages of all of these,
one delights in listening with ease and well-being.

Earlier notes: [N-028](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-028), [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000224"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000224 -->
Whoever has definitely mastered this
is definitively connected with the six classes;
they appear directly, together with their times.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000225"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000225 -->
The teachers of the past, future, and present,
the buddhas, the Blessed Ones,
with a single melody, in the maṇḍala,
for the appearance of the fortunate,
through the complete purity of the six faculties,
in tens apiece, from the three embodiments, [N-029](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-029)
proliferate in particular from the emanation embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-029](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-029).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000226"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000226 -->
Merely through a ray of that proliferation,
the yogin's stream of conceptual thought ceases.

Earlier notes: [N-029](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-029), [N-T17](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000227"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000227 -->
The sites, distinctions, and domains of their vital cores [N-029](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-029)
are attained, and one assuredly enters seeing and realization.

Earlier notes: [N-029](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-029).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000228"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000228 -->
Thus the purposes of sound
should be known in two aspects:
supreme and common—
the individually distinguishing magic for those with the appropriate karma.

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000229"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000229 -->
For the supreme, the five embodiments are manifestly accomplished.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000230"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000230 -->
Through the ten signs of such accomplishment,
one equals the body of Vajradhara.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000231"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000231 -->
Its qualities cannot be exhausted in words.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000232"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000232 -->
In phenomena and the basic space of phenomena,
in the Ground, path, and result,
one plays in bliss.

Earlier notes: [N-T09](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t09).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000233"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000233 -->
The common purposes are these:
treatises on language and valid cognition,
crafts and various magical skills,
archery and activities not previously trained in are accomplished. [N-030](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-030)

Earlier notes: [N-030](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-030).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000234"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000234 -->
One can act upon the five elements,
prevails over weapons and poison,
and becomes skilled in the sounds of the insentient and the aware,
in signs and conventions.

Earlier notes: [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000235"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000235 -->
In brief, one comes to know the treatises on purposes
and the defining characteristics of the commands.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000236"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000236 -->
One distinguishes the varieties of medicines and elixir preparations,
brings ‘penetrating letters’—thal-yig—to perfection, [N-030](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-030)
and worldly applications are spontaneously accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-030](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-030).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000237"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000237 -->
Thus the purposes of word and meaning are accomplished.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000238"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000238 -->
The distinguishing magic definitive for the continuum is set apart.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000239"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000239 -->
[Source heading: Fifth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000240"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000240 -->
There are eight aspects of the sites of penetration:
world systems and buddha-fields,
faults in assertions and the sites of faults,
the activity of projection and application,
what is acknowledged and the crucial point of going—eight in all. [N-031](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-031) [N-T21](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21)

Earlier notes: [N-031](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-031), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20), [N-T21](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000241"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000241 -->
These are held to be the sites of penetration.

Earlier notes: [N-031](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-031).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000242"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000242 -->
Regarding a world system: the times of its formation,
abiding, and destruction;
emptiness, causes and conditions;
its manner of establishment and its measure of extension. [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032)

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000243"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000243 -->
The buddha-fields have thirteen aspects:
beneath the world here
is the realm called Melody of Penetration,
its extent difficult to measure, utterly beautiful.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000244"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000244 -->
Its causes, conditions, and intrinsic nature,
the teacher's teaching and its retinue,
above, below, and in the directions and intermediate directions—
its length and breadth are pleasing, well-arrayed and adorned.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000245"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000245 -->
Then, six worlds below,
that called Holder of Penetration
is wheel-shaped, with unimpeded penetration.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000246"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000246 -->
The teacher's teaching is truth itself;
well-adorned in its array, it is the second.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000247"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000247 -->
Three worlds to the right of this,
that called Protector of Penetration
is entirely adorned with emeralds.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000248"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000248 -->
Its light and colors are definitely clear;
it is a field where the teacher's teaching trains beings,
each with their own karma and characteristics.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000249"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000249 -->
Then, six worlds above,
that called Spreading Penetration
resembles the shape of an opened lotus,
very extensive and level.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000250"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000250 -->
Diverse light blazes; its colors
appear white and red, intermingling.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000251"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000251 -->
Coarse reflected forms do not appear.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000252"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000252 -->
The sources of the teacher's teaching expand;
with the age complete, it is called Penetration.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000253"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000253 -->
Above this world,
still higher, beyond nineteen worlds,
is that called Club of Penetration.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000254"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000254 -->
Its region is utterly complete and delightful. [N-033](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-033)

[^G-U00534]

Earlier notes: [N-033](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-033).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000255"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000255 -->
With diverse small jewels,
it is definitively adorned in the manner of heaps.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000256"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000256 -->
The teacher's teaching possesses the two completions;
it is an array of garlands of skulls.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000257"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000257 -->
In the world's northeastern intermediate direction,
that called Flow of Penetration
resembles a stūpa in shape, with beautiful tiers,
entirely adorned with five arrays. [N-033](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-033)

[^G-U00542]

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032), [N-033](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-033).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000258"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000258 -->
It is the Ground from which diverse sounds and melodies arise.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000259"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000259 -->
The teacher's teaching certainly arises at its time.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000260"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000260 -->
Twenty-one worlds above it,
is that called Fully Pervaded by Penetration.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000261"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000261 -->
In the intrinsic nature of a pure object of focus,
it appears with unimpeded penetration, without outside or inside.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000262"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000262 -->
The teacher's teaching displays arising;
its active causes and conditions: the All-Pervader. [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032)

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000263"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000263 -->
To the left, in this world's southeast,
beyond six world systems,
is that called Sound of Penetration,
thickly covered with moving pennants.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000264"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000264 -->
Victory banners, parasols, and so forth are raised.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000265"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000265 -->
With showers of scattered flowers and fragrance,
the teacher and the teaching
are surrounded by hosts of beautifying reflected forms.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000266"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000266 -->
The play of bliss arises.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000267"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000267 -->
To the right, in this world's southwest,
six worlds away and below, is Wind of Penetration.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000268"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000268 -->
Swaying and active in the open sky,
moving back and forth and floating,
it brings forth sounds through movement.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000269"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000269 -->
The teacher's teaching is stirred by wind.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000270"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000270 -->
There are three engagements endowed with five. [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032)

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000271"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000271 -->
Thirteen worlds stacked above it,
is that called Jewel Penetration,
adorned with auspicious suns,
ornamented with the qualities of desire,
beautiful with magical designs and letters.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000272"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000272 -->
The teacher's teaching is complete in reflected forms,
beautiful with ‘lda-ldi’ lotus eyes. [N-034](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034)

Earlier notes: [N-034](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000273"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000273 -->
Above this world's northwestern intermediate direction,
in the sixth world system,
is that called Gathering Penetration,
with goddesses whose offerings are complete,
filled with light and the fragrance of sandalwood.

[^G-U00578]

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000274"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000274 -->
The teacher's teaching is equal in its parts,
surrounded by beautiful forms, sixteen years of age,
utterly youthful.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000275"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000275 -->
Nine worlds below it,
is that called Conch-Endowed Deity of Penetration,
faithful and intoxicated by sweet fragrance,
with flashing, intensely blazing light,
densely filled with intensely tremulous brightness. [N-034](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034)

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032), [N-034](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000276"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000276 -->
The teacher and the teaching,
its retinue and enjoyments, are complete.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000277"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000277 -->
In the sixth world from here,
is that called Star Penetration.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000278"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000278 -->
Its form of stacked wheels
is completely piled up in fives and nines.

Earlier notes: [N-032](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000279"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000279 -->
It is adorned with awnings and hanging garlands. [N-034](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034)

Earlier notes: [N-034](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000280"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000280 -->
Thunder roars and red tongues of lightning issue forth.

[^G-U00594]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000281"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000281 -->
The teacher's teaching is surrounded by authoritative transmissions.

Earlier notes: [N-T23](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t23).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000282"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000282 -->
Garlands of arising light and the faces of nāgas [N-035](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-035)
illuminate all directions and intermediate directions.

[^G-U00596]

Earlier notes: [N-035](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-035).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000283"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000283 -->
[Source heading: Sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000284"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000284 -->
The phrases of one's own and others' assertions
are decisively resolved through one's own phrases. [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036)

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000285"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000285 -->
To bring one's own position to a definite conclusion,
through authoritative transmission, reasoning, and pith instructions,
remove faults, establish it, and abandon contradictions.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20), [N-T23](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t23), [N-T49](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t49).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000286"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000286 -->
Abandon the three faults and dispel doubt.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000287"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000287 -->
Take up the four logical reasons and an assertion.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000288"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000288 -->
Seize the fortress of abiding and flee to the side. [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036)

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000289"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000289 -->
Draw a wheel in the center of the four directions.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000290"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000290 -->
Establish a logical reason free from taking sides.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000291"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000291 -->
Against others, debate the definitive application of a consequence. [N-T21](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21)

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036), [N-T21](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000292"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000292 -->
Surround assertion, what is to be proved, and logical reason
with projections, three by three.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000293"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000293 -->
The skilled apply these with their defining characteristics complete.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000294"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000294 -->
This is the subject matter established for oneself and others.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000295"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000295 -->
Faults are of two kinds: one's own and others'.

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000296"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000296 -->
Know one's own faults oneself.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000297"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000297 -->
In assertion, logical reason, and what is to be proved,
identify each individual fault
and bring the qualities to completion.

Earlier notes: [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000298"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000298 -->
Know the other's qualities;
let the skilled examine that person's faults.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000299"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000299 -->
From the mere phrases expressing word and meaning,
through the defining characteristics of connection and nonconnection,
reckon the consequences for the pervaded meanings. [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036)

Earlier notes: [N-036](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036), [N-T21](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000300"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000300 -->
[Source heading: Seventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000301"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000301 -->
Projection is wind, the activity of transference.

Earlier notes: [N-037](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-037).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000302"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000302 -->
It mounts phrases and angles. [N-037](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-037)

[^G-U00626]

Earlier notes: [N-037](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-037).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000303"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000303 -->
Above and below the wheel of logical reasons,
it penetrates the reverse and the angles.

Earlier notes: [N-037](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-037), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000304"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000304 -->
Through the modes of subject and property, [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20)
engaging two by two, it projects.

Earlier notes: [N-037](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-037), [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000305"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000305 -->
Wind also projects the afflictions.

Earlier notes: [N-038](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-038).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000306"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000306 -->
Through the impure [wind]—primordial knowing. [N-038](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-038)

[^G-U00632]

Earlier notes: [N-038](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-038).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000307"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000307 -->
With an object of focus for application, maintain one's own position.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000308"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000308 -->
Subject, assertion, and logical reason,
indicated by an example, form one application.

Earlier notes: [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000309"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000309 -->
Know the subject's connection through the object.

Earlier notes: [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000310"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000310 -->
Examine the faults of an assertion before making it.

Earlier notes: [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000311"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000311 -->
Through logical reasons, reckon the meaning of the connection.

Earlier notes: [N-T20](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000312"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000312 -->
Through an example, establish the phenomena that appear.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000313"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000313 -->
Tenets depend on the conceptual mind,
through the expressiveness of knowing in oneself and others.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000314"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000314 -->
From what is acknowledged, consequences follow. [N-T21](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21)

Earlier notes: [N-T21](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000315"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000315 -->
The activities of doing and of analysis,
differences between phrases and realization,
the various junctures at which causes and conditions ripen,
wishing to go and having gone,
with different particular modes of connection—
what is called ‘gone’: the objects,
paths and sites, phrases and engagement,
mindfulness, the actual, and what is not an entity, [N-039](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-039)
abiding, arising, and acting, [N-039](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-039)
paths with reversal and paths without reversal,
what has been accomplished and the act of accomplishing—
these are the causes of what is done and what does it.

[^G-U00651]

Earlier notes: [N-039](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-039).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000316"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000316 -->
[Source heading: Eighth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000317"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000317 -->
To draw the definitive reasons together at their conclusion:
transformation occurs through the magical display of the elements.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000318"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000318 -->
Because seasons change through time,
the yogin's experience and knowing also change.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000319"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000319 -->
Let the skilled discern the times of change.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000320"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000320 -->
Through the course of wind, fire, earth, and water,
fire, wind, earth, and water revolve in ‘four, two.’ [N-040](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040)

Earlier notes: [N-040](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000321"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000321 -->
Earth, wind, water, and fire—two agents of change.

Earlier notes: [N-040](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000322"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000322 -->
Wind, earth, fire, and water, from five parts,
transform and act as the body's Ground.

Earlier notes: [N-040](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000323"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000323 -->
Sites of emptiness—there are eight.

Earlier notes: [N-040](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000324"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000324 -->
The expressiveness of knowing that brings transformation:
proliferation, coiling, and spreading,
gathering and signs—regarding these,
drawing in, abiding, and transforming,
wind, wind, earth, earth, fire, fire, water—
from each one's sixteen functions,
pervading, entering, causing abiding, and guarding.

Earlier notes: [N-040](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000325"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000325 -->
‘Transmission-pairing,’ single-sided pairing, and one common grouping [N-040](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040)
are the agents of change that make one heroic.

Earlier notes: [N-040](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000326"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000326 -->
[Source heading: Ninth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000327"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000327 -->
Clearly distinguishing the two—chapters and their expression— [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041)
the Array of Diversity, the abode of pith instructions,
is the first: definite arising.

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041), [N-T49](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t49).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000328"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000328 -->
It is the Ground, definitely showing what is yet to come.

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000329"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000329 -->
Taking up the Ground together with the introductory setting,
the Array of the Gathered Crucial Points, the support of ordinary mind,
is the second: bringing [it] down upon itself. [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041)

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000330"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000330 -->
It shows what certainly arises from the secret command and the extent of change.

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000331"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000331 -->
The third, the Array of Primordial Knowing,
shows the foundation for indication by words and signs.

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000332"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000332 -->
The fourth, the Array of the Nature of Phenomena,
is the transmission on the nature of ordinary mind, complete in qualities. [N-T22](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t22)

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041), [N-T22](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t22), [N-T23](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t23).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000333"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000333 -->
The fifth is the Array of View and Cultivation,
the crucial point of basic space, complete in freedom from doing.

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000334"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000334 -->
The sixth is the Array of Embodiments,
the secret Ground, complete in qualities.

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000335"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000335 -->
Thus, through these six,
the result of the three embodiments is attained.

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000336"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000336 -->
Know the chapter divisions that show their scope.

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000337"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000337 -->
The treasure from which glorious enlightened intent arises,
also the embodiment of the method of bliss,
prophecy, guidance, and the crucial point of ordinary mind,
protecting beings and oneself, and the wind deity—
the remainder is just that much. [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041)

Earlier notes: [N-041](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000338"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000338 -->
[Source heading: Tenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000339"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000339 -->
Regarding the explanation of ‘perfection’:
phrases of the teaching in which clinging is self-liberated,
a text with neither additions nor omissions,
and completeness without delusion in the meaning.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000340"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000340 -->
The explanation is free from conventions and phrases.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000341"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000341 -->
From the complete aspects of class-formation, [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042)
the languages of the six classes of beings
and all engagements through signs appear.

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000342"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000342 -->
Whatever is transformed by each one's capacity,
is complete in intrinsic nature; thus it is the Great Perfection.

[^G-U00710]

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000343"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000343 -->
The perfection distinguishing appearance and emptiness:
having clearly distinguished the text's phrases and meanings,
it is entirely complete, with nothing remaining.

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000344"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000344 -->
The Ground itself is complete without change.

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000345"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000345 -->
The path is straight and fully complete.

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000346"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000346 -->
The result is complete in the condition in which nothing is to be done.

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000347"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000347 -->
View and cultivation are complete in great freedom from doing.

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000348"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000348 -->
Empowerment, pledges, and vows are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000349"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000349 -->
In brief, everything is complete.

Earlier notes: [N-042](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000350"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000350 -->
[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000351"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000351 -->
True phrases in the introductory setting:
for every individual being trained,
the Ground of training is the five aggregates.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000352"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000352 -->
The introductory setting is what trains that Ground.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000353"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000353 -->
‘Introducing’ refers to expression in phrases.

Earlier notes: [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000354"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000354 -->
‘Ground’ has five aspects.

Earlier notes: [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000355"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000355 -->
The place is held to have three aspects.

Earlier notes: [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000356"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000356 -->
‘Phun’ means gathered together; [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043)
‘Gsum’ is the number [three], that which brings appearance;
‘Tshogs’ is the completeness of qualities.

Earlier notes: [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000357"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000357 -->
The teacher too has three aspects:
‘Phun’ means complete as embodiment;
‘Gsum’ makes the three embodiments appear;
‘Tshogs’ gathers the retinues.

Earlier notes: [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000358"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000358 -->
The retinue too has three aspects:
