‘Phun’ means arrayed as forms;
‘Gsum’ is the number [three], with diversity gathered;
‘Tshogs’ means having faith and gathering.

Earlier notes: [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000359"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000359 -->
The teaching too has three aspects:
‘Phun’ means the quintessence of the innermost core;
‘Gsum’ is the precious collection of scriptures;
‘Tshogs’ liberates individuals.

Earlier notes: [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000360"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000360 -->
Time too is three, in which the four are gathered:
‘Phun’ means all vehicles are complete;
‘Gsum’ is past, present, and future;
‘Tshogs’ is definite completeness.

Earlier notes: [N-043](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000361"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000361 -->
Being uncommon, it is exceptional:
the sign of surpassing all vehicles,
the quintessences gathered into the core,
revealed by the teacher's speech to the retinue,
the foremost teaching, gathering the supremely wondrous.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000362"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000362 -->
As for the causes and conditions of the common introductory setting,
because it accords with others merely in the form of transmission,
it is known as ‘common.’
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000363"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000363 -->
[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000364"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000364 -->
Thus, regarding this very teaching,
the remaining teaching is as follows:
the definite divisions of time are explained—
Endowed with the Core, Endowed with Perfection,
Great Endowment with Perfection,
likewise Endowed with Three and Endowed with Two,
Endowed with Conflict and Great Endowment with Conflict. [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044)

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000365"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000365 -->
Between each of these,
in sixty ages of darkness,
the shoots of the teaching will shift elsewhere.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000366"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000366 -->
In the north, at Endowed with Movement,
the lamp of the teaching will shine clearly.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000367"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000367 -->
A monk called Glorious Protector
will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000368"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000368 -->
His patron, a king
called Jinamitra,
will provide the basis for restoring the teaching.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000369"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000369 -->
It will remain for a thousand and three hundred years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000370"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000370 -->
Then an age of darkness
will remain for sixty [years]. [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044)

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000371"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000371 -->
Then, in the south, at Protector of the Grove,
on an island spacious and beautiful,
the life-pillar of the teaching will descend.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000372"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000372 -->
One called Glory of Joy,
of the class of those with long locks,
will uphold this teaching.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000373"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000373 -->
His patron, of the householder class,
called Accomplishment of Virtue,
will make this very sacred teaching
remain for a hundred years.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000374"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000374 -->
Then the age of darkness
will last a hundred and twenty years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000375"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000375 -->
Then, in the east, at Wind of Bliss,
on the peak of Vajra-Arising Mountain,
this sacred teaching will descend.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000376"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000376 -->
A king called Sūtrade
will uphold the teaching.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000377"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000377 -->
His patron, a brahmin
called Jaya Ākar,
will sustain it for a thousand and four hundred years. [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045)

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000378"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000378 -->
After that, the age of darkness
will last five thousand years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000379"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000379 -->
Then, in the west, on the Island of Loving Care,
at a place called Bharabhati Sāli, [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045)
among the class of fortunate beings,
the three—innermost, quintessence, and core—will descend. [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045)

[^G-U00800]

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000380"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000380 -->
The monk Protector of Bliss
will uphold this sacred teaching.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000381"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000381 -->
His patron, Holder of Heroes,
of the outcaste class, will for a thousand years
make this remain and increase.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000382"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000382 -->
After that, darkness itself
will remain for five thousand years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000383"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000383 -->
Then it will descend here again,
at the place known as the Vajra Seat.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000384"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000384 -->
It will increase and blaze with light
for five hundred and fifty years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000385"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000385 -->
Excellent Intelligence will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000386"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000386 -->
His patron will be a ḍākinī
called Pure Cool-Maker.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000387"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000387 -->
After that, in this world,
earth, water, fire, and wind will clash together.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000388"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000388 -->
It will not remain, but become a great emptiness.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000389"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000389 -->
What is called an age, from its beginning,
will finally reach the time of destruction.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000390"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000390 -->
[Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000391"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000391 -->
The immediate cause of the teaching's destruction
in each of those places
will be an individual called one of perverse craving.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000392"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000392 -->
Each such individual will be
the immediate cause of destroying the entire teaching.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000393"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000393 -->
In the first time of darkness here,
in the west of Jambudvīpa,
a king will have six fingers on his hand,
with an extra blue tooth at the front.

Earlier notes: [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000394"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000394 -->
On the elbow of his right arm
there will be a black mole in the shape of a scorpion,
extremely terrifying.

Earlier notes: [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000395"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000395 -->
Through him, the buddha's teaching will become dark.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000396"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000396 -->
This will last sixty years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000397"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000397 -->
After that, the first Glory-Holder will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000398"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000398 -->
After a thousand and three hundred years,
again, in the north of Jambudvīpa,
on the summit of Majestic Mountain,
one in the form of a monk, lacking a loving mind,
will have two ears on his right side
and triangular eyes covered from below upward. [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046)

Earlier notes: [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000399"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000399 -->
He and a group of non-Buddhist proponents will dispute.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000400"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000400 -->
For sixty years there will be darkness.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000401"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000401 -->
After that, one called Deity of Bliss
will make it increase for a hundred years.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000402"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000402 -->
After that, in the northeast of Jambudvīpa,
beside the flowing Unfordable River,
one with a king's body, endowed with merit,
will have only one eye,
coarse limbs and bodily strength,
and be adorned with archery and magical skills.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000403"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000403 -->
He will suppress those who practice the teaching.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000404"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000404 -->
For sixty years, the teaching itself
will set here and move elsewhere.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000405"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000405 -->
After that, one in the form of a solitary buddha
will appear west of the Vajra Seat. [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046)

Earlier notes: [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000406"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000406 -->
He will make the sacred teaching itself
remain for a hundred and twenty years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000407"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000407 -->
After that, in central Jambudvīpa,
at a place called the Golden City,
one with an elder's body will have a two-tipped tongue,
a small frame and short limbs.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000408"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000408 -->
He will quarrel with groups and take up weapons.

Earlier notes: [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000409"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000409 -->
With his appearance, darkness will again
last sixty years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000410"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000410 -->
After that, in eastern Jambudvīpa,
on the river called Lokita,
on its right bank,
one called Glorious Lion
will sustain it for a thousand and four hundred years.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000411"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000411 -->
Then again, in central Jambudvīpa,
on the bank of the great river Gaṅgā,
a king's son, on his arm,
will have something emerald-colored,
projecting prominently to a measure of four finger-widths. [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046)

Earlier notes: [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000412"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000412 -->
With his appearance, darkness will again
last sixty years.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000413"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000413 -->
After that, at the temple called
Kamalaśīla,
its master, Protector of Youth,
will sustain it for a thousand years.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000414"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000414 -->
After that, in southern Jambudvīpa,
in the town called Brahmā's Island,
one of the commoner class will have a lion's head.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000415"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000415 -->
Three months after his birth,
darkness will last for sixty [years]. [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044)

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000416"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000416 -->
After that, on Vulture Peak,
the monk Beautifying Youth
will sustain it for five hundred and fifty years.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000417"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000417 -->
After that, on the borders of Jambudvīpa,
in the land called Kashmir,
in the City Endowed with Sound,
one with a bird's body and a human face
will be born among those of low lineage.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-046](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000418"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000418 -->
Ten years after his birth,
darkness will last for sixty [years].

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000419"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000419 -->
After that, in Jambudvīpa,
five yojanas east of its center,
through the monk Śākya Dzīka,
for ten years from then,
it will be like a blazing lamp. [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045)

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000420"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000420 -->
Thus, through seven successive stages,
it will increase and diminish in Jambudvīpa.

Earlier notes: [N-044](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000421"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000421 -->
Then it will definitely rise
into the field of Beautiful Array.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000422"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000422 -->
When lifespans fall below ten years,
it will not remain here, but appear there.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000423"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000423 -->
[Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]

[^G-U00910]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000424"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000424 -->
When its flourishing and decline are thus complete,
this core of the sacred teaching
will be in the world of Spacious Protection.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000425"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000425 -->
To make the buddha's teaching remain,
the teacher called Beautiful Utpala Flower,
the buddha, the Blessed One,
will be in the palace of Stacked Bliss-Making,
with a retinue of five thousand.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045), [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000426"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000426 -->
All will be great bodhisattvas,
having attained acceptance of non-arising phenomena,
and only those with virtuous minds will appear.

Earlier notes: [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000427"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000427 -->
With the coming of that very teacher,
those of perverse craving will be definitively tamed.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000428"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000428 -->
This very sacred teaching,
for five thousand thousands of years,
will be made to remain as the core of the teaching. [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047)

Earlier notes: [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000429"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000429 -->
Then again, as before,
it will increase and diminish through seven successive stages.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000430"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000430 -->
After that, in the spacious world system
of Melodious Crest,
the teacher will be Infinite Destroyer of Samsara.

Earlier notes: [N-045](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000431"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000431 -->
His retinue will be seven and a half hundreds,
all arhats whose contaminations are exhausted,
who have done what was to be done and laid down the burden.

Earlier notes: [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000432"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000432 -->
He too will sustain the core of the teaching itself
for seven hundred thousand years.

Earlier notes: [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000433"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000433 -->
Following the course of the earlier sequence,
after seven such periods of abiding,
for eighty-five ages,
the teachings will dissolve into the buddha's embodiment,
and in no world system whatever
will the buddha's teaching remain.

Earlier notes: [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000434"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000434 -->
Then, in the lower world,
for ‘a million, six hundred thousand’ ages, [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047)
the buddha's teaching will remain for a long time.

Earlier notes: [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000435"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000435 -->
There will be no increase or decline in it.

Earlier notes: [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000436"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000436 -->
It will bring beings to maturity.

Earlier notes: [N-047](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000437"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000437 -->
[Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]

[^G-U00948]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000438"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000438 -->
The teaching of a perfectly awakened buddha
is held to have two aspects:
the actual teaching and its reflected form.

Earlier notes: [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000439"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000439 -->
Its reflected form has no fixed pattern:
this and that will appear in a mixed way. [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048)

Earlier notes: [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000440"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000440 -->
The sun makes [it] fully appear.

Earlier notes: [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000441"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000441 -->
The three actual presences of embodiment, speech, and awakened mind—
whoever receives them will, without effort,
unfailingly attain buddhahood.

Earlier notes: [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000442"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000442 -->
Therefore, the crucial point of blessing:
in the emanation of speech as well,
having carefully painted the teacher's colors,
fasten it beneath the left armpit.

Earlier notes: [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000443"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000443 -->
If one reads and retains it at the proper times,
one will certainly, unfailingly awaken. [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048)

Earlier notes: [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000444"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000444 -->
Whoever constantly reads this continuum
will become as described above.

Earlier notes: [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000445"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000445 -->
The seventeen definitive secret commands,
together with the nails of the teaching and the magical letters,
in the earliest field of the emanation embodiment,
were spoken by the teacher Samantabhadra
at the place of Dense Array.

Earlier notes: [N-048](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000446"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000446 -->
Then, in the abode of Those with Long Locks,
the All-Seeing Vairocana
held this, bringing the five families to maturity.

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000447"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000447 -->
After that, through my holding it,
as the sacred life-pillar of the teaching,
a thousand and two buddhas
will definitely arise in reliance on this.

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000448"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000448 -->
Sentient beings born miraculously or from eggs,
from warmth or from wombs,
will be brought to maturity.

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000449"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000449 -->
From this very teaching that trains beings,
twelve wheels of teaching will arise.

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000450"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000450 -->
Its thirty-six deeds,
in dependence on embodiment, speech, and awakened mind,
will appear to the sentient beings who are to be trained.

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000451"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000451 -->
After I have passed beyond sorrow,
in the western land of Uḍḍiyāna,
to the divine lady of Dhanakośa,
a fatherless son, Vajrahe, [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049)
will uphold the sacred teaching.

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000452"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000452 -->
Under his guidance, in the west,
a fortunate brahmin boy,
called Mañjuśrīpati,
will uphold it for seventy-five years.

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000453"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000453 -->
After him, a fortunate one,
in the place called Sośa Island,
of the householder class, called Śrīsiddha,
will uphold it.

[^G-U00995]

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000454"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000454 -->
After him, one of the outcaste class will uphold it—
Jñānasūtra will uphold this very teaching. [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049)

Earlier notes: [N-049](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000455"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000455 -->
After him, one of the householder class—
Vast Seal—will uphold it. [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050)

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000456"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000456 -->
After him, one of the royal class,
an emanation together with an attendant, will uphold it. [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050)

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000457"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000457 -->
After him, in the form of an ascetic,
Spontaneous Victory Banner will do so.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000458"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000458 -->
After him, Sedheśvara will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000459"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000459 -->
After him, those abiding on the grounds—
bodhisattvas—will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000460"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000460 -->
After that, an emanation of Vajrapāṇi,
Vajraphala, will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000461"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000461 -->
After that, the king Lion—
the Good One—will uphold it. [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050)

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000462"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000462 -->
After that, Glorious Word-Specialist will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000463"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000463 -->
After that, Yogi Prabha will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000464"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000464 -->
After that, King Throne-Light will do so.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000465"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000465 -->
After that, the monk Endowed with Glory;
then Supreme Intelligence will uphold it.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000466"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000466 -->
Then the ḍākinī Glory-Holder,
having upheld this very teaching,
will spread it when the lifespan is ten years.

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000467"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000467 -->
Thereafter, its essence is non-abiding. [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050)

Earlier notes: [N-050](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000468"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000468 -->
[Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]

[^G-U01023]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000469"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000469 -->
This secret heart-sphere of the teaching: [N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051) [N-T24](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t24)
after the seven teachings have passed in this way,
and after its definite course through two fields,
when the seven have thus passed, [N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051)
on the summit of the Vajra Seat,
the speech-token of all the teachings—[N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051)
the letters of the Great Naturally Arisen—
will descend together with sound. [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

Earlier notes: [N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051), [N-T24](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t24), [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000470"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000470 -->
Ten million ages from now,
to the Blessed One Wheel-Garland of Lightning
it will appear. [N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051)

Earlier notes: [N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000471"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000471 -->
Then, after three hundred years—
after those three [hundreds] have thus passed—
the vajra of the teaching's awakened mind, too, [N-T04](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04)
will appear to that teacher.

Earlier notes: [N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051), [N-T04](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000472"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000472 -->
Then, after five hundred years,
the teaching's embodiment, too, will descend there.

Earlier notes: [N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000473"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000473 -->
When the actual teaching has descended in this way,
the embers of the teaching will blaze up again.

Earlier notes: [N-051](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000474"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000474 -->
Also among that teacher's retinue,
one called Noble Brahmā-Light,
endowed with miraculous power, toward perfection
will have intense faith and aspiration;
he will definitely uphold [the teaching] and become manifest. [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052)

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000475"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000475 -->
Beginning with him, the teacher's retinue—
twenty thousand noble hearers—
through the discipline teaching for one age,
will be established in the result of an arhat.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000476"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000476 -->
Then there will be six ages of darkness.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000477"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000477 -->
Then two hundred thousand arhats
will gather in his retinue.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000478"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000478 -->
Through the sūtra teaching for eight ages,
he will establish [them] at the culmination of provisional and definitive activity. [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052)

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000479"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000479 -->
Having conferred empowerment endowed with miraculous power,
he will definitely impel [them onward], and the teaching will be complete.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000480"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000480 -->
Again he will show the manner of passing beyond sorrow.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000481"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000481 -->
Then there will be two ages of darkness.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000482"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000482 -->
Then, when the teacher is born,
a retinue of great bodhisattvas—
a hundred thousand—will gather.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000483"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000483 -->
Foremost among those gathered, Glorious Great Terror
will thoroughly collect the teacher's teaching. [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052)

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000484"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000484 -->
Through the higher teaching for eight ages,
all will attain the result in one lifetime. [N-053](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-053)

[^G-U01067]

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052), [N-053](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-053).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000485"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000485 -->
At that time samsara will be emptied from its depths:
sentient beings will attain buddhahood;
there can be no appearance called the six destinies.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000486"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000486 -->
For twenty thousand ages,
the flow of sentient beings' samsara having been cut off, [N-T17](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17)
embodied beings will not appear at all.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052), [N-T17](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000487"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000487 -->
Then, through particular karmic differences,
although sentient beings' afflictions are not manifest,
through the force of dormant tendencies rising up,
the forms taken on will be extremely subtle.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052), [N-T68](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t68).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000488"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000488 -->
From these exceedingly subtle, numerous [forms],
the bodies of sentient beings endowed with form will develop. [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052)

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000489"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000489 -->
Thus, the inconceivable final extent of the teaching,
which cannot be encompassed by expression,
is for now indicated through words.

Earlier notes: [N-052](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000490"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000490 -->
[Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000491"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000491 -->
As for the body and branches of the continuum:
the arrays in its body number two hundred
and exactly fifty. [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054)

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000492"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000492 -->
First, through clarity of ascertainment,
in the branches of the manifold array,
there are exactly seventy questions;
the arrays of the branches number one hundred and one. [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054)

[^G-U01090]

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000493"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000493 -->
The body itself is thereby completed.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000494"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000494 -->
Its branches number two hundred and ten.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000495"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000495 -->
The foregoing body and branches are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000496"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000496 -->
The body is complete; the branches disclose the secret. [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054)

[^G-U01094]

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000497"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000497 -->
The second body is closely taught:
in the array gathering the crucial points,
from twenty-eight questions,
there are thirty-one arrays of ‘crucial-point times’ (gnad dus). [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054)

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000498"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000498 -->
Through these the body itself is complete.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000499"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000499 -->
The branches number two hundred and eight.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000500"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000500 -->
Through these, the second body
and its branches are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000501"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000501 -->
The third body is closely taught:
for twenty-two questions,
there are fifty-eight arrays of primordial knowing;
the branches themselves number two hundred.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000502"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000502 -->
The branches of the third body are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000503"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000503 -->
As for the fourth body and its branches:
there are twenty-one questions,
thirty-two arrays of the nature of phenomena,
and fifty-eight branches.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000504"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000504 -->
As for the fifth body and its branches:
there are eleven questions,
thirty-two arrays of viewing and cultivation,
and sixty-four branches.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000505"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000505 -->
The fifth body and its branches are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000506"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000506 -->
As for the sixth body and its branches:
from the eight questions asked,
there are sixteen arrays of embodiments;
their branches number thirty-two.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000507"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000507 -->
The sixth body and its branches are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-054](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000508"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000508 -->
[Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000509"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000509 -->
The account of the elements is as follows.

Earlier notes: [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000510"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000510 -->
Through the particulars of shared karma,
sentient beings, with karma as their support,
depend on the outer elements.

Earlier notes: [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000511"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000511 -->
Actions, clothing, and faculties, [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055)
retinues and births, are held to become complete.

Earlier notes: [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000512"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000512 -->
The sequence of activity of the inner elements is this:
earth makes the body's foundation; [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055)
through its generation, maturation into flesh is held to occur.

Earlier notes: [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055), [N-T30](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t30).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000513"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000513 -->
Water gathers the whole body together;
through its generation, maturation into blood is held to occur.

Earlier notes: [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000514"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000514 -->
Fire brings the whole body to maturity;
through its generation, maturation into warmth is held to occur.

Earlier notes: [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000515"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000515 -->
Wind raises the whole body;
through its generation, it brings about maturation into breath.

Earlier notes: [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000516"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000516 -->
Through the particulars of their word and meaning,
the general aspects are definitely taught. [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055)

Earlier notes: [N-055](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000517"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000517 -->
Through training with form, the body will be exhausted. [N-056](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056)

Earlier notes: [N-056](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000518"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000518 -->
Through sound, languages will be known. [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

Earlier notes: [N-056](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056), [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000519"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000519 -->
Through smell, taking nourishment from vital essences is accomplished. [N-T16](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16)

Earlier notes: [N-056](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056), [N-T16](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000520"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000520 -->
Through taste, substances are transformed into nectar.

Earlier notes: [N-056](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000521"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000521 -->
[Source annotation at U01144; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U01144]

Earlier notes: [N-056](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000522"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000522 -->
The nectar completes the benefit of other sentient beings
and also one's own benefit. [N-056](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056)

Earlier notes: [N-056](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000523"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000523 -->
[Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]

[^G-U01147]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000524"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000524 -->
The times of sentient beings have eight aspects:
illness, wind, and heat;
knowing, afflictions, awakening ordinary mind, [N-T25](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t25)
primordial knowing itself, and the elements. [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057), [N-T25](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t25).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000525"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000525 -->
In the four times—summer, winter, autumn, and spring—
wind, bile, phlegm, and their conjunction, the four,
shift from their respective places.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000526"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000526 -->
This is the time of sentient beings' wind-mind shifting.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000527"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000527 -->
At this time wind is layered,
or slow and interrupted,
or subtle and hot. [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000528"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000528 -->
This shortens lifespans;
it steals knowing and brings stupefaction.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000529"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000529 -->
At intervals [knowing] becomes distracted and shifts.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000530"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000530 -->
Through the gathering of the five afflictions,
[beings] are projected into places of lower destiny.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000531"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000531 -->
Awakening ordinary mind dissipates, [N-T25](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t25)
flutters greatly, and becomes subtle. [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057), [N-T25](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t25).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000532"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000532 -->
Primordial knowing is not clear.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000533"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000533 -->
The elements proliferate from their dispersal and penetration. [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000534"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000534 -->
This is the time of an individual sentient being.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000535"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000535 -->
As for the times common to sentient beings:
from the first, immeasurable [lifespan],
to the final lifespan of ten years,
sixty successive periods occur.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000536"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000536 -->
Because afflictions change under the power of karma,
through the karma of concordant, common purposes, [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)
from a lifespan of a thousand years,
also among the people of Jambudvīpa,
down to a hundred, the successive periods
number thirty.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000537"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000537 -->
Beginning from there, down to ten,
thirty successive periods occur.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000538"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000538 -->
Through eight stages in the times when habituation shifts,
activity subsequently becomes worse.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000539"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000539 -->
Periods of afflicting spirits and famine,
weapons and illness, arise.

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000540"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000540 -->
Their particulars are many: these are the successive periods. [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

Earlier notes: [N-057](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000541"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000541 -->
[Source heading: Twentieth reply.]

[^G-U01186]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000542"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000542 -->
Among the outer elements, wind
is joined with the element of space.

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000543"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000543 -->
Upon it is water, and above that, earth.

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000544"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000544 -->
The earth's extent and thickness are equal. [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058)

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000545"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000545 -->
‘Four innumerables and one million’;
‘one is a thousand of its quarter.’ [Numerical construction unresolved.] [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058)

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000546"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000546 -->
There are the arrays of continents and Mount Meru,
together with wish-granting trees and jewels,
the sun and moon, and the respective gods,
surrounded by the depths of the great ocean.

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000547"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000547 -->
The Meru mountains and tributary streams
are definitely arrayed with seven precious substances.

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000548"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000548 -->
Inwardly, first there is the formless [realm];
then the form realm, clear light;
gradually, also in the desire realm,
from the stainless moon
and the sun that makes things shine brightly,
the world system proliferates. [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058)

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000549"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000549 -->
Beginning with Mastery over Others' Emanations, in descending order,
then the abodes of the demigods,
then the humans of the four continents, successively.

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000550"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000550 -->
Through afflictions together with selfhood,
the places of lower destiny gradually form.

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000551"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000551 -->
The respective beings of the six classes
form within; their bodily size
and lifespan, through shared karma,
and their respective abodes and merit,
abide in accordance with their continent. [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058)

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000552"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000552 -->
The meaning is destruction and formation,
abiding itself and becoming empty;
birth, aging, illness, and death—
the mechanism of existence itself.

Earlier notes: [N-058](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000553"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000553 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000554"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000554 -->
The shifting of times is as follows.

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000555"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000555 -->
From the initial immeasurable [lifespan]
down to the final ten [years],
there are increases upward and decreases downward.

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000556"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000556 -->
Illness, famine, and weapons—
the three minor ages—join the intervals.

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000557"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000557 -->
When sixty have thus passed,
seven [destructions] are by fire, and seven by water.

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000558"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000558 -->
When these fourteen have been completed,
the remaining [destruction] is by wind. [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059)

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000559"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000559 -->
Thus there are sixty [periods] of abiding,
and the duration of destruction is as long;
for sixty [periods] it abides empty.

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000560"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000560 -->
Thus, from one hundred and eighty [periods]— [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059)
the duration is reckoned as the first age.

[^G-U01233]

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000561"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000561 -->
Likewise, with one hundred and eighty,
there is the second, intermediate age.

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000562"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000562 -->
Through that many such repetitions— [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059)
there is one great age.

[^G-U01237][^G-U01233]

Earlier notes: [N-059](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000563"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000563 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-second reply.]

[^G-U01239]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000564"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000564 -->
Three tens of days
constitute one month.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000565"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000565 -->
When twelve such [months] are complete,
they are reckoned as one year.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000566"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000566 -->
When seven hundred
and twenty mornings and evenings are complete,
they are reckoned as three hundred and sixty days.

Earlier notes: [N-060](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-060).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000567"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000567 -->
Their completion constitutes one year.

Earlier notes: [N-060](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-060).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000568"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000568 -->
Days are distinguished as male and female according to activity. [N-060](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-060)

Earlier notes: [N-060](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-060).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000569"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000569 -->
The months of autumn, spring, summer, and winter—
three for each—
enter into operation and enact the five aspects of the elements.

Earlier notes: [N-060](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-060), [N-061](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-061).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000570"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000570 -->
Rat, ox, and tiger;
long-eared hare, snake, and dragon;
horse, sheep, and monkey;
bird, dog, and pig—through these twelve,
the elemental calculations are extended. [N-061](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-061)

Earlier notes: [N-061](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-061).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000571"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000571 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-third reply.]

[^G-U01257]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000572"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000572 -->
The rites for increasing and restoring life,
accomplishing it and making it flourish—
when a yogin knows how to apply them,
[the lifespan] becomes equal to the sun and moon. [N-062](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062)

Earlier notes: [N-062](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000573"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000573 -->
For others, from equalizing and dismantling,
there are summoning, binding, and bringing down a slayer,
expelling, and the activities of killing.

Earlier notes: [N-062](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000574"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000574 -->
When a yogin knows how to summon, [N-062](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062)
beings are gathered under his power.

[^G-U01265]

Earlier notes: [N-062](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000575"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000575 -->
Increasing and restoring ascendancy,
making it abide and become established,
and, further, the application of equalizing—
these a yogin should undertake. [N-062](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062)

[^G-U01270]

Earlier notes: [N-062](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000576"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000576 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000577"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000577 -->
Dispersal, penetration, and equalizing comprise twelve:
for earth, water, fire, and wind, the four,
three each make twelve. [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063)

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000578"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000578 -->
Water's penetration gathers the faculties;
its dispersal causes disturbance;
its equalizing produces the result.

[^G-A2000-C01-S01]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000579"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000579 -->
Earth's penetration produces particles;
its dispersal produces branches;
through its equalizing, the body's elements are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000580"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000580 -->
Fire's penetration produces illness;
through its dispersal, the body will abide;
its equalizing makes the elements abide.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000581"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000581 -->
Wind's penetration protects the elements;
its dispersal destroys other activities;
its equalizing gathers wind-mind into one.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000582"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000582 -->
These are the limits of what the three do.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000583"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000583 -->
Through water's penetration, earth gathers together.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000584"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000584 -->
[Source annotation at U01286; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U01286]

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000585"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000585 -->
When penetration meets penetration,
nothing is accomplished, and destruction follows. [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063)

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000586"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000586 -->
Through dispersal itself, illness occurs;
through equalizing, elixir preparations and medicines are accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000587"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000587 -->
Fire and wind together destroy the aggregates;
through dispersal, all activities are accomplished;
through equalizing, activities of conflict reach their culmination.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000588"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000588 -->
Through water and fire, purposes are thoroughly accomplished;
through dispersal, one enters another;
through equalizing, long life and ascendancy are accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000589"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000589 -->
Through earth and wind, pacification is accomplished;
through dispersal, increase and subjugation are enacted;
through equalizing, aggressive ritual activities are accomplished. [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063)

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27), [N-T31](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t31).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000590"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000590 -->
As for the activity of wind and water:
through dispersal, all virtuous activities are accomplished;
through equalizing, there is victory in warfare.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000591"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000591 -->
Through earth and fire, increase is accomplished;
through dispersal, transferring the elements is accomplished;
through equalizing, the slayer's rite is accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000592"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000592 -->
At the culmination of the activities of penetration and dispersal,
[these results] will definitely occur, Deity of the Faculties. [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063)

Earlier notes: [N-063](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063), [N-T27](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t27).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000593"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000593 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-fifth reply.]

[^G-U01308]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000594"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000594 -->
To cut the karmic continuum of samsara:
complete in freedom from doing, it is complete.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000595"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000595 -->
Free from extremes, it does not abide in a center.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000596"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000596 -->
Entirely complete, it is free from entities.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000597"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000597 -->
Without remainder, it is not a portion.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000598"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000598 -->
Not being one, the two extremes are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000599"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000599 -->
Not appearing as many, it is beyond one.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000600"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000600 -->
Inexpressible, [it is] the Ground of words. [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064)

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000601"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000601 -->
Free from entities and empty: the core meaning.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000602"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000602 -->
Not falling to any side: the meaning of the view.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000603"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000603 -->
Knowing this blocks the path of samsara.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000604"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000604 -->
Direct perception arising from the crucial point itself—
through the power of familiarity, delusion is destroyed.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000605"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000605 -->
With intrinsic nature pure, there is no distraction.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000606"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000606 -->
Abiding continuously, there is no separation.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000607"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000607 -->
Being self-appearance, differentiating conceptualization is exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000608"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000608 -->
Wind exhausted, one engages in primordial knowing itself.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000609"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000609 -->
Through never being separate from this,
one cuts the flow of the paths of the six destinies.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000610"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000610 -->
Without making or contriving,
through activity by its own momentum, delusion is purified. [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064)

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000611"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000611 -->
Arising in letting be, there is non-arising.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000612"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000612 -->
Without grasping or attachment, one plays as one pleases.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000613"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000613 -->
Settled into its own condition, turbidity clears.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000614"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000614 -->
Being intrinsic nature, it is beyond methods.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000615"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000615 -->
This, too, blocks the gate of samsara.

Earlier notes: [N-064](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000616"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000616 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000617"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000617 -->
The qualities of the teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,
cannot be encompassed by expression.

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000618"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000618 -->
Through sixty excellences,
he upholds the foundation of the sacred teaching. [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065)

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065), [N-T30](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t30).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000619"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000619 -->
For a long time in the world system,
the buddha's teaching abides.

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000620"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000620 -->
Its qualities cannot be encompassed by expression.

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000621"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000621 -->
From what do these arise?

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000622"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000622 -->
From embodiment, speech, and awakened mind, [N-T04](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04)
and three sets of twelve deeds:
the teacher having come into the world,
these are the qualities for those to be trained. [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065)

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065), [N-T04](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000623"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000623 -->
Through the outer, inner, and secret four activities,
there are immeasurable qualities that purify.

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000624"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000624 -->
These follow the sequence taught before.

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000625"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000625 -->
The teaching's growth and completion, too,
are qualities of the Blessed One.

Earlier notes: [N-065](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000626"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000626 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-seventh reply.]

[^G-U01353]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000627"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000627 -->
In the sequence of training in the three embodiments,
making the desired purposes of the elements primary,
above all, by training in the sounds
of earth, water, fire, and wind, one definitely accomplishes [them]. [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066) [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066), [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000628"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000628 -->
Water's sound, gushing,
holds the melody of the ḍākinīs.

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000629"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000629 -->
Through constant familiarity with this,
the emanation embodiment, too, is definitely accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000630"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000630 -->
Earth's sound is cool and heavy,
endowed with the voice of Great Brahmā.

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000631"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000631 -->
Through constant, stable engagement in this,
the complete enjoyment embodiment is definitely accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000632"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000632 -->
For fire's sound, train in lengtheners; [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066)
it reveals the melodic speech of the Great All-Pervader.

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000633"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000633 -->
Whoever engages in listening to this
will definitely attain the qualities of the dharma embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000634"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000634 -->
Wind's sound is pressing and fierce;
it expresses the application of the king of sky-soarers. [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066)

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000635"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000635 -->
Knowing how to repeat this constantly,
one should undertake the common training in the three embodiments.

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000636"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000636 -->
Thus the word and meaning of the four elements
are definitely joined to the outer times:
winter, spring, summer, and autumn;
water, earth, fire, and wind.

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000637"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000637 -->
Definitely relating their sequence to the times,
and bringing it into accord with the yogin's body,
there is no doubt that accomplishment occurs. [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066)

Earlier notes: [N-066](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000638"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000638 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-eighth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000639"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000639 -->
As for the culmination of common worldly activities:
one wishing to fly relies on a pill. [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000640"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000640 -->
One wishing to become invisible rests the ordinary mind;
into a net of empty spheres,
placing both body and ordinary mind, one accomplishes [invisibility].

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000641"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000641 -->
One wishing to attain unimpeded penetration
holds wind and places it in a sphere.

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000642"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000642 -->
At intervals, knowing, like mist,
is to be brought out from Brahmā's abode. [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000643"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000643 -->
One wishing to overcome weapons and poison
directs the ordinary mind toward the four elements.

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000644"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000644 -->
When one combines [them] into powder and performs the activity,
these are definitely accomplished. [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000645"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000645 -->
To overcome dogs, bears, and wolves,
one voices the four elements.

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000646"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000646 -->
When the four dependent connections are made,
one will overcome them.

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000647"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000647 -->
One wishing to be freed from the eight fears
cultivates a wheel endowed with a design
at the crown; cultivating wind,
through this, too, one overcomes those [fears].

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000648"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000648 -->
One wishing to accomplish eye medicine—
the preparation involving crow's wings—
combines [it] properly and constantly repeats
the letters of the clarifiers. [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)
