Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000649"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000649 -->
Then even the three realms appear clearly.

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000650"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000650 -->
In warfare, trade, and arranging a marriage,
whoever wishes to prevail
takes account of the time and direction of the elements.

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000651"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000651 -->
When these are made free from confusion,
there is no doubt of definite accomplishment. [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

Earlier notes: [N-067](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000652"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000652 -->
Whoever wishes to become a sword-holder,
with a preparation of yellow sa-pa-la, [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)
intensely repeats the elemental letters.

[^G-U01414]

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000653"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000653 -->
When [these] are properly applied at the body's five places,
and the time of the elements is thoroughly absorbed, [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)
through teaching the thorough application,
one becomes a great sword-holder.

[^G-U01416][^G-U01417]

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000654"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000654 -->
A person gathering ḍākinīs under his power,
having gone to a place where mamos assemble,
a preparation of precious substances— [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)
at the time when the heads of the elements meet, [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)
properly combines [them] into the pill itself. [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)

[^G-U01422]

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000655"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000655 -->
Whoever holds it inside the mouth
will bring the ḍākinīs under his power.

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000656"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000656 -->
One wishing to accomplish swift feet
adorns sixteen vajra wings
with A and YAṂ.

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000657"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000657 -->
At the time when the bird preparation arises,
without error concerning the time,
one smears paste on the soles of the feet
in accordance with the number. [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000658"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000658 -->
In an instant, there is no doubt
that one will travel throughout Jambudvīpa.

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000659"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000659 -->
Also, to travel through charnel-ground places,
one properly combines the eye-preparation pill
at its own times of application,
and surrounds it with A. [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)

[^G-U01439]

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000660"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000660 -->
Taking the vitality of the elements of one's own year,
by going to the place, one definitely accomplishes [this]. [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)

Earlier notes: [N-068](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000661"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000661 -->
One wishing to take nourishment from vital essences: [N-T16](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16)
the method of combining the five nectars,
and, further, balancing the elements—
a skilled person properly combines them in equal parts
and pours [them] into a precious vessel.

Earlier notes: [N-069](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069), [N-T16](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000662"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000662 -->
Then honey and ud-tsid-ta
are properly added as supplements. [N-069](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069)

Earlier notes: [N-069](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000663"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000663 -->
Taking a drop of mercury at a time, [Editorial safety note: Do not carry out this prescription.] [N-069](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069)
likewise, [a portion] the size of a bean—
if those skilled consume [it],
there is no doubt that it becomes
a great [practice of] taking nourishment from vital essences. [N-069](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069)

Earlier notes: [N-069](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000664"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000664 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-ninth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000665"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000665 -->
When accomplishing the particular embodiments,
one trains in the smells of the four elements.

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000666"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000666 -->
Water's smell gives bliss.

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000667"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000667 -->
Adding [that] whose consuming power greatly increases in this,
and joining [it] to wind, [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070)
one accomplishes the embodiment of the vajra-holder.

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000668"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000668 -->
Earth's smell gathers realization.

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000669"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000669 -->
Through the manner of preparing palāśa
and continually teaching dependent connections,
the embodiment of essence itself, too, is accomplished. [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070)

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37), [N-T28](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t28).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000670"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000670 -->
Fire's smell generates experience.

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000671"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000671 -->
Through familiarity with the experience
of one's own body being clear, Heruka, too, is accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000672"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000672 -->
Wind's smell is an object of training.

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000673"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000673 -->
Combining [it] through the activity of abiding and entering,
if one clearly cultivates the sequence of the definite body,
the embodiments of the five families are well accomplished. [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070)

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000674"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000674 -->
Furthermore, through training in taste and touch themselves,
according to their respective sequences,
there is no doubt that whichever embodiments
one desires are accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-070](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000675"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000675 -->
[Source heading: Thirtieth reply.]

[^G-U01476]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000676"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000676 -->
Whoever cultivates the yoga of sleep
thereby makes dull confusion into the path. [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071)

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071), [N-T26](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t26).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000677"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000677 -->
For the outer times, from the Ground, [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071)
one trains the expressiveness of the yogic body.

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000678"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000678 -->
The crucial point is to hold the channels at their sources.

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000679"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000679 -->
Through the four inner times themselves,
one trains the expressiveness of yoga.

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000680"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000680 -->
The crucial point is to cut the flows of expression.

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000681"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000681 -->
Through the four secret times, too,
one trains the expressiveness of the yogic ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000682"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000682 -->
The crucial point is familiarity with sleep.

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000683"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000683 -->
Through the four times of suchness, [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071)
definitely gathering body, speech, and ordinary mind,
one mixes great clear light with sleep.

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071), [N-T29](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t29).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000684"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000684 -->
Having become familiar in this way, the yogin
definitely joins [this] to the three embodiments.

Earlier notes: [N-071](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000685"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000685 -->
Taking the crucial points of dreams onto the path
consists of preparation and applying the crucial points.

Earlier notes: [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000686"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000686 -->
First training body, speech, and ordinary mind,
and relying on the signs of proficiency,
examination and overpowering, and
habitual tendencies—the three—are definitely to be retained. [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072)

Earlier notes: [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000687"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000687 -->
Then, at the time of applying the crucial points:
training, transformation, cutting off, and
stirring, concentrating the vitality, and releasing at the crucial point;
by applying the crucial points of gathering and reversing, [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072)
karmic dreams are exhausted to their limit,
and the delusion of habitual tendencies is uprooted.

Earlier notes: [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000688"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000688 -->
At this time, through one's own diligence,
for the superior they cease; for the middling they are known;
for the inferior they definitely change.

Earlier notes: [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000689"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000689 -->
As for the beginning of these dreams, too:
for the superior they are forgotten, and for the inferior they cease;
for the middling they are exceedingly clear,
and the inferior knows that [they are dreams]. [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072)

Earlier notes: [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000690"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000690 -->
For the inferior they are unclear, then change.

Earlier notes: [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000691"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000691 -->
Through these, the measure [of proficiency] is reached.

Earlier notes: [N-072](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000692"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000692 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000693"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000693 -->
A person's own ordinary mind, when untamed,
is definitely tamed by cultivating non-conceptuality.

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000694"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000694 -->
Having settled into its own condition, it is one's own. [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073)

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000695"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000695 -->
The sequence of prior training
and the main practice depend on one's own continuum.

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000696"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000696 -->
The sequence of prior training is this:
in the preliminary practice of the body,
the causes of the respective activities are stopped.

[^G-U01522]

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000697"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000697 -->
Assuming the vajra cross-legged posture,
one trains the body, benefiting the ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000698"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000698 -->
For speech, abiding in the syllable HŪṂ,
one applies the seal and trains expressiveness;
by entering the path of seeking the formidable, [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073)
one thus trains speech, benefiting the ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000699"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000699 -->
For the ordinary mind: where it initially arises,
where it abides in between, and where it finally goes—
by examining and investigating these three,
one trains the ordinary mind and knows its natural state.

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000700"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000700 -->
The main practice, relying on one's own continuum,
depends on the crucial points of body and speech
and the crucial point of ordinary mind.

[^G-U01534]

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000701"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000701 -->
Wind is to be made slow by every means.

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000702"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000702 -->
Arising from thorough projection, [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073)
the connection between body and ordinary mind is cut;
there is no turning back into the three realms.

[^G-U01539]

Earlier notes: [N-073](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000703"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000703 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-second reply.]

[^G-U01540]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000704"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000704 -->
Wind has two kinds of purpose.

Earlier notes: [N-074](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000705"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000705 -->
In the world, it generates the complete body,
and [supports] the lifespan through which it is sustained and abides;
it increases power, lifespan, and so forth.

Earlier notes: [N-074](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000706"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000706 -->
It performs the activities of merit and karma;
it generates beings' luster and splendor;
it performs every activity of spreading and gathering.

Earlier notes: [N-074](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000707"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000707 -->
For the transcendent: embodiments, primordial knowing,
and familiarity with spheres themselves;
pervasion that is light, empty, and clear; [N-074](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074)
the exhaustion of the body's elements;
the stopping of the ordinary mind's conceptual thought;
the arising of non-conceptual primordial knowing;
the exhaustion of outer appearances;
and the mixing of wind and ordinary mind, the two. [N-074](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074)

Earlier notes: [N-074](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074), [N-T34](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t34).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000708"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000708 -->
Thus wind performs all activities.

Earlier notes: [N-074](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000709"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000709 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-third reply.]

[^G-U01557]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000710"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000710 -->
The crucial point of the body is the channels:
their places, arrangement, and movement;
their support of the wheels and the body's life-force;
names, particulars, causes, and conditions;
the activities they perform and their characteristics;
afflictive activities and activities of primordial knowing;
manifest appearance through reliance on the path;
illnesses and the particulars of the elements—
they are distinguished by the extent of their abiding in confusion. [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000711"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000711 -->
Constricting stops; wringing binds. [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

[^G-U01567]

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000712"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000712 -->
Pressing makes [them] abide; squeezing makes [them] clear.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000713"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000713 -->
Twisting makes [them] cross; drawing makes [them] known.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000714"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000714 -->
Pushing makes [them] stay; forcefulness makes [them] held. [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000715"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000715 -->
Thus, through the crucial points of the channels, too,
the abode of buddhahood is indicated.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000716"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000716 -->
The winds that depend on the channels
move twenty thousand, one thousand,
and six hundred [times].

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000717"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000717 -->
These are reckoned as the winds of great movement.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000718"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000718 -->
Their activity has two aspects:
moving upward and clearing downward.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000719"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000719 -->
Or, when a skilled person seeks the crucial point,
those definitely moving from the channels—
one hundred thousand, twenty thousand,
six thousand, and six hundred—
definitely arise from the channels.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000720"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000720 -->
They are reckoned as the winds of small movement. [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000721"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000721 -->
Times of illness, afflicting spirits, and lifespan
are to be known by the skilled person.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000722"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000722 -->
Through their crucial point, one strives in methods of application.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000723"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000723 -->
For the great [movements], one calculates the time
and examines supreme and common accomplishments.

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000724"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000724 -->
Through the particulars of their respective activities,
between karma and primordial knowing, the two,
and among afflictions and freedom from conceptualization,
one takes the respective numerical measures. [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000725"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000725 -->
The cultivator trains thoroughly;
through this the measures of the respective winds are grasped.

[^G-U01595]

Earlier notes: [N-075](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000726"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000726 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000727"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000727 -->
One wishing to stabilize the nature of phenomena of the sphere: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)
there are the ultimate and the relative.

[^G-U01598]

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000728"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000728 -->
For now, relying on the relative sphere,
one wishing for buddhahood—
a woman of the family, with complete characteristics,
from gods, demigods, or Brahmā's [class],
or even a low-caste non-Buddhist—[N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)
if one sees [her] complete characteristics,
one definitely undertakes the application of summoning.

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076), [N-T36](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t36).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000729"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000729 -->
Through what is to be relied upon, the channels,
and the objects of focus, one completes the body. [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000730"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000730 -->
Then the relative sphere itself
is lowered, retained, and reversed;
it is placed in the channels and mixed with wind.

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000731"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000731 -->
Emptiness is to be stabilized; the traces of conceptual mind are cut.

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000732"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000732 -->
Body and ordinary mind, the two, are to exchange their positions. [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000733"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000733 -->
Through stabilizing the ultimate sphere,
one finds the empty domains of the dharma embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000734"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000734 -->
Packing the lamp of the empty sphere itself,
one trains the expressiveness of awareness. [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000735"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000735 -->
Engaging in familiarity without [distinguishing] day and night,
this itself appears directly, free from effort.

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000736"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000736 -->
This is the measure of proficiency.

Earlier notes: [N-076](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000737"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000737 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-fifth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000738"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000738 -->
The extent of the activities of ḍākinīs,
if differentiated, cannot be encompassed by expression;
nevertheless, it will be explained in brief.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000739"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000739 -->
Through profound dependent connections,
one is free of food, clothing, and so forth,
attains uncontaminated great bliss,
and gains mastery over the four elements. [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)

[^G-U01627]

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000740"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000740 -->
Through the culmination of activities examined in actuality,
and through examining the domains of form, sound, smell, taste,
and touch, [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)
the culmination of common worldly activities
and higher knowing are perfected,
and one enjoys the fields.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077), [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000741"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000741 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-sixth reply.]

[^G-U01634]

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000742"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000742 -->
There are three particular kinds of transference, [N-T32](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t32)
according to distinctions in faculties:
clear light and illusory body;
for the last, body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)

[^G-U01635]

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077), [N-T32](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t32).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000743"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000743 -->
Transference of body and speech has two aspects:
actual transference and nonactual transference.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077), [N-T32](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t32).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000744"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000744 -->
The actual is a method of training through wind.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000745"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000745 -->
Through training in word, craft, illusory expressiveness,
and dependent connections,
when the respective elements accord inwardly,
it is accomplished by directing body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077), [N-T78](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t78), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000746"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000746 -->
For the nonactual, stabilize the power of the ordinary mind's familiarity.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000747"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000747 -->
Train the illusory body in dreams.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000748"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000748 -->
Through proficiency in this, the body itself
becomes like the form of a shadow.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000749"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000749 -->
Through this, the body of the intermediate state
comes to be held within this condition. [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000750"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000750 -->
Clear-light transference is for the superior:
having trained in the meditative stabilization of sleep,
through proficiency, without [distinguishing] day and night,
when outer appearances are clear,
through this great clear light is accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077), [N-T32](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t32), [N-T33](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t33).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000751"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000751 -->
Its body, too, is threefold. [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)

[^G-U01657]

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000752"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000752 -->
Through the stages of diligence,
into the emanation embodiment, complete enjoyment embodiment, and dharma embodiment,
one can definitely transfer in actuality.

Earlier notes: [N-077](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077), [N-T32](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t32).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000753"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000753 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-seventh reply.]

[^G-U01661]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000754"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000754 -->
The application of dependent connections has two aspects:
accomplishing manifold miracles
and the rites of the crucial points of the elements.

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000755"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000755 -->
Manifold miracles have manifold methods.

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000756"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000756 -->
Grasping the various paths, one applies one's own knowing. [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078)

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000757"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000757 -->
The moving and unmoving are encircled with wind.

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000758"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000758 -->
[Source annotation at U01668; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U01668]

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000759"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000759 -->
They are differentiated through accomplishing body and faculties.

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000760"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000760 -->
The crucial points of the elements definitely produce
beneficial and harmful results for oneself and others.

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000761"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000761 -->
Join [them] with each person's body and elements:
water, earth, fire, and wind.

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000762"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000762 -->
Through the dependent connections of their respective activities,
one restores, increases, or diminishes [them].

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000763"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000763 -->
Earth and water destroy;
earth and fire increase;
earth and wind bring down a slayer;
water and fire cause decline;
water and wind restore;
fire and wind equalize;
the four gathered together produce confusion. [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078)

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000764"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000764 -->
Whatever dependent connections are expressed,
relate them to each yogin's experience.

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000765"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000765 -->
For the mental faculty's dependent connections, inquire of wind. [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078)

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000766"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000766 -->
As for the activity of the three gathered together,
when properly combined with each corresponding arrangement,
through this, too, benefit and harm are accomplished.

[^G-U01688]

Earlier notes: [N-078](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000767"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000767 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-eighth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000768"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000768 -->
One wishing to be liberated from illness and suffering
applies [the methods] distinctly to oneself and others.

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000769"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000769 -->
For others, act through dependent connections;
for oneself, perform transferring and stroking. [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000770"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000770 -->
There are three actions:
through the crucial point of body, speech,
and likewise the abiding of ordinary mind, one is liberated.

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000771"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000771 -->
For the body, in the various bodily mechanisms:
the lion and elephant,
vulture and sheep shake [the body];
the vixen and the person,
worms and so forth—with bodily [forms]
and manifold bodily mechanisms,
outer and inner, upper and lower, limbs and body,
wind, bile, phlegm, and their conjunction,
are combined with the four groups of elemental illnesses. [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000772"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000772 -->
Distinguishing their extremes as hot and cold,
one joins each with its counterpart and is liberated from illness.

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000773"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000773 -->
For speech, there are manifold sounds and voices:
with āli and kāli exceedingly clear,
for the letters abiding by intrinsic nature in the channels,
through the six ways of reversing [them], [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)
one joins [them] to their respective places.

[^G-U01711]

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079), [N-T35](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t35).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000774"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000774 -->
The voices of gods, nāgas, and yakṣas,
rākṣasas and sky-soarers;
Brahmā, the Hundred-Giver, and the All-Pervader;
spirits and wind gods; [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)
kumbhāṇḍas, belly-crawlers, and gandharvas,
and the voices of further entities—[N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)
by joining the respective voices, one is liberated.

[^G-U01716]

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000775"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000775 -->
For ordinary mind, manifold forms of mindfulness,
and the extent of whatever movement occurs,
are distinguished through the yogin's awareness.

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000776"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000776 -->
Furthermore, transform through body, speech, and mental faculty.

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000777"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000777 -->
Or, through the activity of ordinary mind's doing,
transform through manifold deep absorptions,
and distinguish the extent of suffering as well. [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)

Earlier notes: [N-079](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000778"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000778 -->
[Source heading: Thirty-ninth reply.]

[^G-U01727]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000779"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000779 -->
The shifting of the elements is like this:
Ema! It is explained as greatly wondrous.

Earlier notes: [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000780"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000780 -->
Through the distinction of outer and inner,
earth [joins] earth; the remaining [elements], too,
are to be understood by extension from the preceding [account].

Earlier notes: [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000781"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000781 -->
Day and night, morning and evening, days,
months and years, through the manner of error— [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080)
sets of twelve, and the coarse [divisions], too;
penetration, dispersal, and equalizing,
and abiding indeterminately in confusion—the four—[N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080)
are definitely distinguished by time: sixteen.

[^G-U01734]

Earlier notes: [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000782"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000782 -->
Their respective definite times, the intervals of shifting,
and the places of the elements in dependent connections and so forth:
at the time of shifting, there is shifting;
likewise, at the time of entering, entering;
at the time of abiding, abiding;
and in confusion itself, confusion.

Earlier notes: [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000783"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000783 -->
These concern day and night, days and months.

Earlier notes: [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000784"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000784 -->
The places of shifting are examined by the skilled person.

Earlier notes: [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000785"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000785 -->
Through water, earth, fire, and wind,
concordance and discordance,
and the respective combinations of activities and times,
the culminations of activities are to be transformed. [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080)

Earlier notes: [N-080](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000786"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000786 -->
[Source heading: Fortieth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000787"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000787 -->
Distinguishing the elements of the crucial points:
from four hundred and four,
the specified entries are condensed into eighty;
from these, twenty-four;
the supreme abidings themselves are sixteen;
through male–female pairing, they become eight.

[^G-U01757]

Earlier notes: [N-081](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-081).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000788"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000788 -->
Through four pure in their own identity,
the crucial points are gathered and definitely taught. [N-081](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-081)

Earlier notes: [N-081](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-081).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000789"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000789 -->
When the activities of dispersal, penetration, and equalizing
are joined to dates, months, and years
without error,
the crucial points, too, become definite.

Earlier notes: [N-081](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-081).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000790"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000790 -->
[Source heading: Forty-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000791"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000791 -->
Thus, relying on the crucial points,
the branches of the application of calculation are explained.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000792"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000792 -->
Ema! How greatly wondrous!

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000793"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000793 -->
Not spoken by other buddhas,
the sequence of elemental calculation—
relying on which one measures samsara and nirvana—
listen to this utmost secret! [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082)

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000794"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000794 -->
From water, earth, fire, and wind,
there is a sequence of differentiation.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000795"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000795 -->
The fifth one is neuter and empty.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000796"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000796 -->
This is the sequence of knowing and calculating.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000797"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000797 -->
[Source annotation at U01776; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U01776]

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000798"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000798 -->
From the twelve activities
of penetration, dispersal, and equalizing, the mother-years, too,
number twelve; in groups of three,
the respective objects of focus and dependent connections, too,
are examined by the skilled person in their application to times.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000799"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000799 -->
Za-ma, days, months, and years—[N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082)
through the times of dispersal, penetration, and equalizing,
one grasps the times of birth, aging, illness,
and likewise death.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000800"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000800 -->
Planets, stars, and afflicting spirits,
and the sequence of the sun's times:
calculate their particular favorable and unfavorable aspects.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000801"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000801 -->
The skilled person grasps what should and should not be done.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000802"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000802 -->
Through form, sound, smell, and taste, [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)
examine the karmic connections of earlier and later lives.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082), [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000803"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000803 -->
Through what moves the respective elements,
grasp the growth of virtuous application and the times of afflicting spirits.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000804"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000804 -->
Through sixteen inwardly concordant distinctions,
examine karma, afflictions, and primordial knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000805"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000805 -->
The elements generate, abide, and destroy;
empty, wander, and spread without limit;
coil, revolve, and turn back;
become sharp, sluggish, fierce, and weak;
and [act as] enemy, slayer, and friend. [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082)

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000806"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000806 -->
Through these, calculate the categories of each respective activity.

Earlier notes: [N-082](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000807"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000807 -->
Through the forms of the outer elements,
taking ya bzhi [ཡ་བཞི་; sense unresolved], bring each into accord. [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083)

[^G-U01803]

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000808"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000808 -->
Through the respective combinations of their elements,
grasp the times of lifespan and ascendancy.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000809"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000809 -->
[Source annotation at U01806; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U01806]

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000810"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000810 -->
Birth, illness, and death
are definitely related to oneself and others.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000811"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000811 -->
For the months of winter, summer, spring, and autumn,
counting three [for each] makes twelve.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000812"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000812 -->
The days of a thousand years, too, number three hundred thousand. [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083)

[^G-U01811]

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000813"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000813 -->
[Joined source fragment at U01812; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U01812][^G-U01811]

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000814"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000814 -->
Through the stars' not being two,
years and months are piled one upon another. [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083)

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000815"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000815 -->
Through what transforms day and night,
there are two times when day and night are equal.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000816"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000816 -->
Among the categories known as dates,
in the ascending half, nights are long.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000817"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000817 -->
Then, by one hundred and eighty,
in the descending half, days are long. [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083)

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000818"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000818 -->
For making day and night long or short,
through distinguishing the means of male and female elements,
from the twelvefold sequence of day and night,
the skilled person grasps the times of reversal.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000819"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000819 -->
Through the length and shortness of day and night,
virtue and nonvirtue are distinguished.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000820"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000820 -->
For days, too, by extension from day and night,
months and years: dispersal, penetration, and equalizing.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000821"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000821 -->
[Source annotation at U01829; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U01829]

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000822"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000822 -->
Join the twelve times of dependent connections.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000823"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000823 -->
Knowing all twelve in sequence,
one completes Mañjuśrī's knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-083](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000824"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000824 -->
Through the magical displays of the outer elements
and the categories of long and short days and nights,
join the elemental years to the measures of time.

Earlier notes: [N-084](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-084).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000825"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000825 -->
For years, apply the respective extensions;
at the outset, join their own times equally.

Earlier notes: [N-084](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-084).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000826"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000826 -->
Wind, light, and great sound;
earthquakes, evenness, and reflections;
and the manner in which [they] vanish into space—
definitely relating these to their respective directions,
one knows frost, heavy rain, and absence of rain;
famine, illness, and classes of afflicting spirits;
conflict and movement of ‘mountain-throne’ (ri khri), [N-084](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-084)
through their respective times and classes.

Earlier notes: [N-084](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-084).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000827"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000827 -->
Kings, common people, and spiritual practitioners;
queens, ministers, and paṇḍitas;
the old, the young, and livestock;
further, the particulars of the various animals;
the forms of men and women;
fruit harvests, field crops, and medicinal harvests—
their success and failure, favorable and unfavorable aspects,
are to be known by the yogin in sequence. [N-084](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-084)

Earlier notes: [N-084](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-084).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000828"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000828 -->
[Source heading: Forty-second reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000829"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000829 -->
Ema! Wondrous and marvelous,
this bliss of the elements is like this:
not known in other teachings,
utterly secret and unsurpassed.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000830"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000830 -->
Because you, fortunate one, command great regard,
unable to refuse [you], I explain it to you. [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085)

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000831"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000831 -->
The supremely fortunate, diligent person,
free from the mental faculty [turned toward] samsara,
should offer to and praise the guru.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000832"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000832 -->
Abandoning all worldly activities,
accord with the respective elements:
water, earth, fire, and wind.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000833"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000833 -->
Definitely relate each to its respective body.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000834"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000834 -->
Through the order of the respective elements,
definitely examine the forms of entities.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000835"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000835 -->
Regarding form, sound, smell, and taste,
and the qualities of their respective desires,
one should train, beginning with sound. [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085) [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085), [N-T03](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000836"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000836 -->
Furthermore, the fortunate yogin
properly examines and investigates the colors
and shapes of the respective entities,
and applies [them] to common and supreme accomplishment.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000837"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000837 -->
Then apply [this] to their respective sounds.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000838"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000838 -->
Through definite proficiency in sound,
undertaking the remainder brings accomplishment.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000839"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000839 -->
Thus, concerning the outer elements,
through superior, middling, and inferior diligence,
in days, months, and years,
At their respective measures, [they] are accomplished through signs.
Thus, regarding the accomplishment of embodiment:
the emanation embodiment and complete enjoyment embodiment,
the dharma embodiment and the embodiment of essence itself—
[one] joins [them] as the supreme result of embodiment. [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085)

[^G-A2000-C01-S02][^G-U01883][^G-U01884]

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000840"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000840 -->
For one is neuter and empty.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000841"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000841 -->
The distinctions of sound and so forth
depend on the respective particulars
of the times of abiding, entering, arising, and reversing.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000842"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000842 -->
For the application of common activities,
relying on the voices of ḍākinīs, the six classes,
and the respective elements,
one accomplishes higher knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000843"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000843 -->
Free of food and clothing, and, regarding the elements,
free from sound, one confers empowerment. [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085)

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000844"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000844 -->
The body's elements are exhausted,
and [the body] becomes like the shadow of a lamp.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000845"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000845 -->
Thoroughly free from the sufferings of aging and death,
one becomes equal to the sun and moon.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000846"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000846 -->
Having cut the flow of outer and inner breath, [Editorial note: Do not attempt breath cessation from this draft.] [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085)
all differentiating conceptualization is stopped.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000847"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000847 -->
Relying on non-conceptual meditative stabilization,
one closely joins the basic space of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085), [N-T33](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t33).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000848"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000848 -->
Having assumed a common worldly body,
when this itself is exhausted,
there is no return to the three realms.

Earlier notes: [N-085](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000849"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000849 -->
[Source heading: Forty-third reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000850"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000850 -->
The activity of transferring has two aspects:
entities themselves and emptiness.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000851"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000851 -->
Entities are included within form.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000852"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000852 -->
When one is thus proficient in the elements,
having stabilized familiarity with wind,
one can make any entity possessing form move
by placing knowing within it.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000853"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000853 -->
This is merely transferring into an entity. [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000854"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000854 -->
For emptiness, familiarity of ordinary mind is primary.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000855"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000855 -->
By joining the basic space of phenomena and the dharma embodiment
nondually, one knows [them] as empty.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000856"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000856 -->
In emptiness, whose intrinsic nature is clarity,
ordinary mind, too, is in that condition.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000857"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000857 -->
Through completion of the rite of dependent connections,
For transferring at the crucial point, the method of releasing
and the utterance of voices—through these, one should know. [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000858"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000858 -->
Through the meeting and gathering of the respective elements,
one is to transfer through seeing alone.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000859"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000859 -->
On seeing the form of an animal,
the skilled person joins [this] to wind.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000860"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000860 -->
But when accomplishment is reached,
through an invisible form all bodies are accomplished. [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000861"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000861 -->
On seeing material substances to which one is attached,
take [them] by transferring, to offer to the guru.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000862"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000862 -->
It is not to be done otherwise.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000863"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000863 -->
Likewise, youthful forms and so forth: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)
when the guru, the hero, commands,
through familiarity with wind, take these too.

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000864"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000864 -->
Possessing an ordinary mind that enters,
the yogin should transfer. [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

Earlier notes: [N-086](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000865"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000865 -->
[Source heading: Forty-fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000866"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000866 -->
A greatly fortunate yogin wishing to transfer,
within what is gathered by ordinary mind, together with its family,
to living beings appearing in form,
through inwardly concordant transformations of activity,
transfers his own illness and so forth to others.

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000867"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000867 -->
By transferring in this way, one is released from the actual [illness]. [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087)

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000868"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000868 -->
Further, the application of dependent connections is taught.

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000869"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000869 -->
From the four elements of insentient matter,
through dependent connections that transform entities,
with numbers, signs, and voices,
casting the object of focus, one transfers illness and so forth.

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000870"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000870 -->
Sticks, pebbles, and grit;
grass, fragments of firebrands, potsherds, and so forth;
likewise, water, earth, and space—
definitely taking these up, one transforms the activity of transferring
through the power of the yogin's ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000871"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000871 -->
The skilled person examines its signs and measures:
what has been placed is broken and turned,
raised, laid down, and straightened;
shifted, lifted, and discarded;
held, wiped, and struck.

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000872"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000872 -->
Through these, the practitioner should know thoroughly. [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087)

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000873"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000873 -->
Possessing the gesture of summoning,
food and wealth bound by miserliness
are taken by transferring, to offer to the guru.

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000874"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000874 -->
When this itself is clear as an object of focus in ordinary mind,
like iron gathering to a lodestone,
one definitely takes [them].

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000875"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000875 -->
But when undertaken for one's own worldly desires,
it is not accomplished. [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087)

Earlier notes: [N-087](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000876"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000876 -->
[Source heading: Forty-fifth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000877"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000877 -->
Not explained in any other continuum:
the elements' crucial points—drawing forth and reversing.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000878"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000878 -->
I will definitely explain their profundity; listen.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000879"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000879 -->
Wind, bile, and phlegm;
the conjunction of two and the conjunction of three;
their halves and twelve parts—[N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088)
definitely relating their sequence to the times,
for two causes and eight conditions,
identify hot and cold.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000880"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000880 -->
Join the outer and inner domains as pairs.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000881"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000881 -->
The skilled person distinguishes body and limbs,
relating the upper, lower, and middle places.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000882"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000882 -->
Possessing the three places, one draws out illnesses. [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088)

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000883"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000883 -->
Through the body's manifold bodily mechanisms
and the culmination of activities of restraining through vital essences,
draw all elemental illnesses outward.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000884"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000884 -->
Definitely relating body and limbs, outer and inner,
one should turn ordinary mind back. [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088)

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000885"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000885 -->
For speech, act through manifold sounds and voices,
manifold combinations of letter-signs,
abiding, and the meaning beyond expression.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000886"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000886 -->
For ordinary mind, relate earth, water, fire, and wind,
and manifold objects of focus, to the illness.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000887"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000887 -->
Reversal is through the crucial points of the faculties.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000888"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000888 -->
Through the respective numbers and measures,
definite signs, measures, and the interval of liberation—
by definitely knowing their measures of time,
one's own body is liberated from illness,
and one knows the application of dependent connections for others' bodies.

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088), [N-T37](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t37).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000889"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000889 -->
By acting in this way, the purpose is accomplished. [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088)

Earlier notes: [N-088](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000890"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000890 -->
[Source heading: Forty-sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000891"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000891 -->
The enlightened intent of tenet systems has two aspects: [N-089](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-089)
the enlightened intent for entering the worldly path,
and the enlightened intent of the ultimate meaning.

Earlier notes: [N-089](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-089), [N-090](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-090), [N-T38](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t38).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000892"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000892 -->
Through their respective Grounds and faculties,
the worldly and the transcendent
are held to be two vehicles.

Earlier notes: [N-090](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-090).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000893"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000893 -->
The Ground is worldly;
from this, there are two assertions.
The transcendent, in terms of cause and result—
For the cause there are three; for the result, two.

<!-- pair: DTG-000906 -->

<a id="dtg-000963"></a>
