Through time, number, and appropriation,
relate [these] to aggregates, elements, and so forth.

Earlier notes: [N-095](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-095), [N-T90](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t90).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000973"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000973 -->
Through the crucial point that reverses, too,
reverse the delusion of the respective embodied beings.

Earlier notes: [N-095](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-095).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000974"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000974 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000975"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000975 -->
Regarding the individual characteristics of objects of knowledge,
through knowing the transformations of general and particular,
although the respective assertions differ,
for viewing the worldly activity of the mental faculty,
conceptual minds delighting in elaborations
temporarily engage with words. [N-096](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-096)

[^G-U02183]

Earlier notes: [N-096](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-096).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000976"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000976 -->
In the tradition, through two particular combinations,
place those with perception at the limit.
Through pith instructions on the crucial point of direct perception,
cut the elaborations that distinguish samsara and nirvana.

[^G-A2000-C01-S04]

Earlier notes: [N-096](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-096).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000977"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000977 -->
Through the wheel of the crucial points of cultivation,
cut the elaborations of the connection of wind-mind.

Earlier notes: [N-096](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-096).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000978"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000978 -->
Through the sequence of the path of entering knowing,
cut the conceptual minds of doubt and delusion. [N-096](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-096)

Earlier notes: [N-096](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-096).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000979"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000979 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-second reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000980"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000980 -->
By the guru, the great vajra-holder,
the sequence will be explained.

Earlier notes: [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000981"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000981 -->
The guru whose characteristics are complete
is the source of all qualities
and upholds the Ground of complete awakening. [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097)

Earlier notes: [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43), [N-T45](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t45).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000982"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000982 -->
There are two gurus: root and branch.

Earlier notes: [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000983"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000983 -->
For those whose respective characteristics are complete,
with sovereignty and one's own body,
retinue, and enjoyments, rely on [them].

Earlier notes: [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000984"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000984 -->
Pleasing [them] through bodily and verbal activity,
rely on the guru in equal union with the buddha. [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097)

Earlier notes: [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000985"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000985 -->
Endowed with faith and discerning knowing,
with aspiration and without delusion,
free from delusion and error about what is to be expressed,
with clear faculties, rely on the guru.

Earlier notes: [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000986"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000986 -->
From this reliance, the qualities [attained]
are like a wish-granting tree, a wish-fulfilling jewel,
and a cow that yields whatever is desired:
one attains immeasurable qualities.

Earlier notes: [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000987"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000987 -->
Focusing on that, rely on the guru,
for it turns one back from the battlefield of samsara. [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097)

Earlier notes: [N-097](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097), [N-T43](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t43).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000988"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000988 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-third reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000989"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000989 -->
The outer elemental signs are solidity,
heat and burning, gathering and moisture,
lightness and separation, and pervasion.

Earlier notes: [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000990"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000990 -->
Through portions of what is to be learned about the elements,
the yogin's body and speech
hold these as well.

Earlier notes: [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000991"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000991 -->
The definite times of the twelve years,
properly joined in accord with oneself:
at the beginning of each respective time,
sound, instructions, and so forth—[N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098)
from twelve magical displays of objects of focus,
the gods—among the worldly gods,
the eleven who act as protectors—
are definitely taken as objects of focus as portents,
and made to appear in Jambudvīpa. [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098)

[^G-U02225]

Earlier notes: [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000992"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000992 -->
Through their manner of vanishing into space,
the magical displays, too, are definitely distinguished.

Earlier notes: [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000993"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000993 -->
Grasping the directions of one's own place in Jambudvīpa,
to the kings of the east and so forth,
great people, common people, and others,
they definitely show virtue and nonvirtue.

Earlier notes: [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000994"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000994 -->
Likewise, the success and failure of kingdoms,
virtue and nonvirtue, are examined by the skilled person.

Earlier notes: [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000995"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000995 -->
One thereby gains accomplishment concerning entities,
and worldly higher knowing is definitely accomplished. [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098)

Earlier notes: [N-098](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000996"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000996 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000997"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000997 -->
The time is when the continuation of karma awakens,
when the elements transform, and at the crucial point of ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-099](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-099), [N-T44](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t44).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000998"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000998 -->
Through purification of delusion, one's own time is found.

Earlier notes: [N-099](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-099).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-000999"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-000999 -->
That is the time of its reversal.

Earlier notes: [N-099](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-099).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001000"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001000 -->
The teaching's hearth (thab) is the empty,
emptiness itself, and the terminator. [N-099](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-099)

Earlier notes: [N-099](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-099).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001001"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001001 -->
The remaining times of individual persons
are the same as what was taught above.

Earlier notes: [N-099](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-099).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001002"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001002 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-fifth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001003"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001003 -->
Ema! Lord of gods, sovereign,
faithful one of great fortune, listen.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001004"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001004 -->
Hidden in other continua,
known by no one,
the rite of conferring empowerment will be explained.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001005"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001005 -->
Outer, inner, secret,
and the fully complete secret:
through these four combinations,
the four—beings' bodies, speech, mental faculty, and ordinary mind—
in order to purify [them],
through four empowerments, too,
bring the fortunate to maturity. [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001006"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001006 -->
With elaborations, without elaborations,
utterly without elaborations,
and, likewise, completely without elaborations:
through these four distinctions,
the faithful mature their own continua.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001007"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001007 -->
For the purpose of [empowerment] with elaborations, too,
approach, accomplishment, and close approach
are to be undertaken by a guru whose characteristics are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001008"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001008 -->
Furthermore, with the earth rite,
preparations, lines, and spreading colors,
construct the maṇḍala that occurs in the continuum.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001009"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001009 -->
Then, for the rite of conferring empowerment, too,
beginning with the hearer vehicle,
complete the eight stages,
then confer the Great Perfection empowerment of awareness's expressiveness. [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001010"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001010 -->
Explain their respective enlightened intents,
entry, and distinctive capacities.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001011"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001011 -->
To mature the faithful who enter without elaborations,
for the supreme empowerment without elaborations,
relying on a maṇḍala and vase,
give empowerment in verses.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001012"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001012 -->
For [empowerment] utterly without elaborations,
distinguish samsara and nirvana through the secret preliminary, as below. [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100), [N-T47](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t47).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001013"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001013 -->
Then, through the characteristics of space,
abandon the obscurations of body, speech, and mental faculty.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001014"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001014 -->
Definitely examine [this] through its characteristics.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001015"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001015 -->
When activity is impure,
purify [it] through virtue appropriate to each individual.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001016"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001016 -->
For empowerment, relying on signs,
introduce the own identity of clear self-awareness. [N-T46](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t46)

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100), [N-T46](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t46).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001017"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001017 -->
The skilled person should give pith instructions that explain the signs.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100), [N-T49](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t49).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001018"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001018 -->
For [empowerment] completely without elaborations,
open the gate of the maṇḍala of ordinary mind,
and definitely undertake the bodily posture.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001019"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001019 -->
Becoming familiar with it, apply the gaze.

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001020"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001020 -->
Directly perceived awareness is the sign of authoritative transmission. [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

Earlier notes: [N-100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001021"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001021 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001022"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001022 -->
Hero Free from Conceptualization, son of the gods,
to satisfy the sovereign Delight-Maker,
the pledges depending on empowerment will be explained.

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101), [N-T50](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t50).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001023"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001023 -->
Though words do not suffice for their particulars,
in brief, relate embodiment, speech, and awakened mind
to the yogin's body, speech, and mental faculty.

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001024"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001024 -->
The objects of focus—the guru and vajra siblings—
are distinguished as the objects to be guarded.

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001025"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001025 -->
Vows are included in body and speech. [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101)

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101), [N-T51](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t51).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001026"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001026 -->
The site of the vajra secret is to be held.

[^G-A2000-C01-S05]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001027"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001027 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-seventh reply.]

[^G-A2000-C01-S06]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001028"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001028 -->
Again, Lord of Gods, Joy-Maker, listen.

[^G-A2000-C01-S07]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001029"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001029 -->
Through verbal expressions and the categories of tenet systems,
the nine stages involve the activity of the mental faculty.

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001030"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001030 -->
Separated from the crucial point of direct perception,
future sentient beings to be trained
will place their trust in words.

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001031"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001031 -->
Sentient beings with coarse conceptual thought,
because they engage in words, are not mature.

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001032"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001032 -->
At the final time of this teaching,
this is called the ground of aspirational activity. [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101)

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101), [N-T52](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t52).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001033"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001033 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-eighth reply.]

Earlier notes: [N-101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001034"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001034 -->
Beyond words, elaborations are greatly complete. [N-102](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102)

Earlier notes: [N-102](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001035"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001035 -->
This continuum of definite secrecy,
relying on the path of direct perception,
gathers one's own knowing into awareness.

Earlier notes: [N-102](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001036"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001036 -->
Gathering all buddhas,
the definite nature of phenomena of self-awareness
is enacted through evenness without mixing. [N-102](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102)

Earlier notes: [N-102](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102), [N-T46](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t46).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001037"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001037 -->
This is the stage beyond words.

Earlier notes: [N-102](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001038"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001038 -->
[Source heading: Fifty-ninth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001039"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001039 -->
The crucial point of direct perception: body, speech, and ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001040"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001040 -->
Definitely bring their respective crucial points into accord.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001041"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001041 -->
Furthermore, the body [abides] in the three embodiments;
one should never depart from them. [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001042"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001042 -->
For speech, training and abiding, [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)
and likewise passing beyond, are to be taught.

[^G-U02332]

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001043"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001043 -->
Ordinary mind is accomplished by directing it toward basic space.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001044"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001044 -->
Do not separate from the pith instructions on the three crucial points.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103), [N-T49](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t49).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001045"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001045 -->
Furthermore, rely on the crucial points of the door, object,
wind, and awareness.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001046"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001046 -->
For the door, view the three embodiments with the eyes.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001047"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001047 -->
For the object, be free of what obscures.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001048"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001048 -->
Through wind and awareness being slow, primordial knowing is accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001049"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001049 -->
In this there are four visions.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001050"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001050 -->
Through the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena,
one transcends words held through mental examination. [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103), [N-T54](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t54), [N-T53](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t53).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001051"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001051 -->
Through the vision of increasing experience,
delusive appearances are made to subside.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103), [N-T54](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t54).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001052"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001052 -->
Through the vision of awareness reaching its limit,
one transcends the path appearances of realizing the three embodiments.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103), [N-T54](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t54).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001053"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001053 -->
Through the vision of the exhaustion of nature of phenomena,
one cuts the flow of samsara of the three realms.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001054"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001054 -->
Rely on this to the end and have conviction. [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001055"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001055 -->
With a Ground in the three nonmovements,
one arrives at the crucial point of wind-mind.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001056"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001056 -->
Taking the measure through three ways of remaining,
dreams are reversed; for body, speech, and mental faculty,
signs and measures are definitely grasped.

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001057"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001057 -->
Fixing [this] with the three attainments,
contaminated aggregates do not appear. [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

Earlier notes: [N-103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001058"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001058 -->
[Source heading: Sixtieth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001059"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001059 -->
Ema! How greatly wondrous!

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001060"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001060 -->
Unless samsara and nirvana are distinguished through the secret preliminary,
the connection of the three realms with body, speech,
and mental faculty will not be cut.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104), [N-T47](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t47).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001061"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001061 -->
Therefore, I explain distinguishing samsara and nirvana through the secret preliminary.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104), [N-T47](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t47).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001062"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001062 -->
This has three aspects. [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001063"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001063 -->
For the body: walking and remaining,
compressing [the body] and manifold bodily mechanisms,
extending and gathering the limbs,
and relying on manifold actions.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001064"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001064 -->
Rousing [these] with a mindful ordinary mind,
the activities of the forms of the six destinies—
take [them] up in ordinary mind and perform [them] with the body.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104), [N-T48](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t48).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001065"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001065 -->
Whoever can engage in this way—
whatever is undertaken, its flow is later cut. [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001066"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001066 -->
This has two purposes,
examined as common and supreme.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001067"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001067 -->
When signs and measures are reached in this way,
the yogin begins verbal activity.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001068"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001068 -->
Because expression has the intrinsic nature of conceptual thought,
[use] manifold sounds and voices.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001069"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001069 -->
Because whatever is spoken has no reversal, [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)
[use] the voices of gods, nāgas, and yakṣas,
gandharvas, kumbhāṇḍas, and the All-Pervader;
utter their favorable and unfavorable varieties.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001070"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001070 -->
In brief, the voices of the six destinies:
utter ordinary mind's mindfulness and reflection through speech. [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104), [N-T48](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t48).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001071"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001071 -->
Here, too, there are two purposes:
the distinction between supreme and common culminations.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001072"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001072 -->
Having reached the measure in this way, too,
the yogin should train ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001073"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001073 -->
For past, future, and present,
rely on the wheel of mindfulness and reflection, proliferating and gathering.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104), [N-T48](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t48).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001074"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001074 -->
Ordinary minds of liking and disliking, favorable and unfavorable—
all manifold [notions] of existence and nonexistence—
examine outwardly and reflect inwardly.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001075"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001075 -->
The mindfulness of whatever is reflected on—
afterward, its flow of arising will definitely be cut. [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104), [N-T48](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t48).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001076"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001076 -->
Its purposes are distinguished by what was said above.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001077"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001077 -->
When activity has been completed in this way,
continually apply [it] to the crucial point of direct perception.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001078"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001078 -->
When that is complete, one does not enter the three realms.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001079"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001079 -->
It should be taught in utmost secrecy.

Earlier notes: [N-104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001080"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001080 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001081"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001081 -->
Relying on bodily activity,
one wishing to retain the inner wind:
the twenty-one activities of retaining wind— [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)
when the yogin performs and engages in these,
one becomes familiar with wind and proficient in the body.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001082"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001082 -->
For one endowed with ornaments,
make a wheel of dependent connections at the place.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001083"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001083 -->
Direct wind and knowing toward the ornaments.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001084"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001084 -->
While moving, apply the manner of gazing with the eyes.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001085"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001085 -->
While remaining, project ordinary mind. [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001086"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001086 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-second reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001087"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001087 -->
Through twenty-one trainings in retaining ordinary mind,
first, bring it to abide;
second, separate body and ordinary mind;
third, see the nature of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001088"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001088 -->
At this time, the crucial points of body, speech, and ordinary mind
are to be allowed to settle by one endowed with yoga.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001089"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001089 -->
Also with the signs of the wind that causes movement,
mixing, projecting, and gathering—
when the skilled person relies on these crucial points,
the flow of ordinary mind's conceptual thought is cut. [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001090"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001090 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-third reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001091"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001091 -->
There are twenty-one signs of death, too.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001092"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001092 -->
Having gone to a secluded charnel-ground place,
offer to the guru and make a torma.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001093"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001093 -->
Furthermore, with food and drink,
satisfy other beings as well.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001094"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001094 -->
Then examine the outer, inner, and secret
signs of death, too.

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001095"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001095 -->
When they are unfavorable, undertake reversal;
when favorable, make supplication to the guru. [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

Earlier notes: [N-105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001096"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001096 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001097"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001097 -->
Lord of the gods, for the sake of sentient beings,
the answer to that compassionate question you asked—
I teach it; grasp it without delusion.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001098"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001098 -->
In the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature,
although the conceptual mind of a view with a directed object of focus is exhausted,
for now, establish certainty through words. [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001099"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001099 -->
Through the crucial point of directly perceiving the nature of phenomena, too,
one realizes that activities have neither favorable nor unfavorable [status].

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001100"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001100 -->
Through definitely gathering the heart-sphere,
faculties are neither sharp nor dull.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001101"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001101 -->
Because the limit of the words of mental examination is exhausted,
one sees through the faculty of the eye of discerning knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106), [N-T53](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t53).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001102"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001102 -->
Because words do not reveal the nature of phenomena,
the common vehicles do not accomplish the meaning.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001103"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001103 -->
Because the three embodiments are path appearances,
seeing this, one does not return to the three realms. [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001104"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001104 -->
Because all phenomena are equally of one taste,
there is no dependence on Ground and result.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001105"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001105 -->
Because self-awareness is free from all conceptual thought,
there are four ways of liberation.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106), [N-T46](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t46).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001106"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001106 -->
Through confidence in liberation at the four crucial points,
samsara and passing beyond sorrow do not abide.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001107"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001107 -->
Phenomena that have not arisen and are not made
have neither Ground nor root.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001108"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001108 -->
The crucial points of liberation through hearing and seeing
are realized through two, five, and eight. [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001109"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001109 -->
Through four great confidences, one joins basic space.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001110"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001110 -->
Through three instances of pointing out, one should gain conviction.

Earlier notes: [N-106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001111"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001111 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-fifth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001112"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001112 -->
Next, the life-tree of cultivation is explained.

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107), [N-T55](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t55).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001113"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001113 -->
Definitely relate [this] through the authoritative transmission of ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001114"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001114 -->
Furthermore, concerning the crucial points of cultivation,
preliminary practice, main practice,
and subsequent practice are held to be three.

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001115"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001115 -->
Training the body, taming the nature of ordinary mind,
and completing the activities of speech,
the nature of ordinary mind enters the path.

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001116"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001116 -->
Relying on wind, body and ordinary mind separate.

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001117"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001117 -->
In order to cut the opening from which the three realms proliferate,
reverse attachment to body and speech from its depths.

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001118"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001118 -->
Because the precipices of the six destinies are equal,
press the crucial points of body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107)

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001119"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001119 -->
In order to mix the nature of ordinary mind with phenomena,
draw wind and knowing upward.

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001120"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001120 -->
In order to exhaust delusive appearances,
continually become familiar with wind. [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107)

Earlier notes: [N-107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001121"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001121 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001122"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001122 -->
For the application of activity, in basic space and awareness,
endowed with three fixings, without separation,
continual familiarity makes activity easeful.

[^G-U02484]

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108), [N-T55](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t55).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001123"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001123 -->
When all phenomena are free from doing,
and the connection of samsara and nirvana is cut,
delusion with conceptual thought is stopped.

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002667"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002667 -->
By cutting the flow of samsara's precipice,
of the great yogin's activity—[N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108)

[^G-U02489]

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002668"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002668 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-seventh reply.]

[^G-SCAN-CH1-LAYER-02489]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002669"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002669 -->
although the result cannot be expressed,
one reaches the exhaustion of phenomena,
and the limits of one's own tenet system, too, collapse.

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001125"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001125 -->
At this time, the guru's pith instructions subside.

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108), [N-T49](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t49).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001126"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001126 -->
Because the limits of viewing, cultivation, and activity are empty,
there is no appearance as phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001127"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001127 -->
Because the flow of embodiments and primordial knowing is cut,
there are neither buddhas nor sentient beings.

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001128"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001128 -->
In brief, nothing whatsoever abides.

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001129"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001129 -->
Because there is no going, there is no coming. [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108)

Earlier notes: [N-108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001130"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001130 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-eighth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001131"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001131 -->
One wishing to cut the flow of samsara,
having reversed attachment to body and speech,
should press the crucial point of direct perception.

Earlier notes: [N-109](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-109).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001132"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001132 -->
Through the application of calculating delusion,
grasp the measures of the times of samsara and nirvana,
and become skilled in the crucial point of reversal.

Earlier notes: [N-109](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-109).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001133"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001133 -->
By reaching the measure of the six capabilities,
one is thoroughly liberated from the six faults.

Earlier notes: [N-109](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-109).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001134"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001134 -->
This cuts the flow of samsara. [N-109](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-109)

Earlier notes: [N-109](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-109).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001135"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001135 -->
[Source heading: Sixty-ninth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001136"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001136 -->
Taking nourishment from vital essences is like this:
not taught in any other continuum,
Ema! It is explained as greatly wondrous.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001137"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001137 -->
Lord of gods, hero, listen!

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001138"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001138 -->
By relying on precious substances,
the lifespan becomes equal to the sun and moon.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001139"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001139 -->
Through preparations of various stones,
weapons do not penetrate the body.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001140"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001140 -->
Through preparations relying on herbs,
white hair and wrinkles disappear. [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110)

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001141"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001141 -->
Through taking nourishment from vital essences of medicinal preparations,
one becomes endowed with splendor and youthful.

[^G-U02522]

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001142"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001142 -->
Through preparations relying on meat,
the bodily constituents are sustained, and strength develops.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110), [N-T60](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t60).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001143"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001143 -->
Furthermore, relying on wind,
greatly wondrous taking of nourishment from vital essences is explained.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001144"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001144 -->
What is called cutting the flow of the elements
is known through the upper and lower winds.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001145"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001145 -->
For the skilled person, wind is easily made to abide.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001146"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001146 -->
Next, through calculating wind's movement,
there is the yoga of food.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001147"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001147 -->
Relying on the elements,
whatever appears becomes food.

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001148"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001148 -->
Through taking nourishment from vital essences in this way,
the torments of beings are cut off. [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110)

Earlier notes: [N-110](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001149"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001149 -->
[Source heading: Seventieth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001150"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001150 -->
The yoga of clothing is like this:
perform it through wind and profound dependent connections.

Earlier notes: [N-111](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-111).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001151"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001151 -->
From projecting and gathering wind,
through the crucial point of going, coming, and holding, too,
distinguish the respective elemental objects of focus
in accord with the yogin's body.

Earlier notes: [N-111](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-111).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001152"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001152 -->
Through the application of dependent connections,
with two, six, seven, and three,
properly combine pills and powders,
and carry out the culmination of ingesting and applying [them]. [N-111](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-111)

Earlier notes: [N-111](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-111).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001153"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001153 -->
[Source heading: Seventy-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001154"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001154 -->
Next, for cutting the flow of the body's elements,
greatly wondrous taking of nourishment from vital essences is explained.

Earlier notes: [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001155"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001155 -->
By continually circulating and reversing ‘great odor’ (dri chen), [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)
finally, the body's discharges are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001156"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001156 -->
Alternatively, extracted vital essence and liquid butter—
by relying on [these] in the proper measure,
the flows of the elements are cut.

Earlier notes: [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001157"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001157 -->
Because the flow of the body's elements is cut,
living creatures do not abide in the body;
there are no feces, urine, and so forth;
outer and inner wind, too, cease to be. [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)

Earlier notes: [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001158"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001158 -->
At this time, the great yogin
becomes pure with respect to both food and clothing.

Earlier notes: [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001159"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001159 -->
Through this, concerning lifespan and enjoyments,
whatever is desired is accomplished. [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)

Earlier notes: [N-112](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001160"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001160 -->
[Source heading: Seventy-second reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001161"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001161 -->
For the four elements,
a yogin who makes their tastes equal
brings the crucial points of earth, water, fire, and wind into accord.

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001162"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001162 -->
Mixing [them] with the body, raise [them] through wind.

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001163"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001163 -->
Transform one's own body through the respective elements,
and, in engaging in this itself,
cultivate the maṇḍala gathering the crucial points. [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113)

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001164"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001164 -->
Through reaching the measure of familiarity with this,
the powers of the four become equal in their own identity.

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001165"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001165 -->
One engages easefully and is free from destruction.

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001166"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001166 -->
Through this, too, both food and clothing are accomplished,
and lifespan and enjoyments are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001167"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001167 -->
[Source heading: Seventy-third reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001168"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001168 -->
The qualities of the treasury are like this:
with common and supreme accomplishments,
one plays as one pleases, accomplishing [them] easefully.

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001169"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001169 -->
The branches of dependent connections are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001170"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001170 -->
Miracles and higher knowing, too,
are definitely accomplished through engaging with the treasury.

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001171"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001171 -->
Illnesses and measures of time become known. [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113)

Earlier notes: [N-113](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001172"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001172 -->
[Source heading: Seventy-fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001173"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001173 -->
One wishing to accomplish noble miracles,
with a support that is an entity or with what is not an entity,
definitely accomplishes [them] through training in wind.

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001174"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001174 -->
Word, craft, and dependent connections;
signs, phrases, and conventions;
examples, actions, and methods of training—
relying on these and engaging in karmic wind,
one accomplishes whatever miracles are desired.

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114), [N-T78](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t78), [N-T59](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t59).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001175"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001175 -->
Through cultivating primordial knowing free from conceptualization,
reflecting on emptiness, and the crucial point, too,
without endeavor or effort, miracles
will definitely be accomplished. [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001176"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001176 -->
[Source heading: Seventy-fifth reply.]

[^G-U02597]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001177"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001177 -->
One wishing to enjoy inexhaustible treasure
relies on the mechanism of the market (khrom).

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001178"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001178 -->
Become skilled in calculating its application. [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001179"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001179 -->
Endowed with times of three, four, and two,
having concealed [it] for ten months and thoroughly dried [it],
begin directing [it] toward whichever direction is desired.

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001180"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001180 -->
By joining [it] with rays and wind,
whatever activities one wishes to undertake are accomplished. [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001181"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001181 -->
Alternatively, through profound practice of accomplishment,
distinguishing approach, accomplishment, and the culmination of activities,
rely on the lord of wealth.

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001182"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001182 -->
Through familiarity with mantra, enjoy [the treasure].

Earlier notes: [N-114](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001183"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001183 -->
[Source heading: Seventy-sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001184"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001184 -->
By bringing four and five under one's power,
the master of ordinary mind holds the body.

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002670"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002670 -->
Within the wheels of the three places, too,
with four and five syllables, and the assignment of forms, [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002671"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002671 -->
[Source annotation at U02615; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U02615]

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002672"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002672 -->
perform proliferating, gathering, absorbing, and coiling.

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001186"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001186 -->
Through completing this, there is flight,
entry without separation, and no burning;
one cannot sink and can run. [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001187"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001187 -->
[Source annotation at U02620; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U02620]

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001188"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001188 -->
The teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,
all his qualities complete in themselves,
without utterance or spoken discourse,
from the play of naturally arisen primordial knowing,
as a miraculous display of words, phrases, and names,
appears to those of good fortune.

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001189"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001189 -->
Thus, the secret phrases of the Great Perfection—
the lord of the gods, having definitely heard them,
with respectful body and palms properly joined,
rejoiced in the teacher himself. [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001190"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001190 -->
From the Great Perfection of everything,
intrinsic nature became free from elaborations,
inseparable from the basic space of the nature of phenomena. [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001191"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001191 -->
Thus, from The Great All-Penetrating Word, the Root of All Phenomena,
this is the first chapter: ‘Array of Diversity: The Definite Arising of the Root of Naturally Arisen Pith Instructions.’ [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

[^G-U02635]

Earlier notes: [N-115](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115), [N-116](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-116).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001192"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001192 -->
[Unresolved source inscription at the Chapter 1/2 boundary; no transcription supplied.]

[^G-A2000-C01-S09]
<!-- /pair -->

## Chapter 2

<a id="dtg-001193"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001193 -->
Then the lord of the gods, free from conceptual thought,
though inseparable from the basic space of phenomena,
for the sentient beings of future times,
through realization, definitely from samsara—
with the conceptual mind [intent on] liberating them,
first arousing ordinary mind,
addressed the teacher himself in these words: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117)
‘Kyé, kyé! Miraculously born embodiment!

Earlier notes: [N-116](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-116), [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117), [N-T57](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t57).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001194"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001194 -->
For sentient beings, from the samsara of the three realms,
what is the crucial point that brings liberation?

Earlier notes: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001195"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001195 -->
Blessed One, I make this request.

Earlier notes: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001196"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001196 -->
What is the beginning of sentient beings' samsara?

Earlier notes: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001197"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001197 -->
By what is the final extent of passing beyond [sorrow] grasped? [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117) [N-122](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122)

Earlier notes: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117), [N-122](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122), [N-T61](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t61).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001198"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001198 -->
What is this crucial point of buddhas' not being deluded?

Earlier notes: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001199"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001199 -->
From what does sentient beings' delusion arise?

Earlier notes: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001200"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001200 -->
Where does the primordial knowing of self-awareness abide?

Earlier notes: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001201"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001201 -->
From what does its path arise?

Earlier notes: [N-117](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001202"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001202 -->

<a id="dtg-001226"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001226 -->
Then the teacher, the vajra-holder,
from the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature free from conceptual elaborations,
though not abiding as words, phrases, or names,
spoke to the lord of the gods in these words.

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001227"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001227 -->
From the space in which conceptual thought is exhausted,
though not proclaimed as words, phrases, or names,
great naturally arisen primordial knowing itself,
from intrinsic nature, unmade from the outset,
arose thus as unarranged phrases: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)
‘The beginning of samsara:
from the undifferentiated, unmade, self and other,
an object held as a focus arose.

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001228"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001228 -->
From conceptualizing and holding, distinct appearances [arose].

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001229"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001229 -->
In the twelve connections of focusing and holding,
there is the very beginning of samsara. [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001230"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001230 -->
This is the manner of delusion through three ignorances.

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001231"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001231 -->
The single cause, undifferentiated, is the root:
not knowing [this] to be one's own identity.

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001232"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001232 -->
This, too, is the beginning of samsara.

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001233"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001233 -->
From this: one, three, and four hundred—[Numerical grouping unresolved.] [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)
definitely grasped through eight surrounding conditions—
the application of calculating delusion abiding in the Ground. [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001234"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001234 -->
From this, there is the co-emergent [ignorance]. [N-T58](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t58)

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118), [N-T58](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t58).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001235"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001235 -->
From the ultimate and the relative, apparent and imputed—
the objects belonging to the two truths—
engagement in all activities becomes twenty. [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001236"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001236 -->
From the particulars of the apparent aspects of those objects,
[imputing ignorance has] four definite conditions: [N-T58](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t58)
definitely grasped through cause and dominance,
the object of focus itself, and simultaneity. [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118), [N-T58](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t58).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001237"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001237 -->
The portions of delusion, too, are eight and two.

Earlier notes: [N-118](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001238"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001238 -->
Because the times of the objects of focus coincide,
as for the delusion imputed as formations:
for a hundred gatherings, one agent. [N-119](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-119)

<!-- /pair -->

<!-- pair: DTG-001239 -->
Through the completion of all activities, limitless [forms] arise.

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001240"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001240 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001241"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001241 -->
According to the manner of the delusion of consciousness,
the objects appearing are eight and six.

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001242"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001242 -->
Examination through knowing becomes twenty.

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001243"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001243 -->
Through this, calculate the body and limbs. [N-119](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-119)

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001244"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001244 -->
Furthermore, for the portion of delusion of name-and-form:
from naming, four designations, sixty-two—[Numerical grouping unresolved.] [N-119](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-119)
samsara and nirvana, four, together with letters.

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001245"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001245 -->
For form, distinguish fine and coarse characteristics;

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001246"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001246 -->

<a id="dtg-001247"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001247 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001249"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001249 -->
For the portion of contact, the manner of ordinary mind's delusion:

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001251"></a>

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001253"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001253 -->
From conceptual thought concerning the apparent aspect endowed with mental faculty,

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001254"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001254 -->

<a id="dtg-001255"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001255 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001257"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001257 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<!-- pair: DTG-001261 -->
For the twelve delusions of becoming,
the undifferentiated portion has sixteen.

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001262"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001262 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001263"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001266 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001267"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001267 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001268"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001268 -->
From aging and death, there are nine conditions of delusion.

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001269"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001269 -->

<!-- pair: DTG-001270 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<!-- pair: DTG-001271 -->

bodies, faculties, years,

<!-- pair: DTG-001272 -->

<!-- pair: DTG-001273 -->

<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001274"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001274 -->
For sentient beings' realms, with their many [aspects],
in order to enter, engagement in all activities to their culmination,
endowed [with these], is complete and closely taught. [N-121](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-121)

<!-- pair: DTG-001275 -->
[Source heading: Second reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001276"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001276 -->
Lord of the gods, listen to this great wonder.

Earlier notes: [N-122](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001277"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001277 -->
The final extent of passing beyond sorrow:
when all conceptual thought of delusion is exhausted,
and the appearance of the agency of samsara is absent,
delusion is self-appearance with conceptual thought exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-122](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122), [N-T61](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t61).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001278"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001278 -->
To posit transcendence, the realm of sentient beings,
in its intrinsic nature, too, has this very object:
not dependent on another, completely established;
empty of imputed activity and expression;
not distinguished as possessing an extreme. [N-122](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122)

Earlier notes: [N-122](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122), [N-T61](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t61).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001279"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001279 -->
Entering in pairs and pervading everywhere—
activities, faculties, conceptual minds, and objects—
the activity of entering is immediate. [N-122](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122)

Earlier notes: [N-122](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001280"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001280 -->
[Source heading: Third reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001281"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001281 -->
Thus, concerning the beginning and end of samsara and nirvana,
because the buddhas have not become deluded,
through a noble faculty from the Ground,
they know self-appearance to be without intrinsic nature.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001282"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001282 -->
Conceptual examination and mental faculty have not strayed outward;
movement is cut off of itself. [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123)

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001283"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001283 -->
Furthermore, in the great natural state,
what is called delusion is pure from the outset;
it cannot be contrived into nondelusion.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001284"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001284 -->
Phenomena that appear without existing, yet are not seen—
the conceptual thought that arises through doing ceases of itself.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001285"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001285 -->
There is no appearance as an object of focus through delusion.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001286"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001286 -->
Because the primordial radiance of intrinsic nature has not stirred,
all phenomena are equally of one taste.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001287"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001287 -->
Free from holding and conceptualizing, there is primordial buddhahood.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001288"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001288 -->
It cannot be contrived or transformed.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001289"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001289 -->
Its crucial point is the object:
the object, the empty, and awareness's expressiveness are equal.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001290"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001290 -->
This is not known through differentiating conceptual thought. [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123)

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001291"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001291 -->
Furthermore, the crucial point of not being deluded:
through knowing one, all are liberated.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001292"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001292 -->
Through knowing that itself, there is reversal.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001293"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001293 -->
With no appearance and no exhaustion,
conceptual thought of arising, awareness, and abiding is exhausted. [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123)

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001294"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001294 -->
Not differentiated through doing, it cannot be contrived.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001295"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001295 -->
Because there is no conceptual mind of action and agent,
with objects and conditions cleared away, there is buddhahood.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001296"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001296 -->
Free from extremes and without cessation,
one abides in fully complete deep absorption.

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123), [N-T67](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t67).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001297"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001297 -->
Because it is great, there is nothing above it,
for intrinsic nature has not stirred. [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123)

Earlier notes: [N-123](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001298"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001298 -->
[Source heading: Fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001299"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001299 -->
Sentient beings' delusion, by wandering in
the root dependent connections taught above,
arises as the foundation of calculating delusion's mechanism.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124), [N-T69](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t69).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001300"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001300 -->
From the appearance of the nature of phenomena's own radiance,
the appearance of conceptualizing and holding captivates the mental faculty.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001301"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001301 -->
The object of delusion, through subtle activity,
is obscured, and self-appearance becomes impure. [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001302"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001302 -->
From this, dormant tendencies themselves,
subtle, and the portions of particles,
come to be mere movement and holding.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124), [N-T68](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t68).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001303"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001303 -->
From knowing that becomes subtle through subtlety,
conceptual examination and the apprehended portion become two.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001304"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001304 -->
The appearance of subtle-particle primordial knowing ceases.
