Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001305"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001305 -->
Mere immediate object-knowing arises. [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001306"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001306 -->
The twelve phenomena, ignorance and so forth,
become the twelve elemental classes of beings.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001307"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001307 -->
Their inner knowing, bodies of elements,
aggregates, elements, and also
sense bases—the gatherings of activities assemble.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001308"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001308 -->
From immeasurable gatherings of afflictions,
the assemblages of conceptual thought in beings' bodies are gathered.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001309"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001309 -->
From the many gatherings of dependent connections,
there are particular discordant forms and so forth. [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001310"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001310 -->
The causes generated by the four elements
are thoroughly grouped by class and number.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001311"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001311 -->
From different, distinct colors,
they become manifest from the preceding portions.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001312"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001312 -->
Through the gathering of conceptual thoughts from the Ground,
also through twelve activities of agency,
the elemental aggregates are supported,
made to abide, form, and be destroyed.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001313"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001313 -->
From the twelve manners of delusion,
through maturing into agents,
beings' karma and merit,
and pure and impure forms,
are made to appear in their respective distinctions. [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001314"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001314 -->
Furthermore, of the environment and its inhabitants,
the activities of the elements, too, are performed by wind.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124), [N-T66](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t66).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001315"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001315 -->
Wind projects and gathers,
and makes [them] go, come, and enter.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001316"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001316 -->
Therefore, wind performs all activities.

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001317"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001317 -->
Reversal itself also depends on wind;
distinguish [it] by the culmination of activities of entering and increasing. [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

Earlier notes: [N-124](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001318"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001318 -->
[Source heading: Fifth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001319"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001319 -->
The primordial knowing of self-awareness abides in the body,
like oil from a sesame seed.

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001320"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001320 -->
The body's luster and splendor
are pervaded by the moisture of primordial knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125), [N-T65](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t65).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001321"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001321 -->
The embodiments that accord with the body,
and the signs of primordial knowing's light and colors—
through activities, entry, and embodiments,
[primordial knowing] pervades together with splendor. [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125), [N-T65](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t65).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001322"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001322 -->
Through discerning knowing itself and the activities of the lamps,
the characteristics of ultimate and relative are distinguished.

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001323"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001323 -->
The respective crucial points of the spheres that bring maturity,
the conceptual thought that causes movement and holds coarsely,
and the four and two portions of primordial knowing's appearance—
through distinctions of arising and entering activity,
in the precious palace of the heart (citta), [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)
a gathering of precious [qualities], with eight corners and doors,
there are five primordial knowings and five embodiments;
five winds, and five expressivenesses of knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125), [N-T62](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t62).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001324"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001324 -->
All have the intrinsic nature of fivefold groups. [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001325"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001325 -->
The crucial point of embodiments is through radiance;
that of primordial knowing is through its manner of abiding.

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001326"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001326 -->
Spheres are accomplished through retaining and stirring.

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001327"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001327 -->
Light is spread and gathered. [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001328"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001328 -->
The crucial point of wind is binding what is connected.

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001329"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001329 -->
The activity of entering is distinguished by its culmination.

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001330"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001330 -->
The karmic wind that causes its movement is exhausted,
and [this] is joined to the arrangement of delusion. [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001331"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001331 -->
[Source annotation at U02885; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U02885]

Earlier notes: [N-125](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125), [N-126](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126), [N-T62](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t62).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001332"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001332 -->
The crucial point of awareness is arising and
retaining and directing [it] toward space. [N-126](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126)

[^G-U02887]

Earlier notes: [N-126](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001333"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001333 -->
[Joined source fragment at U02888; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U02888][^G-U02887]

Earlier notes: [N-126](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001334"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001334 -->
[Source annotation at U02889; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U02889][^G-U02887]

Earlier notes: [N-126](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001335"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001335 -->
The crucial point of basic space is gathering,
and joining summoning with the elements' dispersal and spreading.

Earlier notes: [N-126](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001336"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001336 -->
The crucial point of appearances is increase and exhaustion;
the manifold are liberated in their own places. [N-126](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126)

Earlier notes: [N-126](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001337"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001337 -->
[Source heading: Sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001338"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001338 -->
The path of the primordial knowing of awareness:
from the wheels of one's own channels,
where the three embodiments abide complete in themselves,
Roma, Kyangma, and Kundarma—
through these three ‘mothers’ [ma], the body is held.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127), [N-T63](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t63).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001339"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001339 -->
The central [channel] supports the four wheels. [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127), [N-T63](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t63).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001340"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001340 -->
Because these abide on the right, left, and center,
through the distinction of relative and ultimate,
they generate the body and limbs
and also bring the lamps to maturity.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001341"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001341 -->
Through Roma, tastes enter the wheels:
wind stirs [them] and projects the spheres.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127), [N-T63](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t63).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001342"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001342 -->
Ro [‘taste’], through reliance on the channels,
expands body and ordinary mind through bliss.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001343"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001343 -->
Ma [‘mother’] gathers the refined parts
and makes a foundation for the upper and lower abodes. [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127), [N-T65](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t65), [N-T69](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t69).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001344"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001344 -->
Here, retaining and packing are the crucial point.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001345"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001345 -->
On its six branches,
bloodletting and cauterization obstruct primordial knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001346"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001346 -->
To attain warmth, massage is the crucial point.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001347"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001347 -->
Wind and spheres are to be summoned. [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001348"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001348 -->
Through Kyangma, in the generative wheel,
wind projects and gathers, and spheres enter.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127), [N-T63](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t63).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001349"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001349 -->
Rkyang [‘single’] is so called because it has no counterpart;
it reveals the enlightened intent of the unique.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001350"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001350 -->
Ma [‘mother’] clears away the turbid parts
and generates the splendor of essence.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127), [N-T65](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t65).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001351"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001351 -->
Here, loosening and massage are the crucial point.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001352"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001352 -->
Because its branches do not appear,
apply spheres to the four junctures of maturation.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001353"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001353 -->
One wishing to gain mastery over food
packs wind and does not discard saliva.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001354"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001354 -->
[This] is to be thoroughly examined through the pledge. [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

[^G-U02927]

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001355"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001355 -->
Through Kundarma, mindfulness
and the wheel of the summit mature through gathering.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127), [N-T63](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t63).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001356"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001356 -->
It reverses karmic wind and retains primordial knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001357"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001357 -->
Kun [‘all’] grants the qualities of the emanation embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001358"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001358 -->
'Dar [‘quivering’] [grants] the qualities of the complete enjoyment embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001359"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001359 -->
Ma [‘mother’] directly reveals the dharma embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001360"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001360 -->
For its twelve branches,
loosening and rolling are the crucial point.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001361"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001361 -->
To increase the lifespan, application [of an ointment] is taught.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001362"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001362 -->
Spheres are to be [worked with] through body and ordinary mind. [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001363"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001363 -->
From these paths of primordial knowing,
spheres come to be accompanied by embodiments.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001364"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001364 -->
Wind performs all the activities of going and coming.

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001365"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001365 -->
The master of ordinary mind is held by the mental faculty;
through the path, [it] is held and summoned. [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

Earlier notes: [N-127](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001366"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001366 -->
[Source heading: Seventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001367"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001367 -->
The door through which primordial knowing arises:
the gathering of all refined bodily vital essence
emerges through the door called cakṣu (‘eye’). [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

Earlier notes: [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128), [N-T65](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t65), [N-T62](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t62).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001368"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001368 -->
Holding the two portions, it reveals appearances. [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

Earlier notes: [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001369"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001369 -->
From the generative channel, arising in connection,
becoming one at the tip,
making the faculty arise toward its object—
one has five branches,
appearing at the respective sense gates.

Earlier notes: [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001370"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001370 -->
In particular, through its faults and actions,
from a coil like a buffalo's horn (ba-min), [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)
what is called a-bras is half white and half black. [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

Earlier notes: [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128), [N-T62](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t62).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001371"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001371 -->
It is aware and holds forms,
and completely reveals knowing's own expressiveness.

Earlier notes: [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001372"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001372 -->
Here primordial knowing actually arises.

Earlier notes: [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001373"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001373 -->
Through direct perception and its own crucial point,
in the self-appearing object of the pure nature of phenomena,
what is accompanied by conceptual thought abides in cessation. [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

Earlier notes: [N-128](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001374"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001374 -->
What brings this about?

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001375"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001375 -->
From the arising of the ultimate nature of phenomena,
there are what are called the four lamps.

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001376"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001376 -->
Through maturing, entering, and pervading emptiness,
they gather the nature of phenomena's appearance as object. [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129)

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001377"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001377 -->
The water lamp of the far-reaching lasso gathers forms. [N-T64](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64)

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129), [N-T64](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001378"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001378 -->
Wind moves and projects [them].

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001379"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001379 -->
Through increase and decrease in coming and going,
it gathers primordial knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001380"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001380 -->
The lamp of the empty sphere performs all activities. [N-T64](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64)

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129), [N-T64](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001381"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001381 -->
Taking the lead on the path of transcendence,
through wind it generates luster and splendor;
transforming [them], it reveals appearances of primordial knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129), [N-T61](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t61), [N-T65](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t65).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001382"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001382 -->
The lamp of naturally arising discerning knowing
gathers all phenomena into equal, single taste,
and holds self-appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001383"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001383 -->
This cuts the connected flow of habitual tendencies.

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001384"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001384 -->
Gathering the refined part of basic space,
and retaining the embodiments of awareness,
through maturing the three crucial points into vital essence,
it causes samsara itself to be abandoned. [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129)

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129), [N-T65](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t65).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001385"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001385 -->
Thus, because the lamps arise through the eyes,
with the crucial points of the buddhas' enlightened intent gathered,
the objects of self-appearance are held. [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129)

Earlier notes: [N-129](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001386"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001386 -->
[Source heading: Eighth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001387"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001387 -->
I will explain the crucial point of the lamps.

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001388"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001388 -->
Fortunate son of the gods, listen!

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001389"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001389 -->
Through the object, awareness, and wind,
because the nature of phenomena is taken as the path,
the nature of phenomena in which primordial knowing is complete—
this, too, is the crucial point of the lamps.

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001390"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001390 -->
The crucial point of the water lamp of the far-reaching lasso is nonmovement. [N-T64](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64)

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130), [N-T64](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001391"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001391 -->
At this time, join [it] with the three gazes.

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001392"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001392 -->
The crucial point of the discerning-knowing lamp is training.

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130), [N-T64](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001393"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001393 -->
Through three rites, increase [it],
and through the crucial point of accomplishment, expand its extent.

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001394"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001394 -->
The crucial point of the sphere lamp is packing.

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130), [N-T64](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001395"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001395 -->
By not separating the channels, thumb, and finger,
primordial knowing increases. [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130)

Earlier notes: [N-130](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001396"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001396 -->
The crucial point of basic space is guiding.

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131), [N-T71](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t71).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001397"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001397 -->
The yogin, in the methods of training with the sun,
should train thoroughly and guide [this]. [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131)

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131), [N-T71](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t71).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001398"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001398 -->
One should also train with the moon.

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001399"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001399 -->
Whoever continually does this
cuts the flow of delusive appearances.

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001400"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001400 -->
The pure light of the five primordial knowings—
earth, stones, mountains, and crags emptied of their traces—
in isolated patches and mere portions of a region,
appears to one skilled in guiding. [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131)

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131), [N-T71](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t71).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001401"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001401 -->
At this time, adjustment through methods is the crucial point.

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001402"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001402 -->
The complete nature of phenomena is attained.

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001403"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001403 -->
Through familiarity in this way, the power of the nature of phenomena
[brings] the body's elements to completion in their own places.

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001404"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001404 -->
Thus, because the flow of the body's elements is cut,
without the appearance of contaminated aggregates,
one attains manifest buddhahood in this. [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131)

Earlier notes: [N-131](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001405"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001405 -->
[Source heading: Ninth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001406"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001406 -->
The characteristics of the lamps are like this:
in general, they reveal appearances,
join primordial knowing as mother and child,
and connect the boundary of samsara and nirvana.

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001407"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001407 -->
Through their own radiance being clear, within a body of light,
they gather primordial knowing free from conceptualization.

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001408"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001408 -->
Within the appearances of objects accompanied by conceptual thought,
they hold the foundation of non-conceptual primordial knowing. [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132)

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132), [N-T77](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t77).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001409"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001409 -->
The characteristic of the far-reaching-lasso lamp:
through pervading it enters; through entering it pervades.

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001410"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001410 -->
It abides as the master-agent of the four elements.

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001411"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001411 -->
It holds, enters, spreads, and acts. [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132)

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001412"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001412 -->
The characteristics of discerning knowing:
entering clear words, it gathers the foundation;
it burns afflictions, karma, and habitual tendencies;
it matures and cuts the life-force of samsara and nirvana. [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132)

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132), [N-T77](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t77).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001413"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001413 -->
The characteristics of the empty sphere:
round, with intrinsic nature entirely clear,
red and colored, with clear light,
it spreads with elaboration. [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132)

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001414"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001414 -->
The characteristics of pure basic space itself:
blue, complete in itself, with two portions.

Earlier notes: [N-132](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132), [N-T70](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t70).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001415"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001415 -->
Through methods and discerning knowing being complete,
from the two paired portions, it holds two embodiments
and makes appearances spread abundantly.

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001416"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001416 -->
These, too, complete one's own knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001417"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001417 -->
They reveal a reflection of the all-pervading lord, the hero.

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001418"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001418 -->
They draw vajra chains from the balance (srang). [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133)

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133), [N-T72](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t72).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001419"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001419 -->
Like the form of a complete maṇḍala,
they reveal the splendor of the primordial knowings,
and also reveal the deep splendor and luster of light.

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001420"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001420 -->
There is aspiration for embodiments and spheres. [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133)

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133), [N-T72](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t72).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001421"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001421 -->
Thus, the embodiment in which primordial knowing is complete—
whoever sees this attains buddhahood.

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001422"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001422 -->
Where the three embodiments are exhausted, intrinsic nature is liberated.

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001423"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001423 -->
Crossing the precipice of apprehended object and apprehending subject,
the tether of samsara and nirvana is cut. [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133)

Earlier notes: [N-133](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001424"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001424 -->
[Source heading: Tenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001425"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001425 -->
Its crucial point of the object is like this:
in the empty outer object, free from clouds,
yoga abides in the birds' path through the sky.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001426"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001426 -->
Not separate from this is the nature of phenomena. [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001427"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001427 -->
In general, through their individual characteristics,
objects are distinct; completion in one is granted. [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001428"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001428 -->
Through primordial knowing gathering, ordinary mind's flow stops.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001429"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001429 -->
Through the elements gathering, the body is complete.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001430"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001430 -->
From appearances of great distinction,
[this] abides as the object of maturing, entering, and moving.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001431"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001431 -->
Because the limit of conceptual thought is exhausted, one is liberated from samsara.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001432"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001432 -->
The far-reaching lasso's objects are forms and so forth.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001433"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001433 -->
It gathers, holds, and projects [them].

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001434"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001434 -->
Through the crucial point, bind and place [them],
transform entities and cut the root;
draw [them] forth, and train with form. [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001435"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001435 -->
Discerning knowing's objects are words and meaning.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001436"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001436 -->
Because words and meaning are intended to be connected,
there is knowing through indication, and through expression.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001437"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001437 -->
Indication: examples and symbolic indications;
indication through words is expressed last.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001438"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001438 -->
Knowing through expression: conventions,
pointing out, guiding,
decisive resolution, and distinguishing. [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134), [N-T71](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t71).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001439"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001439 -->
The sphere's object is color:
five, six, three, two, and six.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001440"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001440 -->
Clear, warm, cool,
light, and moving—it holds these identities.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001441"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001441 -->
These are the elements of the ultimate nature of phenomena. [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001442"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001442 -->
Basic space's object is empty,
clear, free from obscuration, and emitting splendor;
pervading and vast, gathered into one.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001443"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001443 -->
In space whose fine particles have cleared,
the pure nature of phenomena is complete in evenness.

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001444"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001444 -->
Clear, it is the radiance of the five primordial knowings,
the very body of pure clear light. [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

Earlier notes: [N-134](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001445"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001445 -->
[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001446"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001446 -->
The supports of the lamps of pure primordial knowing:
in general, through support and what relies upon it,
the seeds of samsara and nirvana increase.

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001447"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001447 -->
The far-reaching lasso's support is appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001448"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001448 -->
Through the distinction of pure and impure,
it relies on karma and accumulations,
and on embodiments and primordial knowing. [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001449"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001449 -->
Naturally arising discerning knowing relies on
words, conceptual thought about is and is not,
tenet systems, viewing and cultivation,
and the nature of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001450"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001450 -->
The empty sphere relies on
consciousness itself, the intervening space,
channels, wind, and spheres,
and primordial knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001451"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001451 -->
Perfectly pure basic space relies on
the space between the eyebrows, space,
light and spheres,
and manifold embodiments. [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135), [N-T70](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t70).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001452"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001452 -->
Furthermore, the distinctive supports and objects:
through three, they rely on the distinction of gathered agency. [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001453"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001453 -->
By relying on the far-reaching lasso itself,
free from faults, having abandoned action and agency,
one engages in the mother nature of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001454"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001454 -->
Through purifying karma and habitual tendencies,
relying on the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena,
one empties samsara from its depths,
and can touch the boundary of passing beyond sorrow.

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001455"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001455 -->
Through the nature of ordinary mind in which conceptual thought and examination are exhausted,
samsara is cast far away.

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001456"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001456 -->
Having seen the embodiment of pure primordial knowing,
the point of aim of samsara and nirvana, the two, is destroyed. [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135), [N-T72](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t72).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001457"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001457 -->
Whoever sees a beautiful flower—
through this, the nature of phenomena is led into an irrigation channel.

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001458"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001458 -->
Having placed awareness on the balance (srang),
one is brought to where phenomena are exhausted. [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135), [N-T72](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t72).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001459"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001459 -->
A person who becomes familiar with basic space,
having emptied delusive appearances,
through seeing the object of the pure nature of phenomena,
emerges from the very source of objects of attachment. [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

Earlier notes: [N-135](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001460"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001460 -->
[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001461"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001461 -->
The crucial point of the stages of conceptual mind is like this:
through distinctions in the vessel of the elements,
through distinctions in the elements' junctures of maturation,
and through differences in beings' conceptual minds,
families and faculties differ.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001462"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001462 -->
Furthermore, through the magical displays of the elements:
water's earth, its activity purified,
at first can spread;
finally it does not forget the teaching it has grasped.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136), [N-T73](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t73).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001463"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001463 -->
Water's fire grasps readily and forgets.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136), [N-T73](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t73).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001464"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001464 -->
Water's wind transforms conceptual mind,
entering everything, without holding a ground. [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136)

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001465"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001465 -->
[Source annotation at U03147; no additional root text. See attached endnotes.]

[^G-U03147]

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001466"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001466 -->
Water's wind [brings] spaciousness,
and brings object and knowing into contact.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001467"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001467 -->
Water's water has a strong inclination,
with little perception, and grasps. [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136)

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001468"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001468 -->
From the pure elements of earth:
earth's water increases words
and multiplies manifold conceptual thoughts.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136), [N-T73](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t73).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001469"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001469 -->
Earth's fire [makes] the faculties supremely pure,
revealing them as clear and endowed with gentleness.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001470"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001470 -->
Earth's wind [brings] stupefaction
and interruptions, alternating at intervals.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136), [N-T74](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t74).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001471"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001471 -->
Earth's earth extensively holds joints and limbs. [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136)

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001472"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001472 -->
Fire's earth expands the faculties.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136), [N-T73](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t73).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001473"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001473 -->
[Fire's] water reveals entering and pervading.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001474"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001474 -->
[Fire's] wind makes the faculties exceedingly sharp.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001475"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001475 -->
Fire's fire grasps readily and forgets.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001476"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001476 -->
Wind's earth has great engagement with meaning.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136), [N-T73](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t73).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001477"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001477 -->
[Wind's] water makes the object of deep absorption clear.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001478"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001478 -->
[Wind's] fire enables enjoyment of words.

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001479"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001479 -->
Wind's wind holds conceptual thought and examination. [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136)

Earlier notes: [N-136](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001480"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001480 -->
When these elements are impure,
they reveal engagement in all mistaken activities.

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001481"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001481 -->
Appearances distract, and knowing changes.

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001482"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001482 -->
Conceptual thought is coarse and arouses nonvirtue.

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001483"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001483 -->
Even the ordinary mind [oriented] toward the teaching is abandoned. [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137)

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001484"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001484 -->
Thus, through the vessel of the elements being pure,
the many paths of liberation are revealed,
and the three embodiments are authentically established.

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001485"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001485 -->
Then, having entered the mother's womb,
through the calculation of years, months, and days,
and the respective elements' own times,
having definitely grasped the measures of time,
through restoring each respective element,
the mantra practitioner should diligently rely on dependent connections.

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001486"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001486 -->
Through the rite of generating the elements,
faculties are sharp, and primordial knowing is clear.

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001487"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001487 -->
Form and color are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001488"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001488 -->
One is loving toward beings and endowed with enjoyments,
maturing into one who abandons misdeeds and acts virtuously. [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137)

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001489"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001489 -->
Thus, the elements protect the vital essence.

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001490"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001490 -->
Through differences in the vessel's color,
just as the color of glass (man-shel), too, changes,
the stainless nature of ordinary mind, too,
is like that through the vessel of the elements. [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137)

Earlier notes: [N-137](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137), [N-T74](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t74).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001491"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001491 -->
[Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001492"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001492 -->
This is the crucial point of taking [the teaching] up in experience:
the support of focus and the abiding of reflection.

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001493"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001493 -->
The objects of focus are form, sound, smell, taste, and touch.

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001494"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001494 -->
Train in the domains of the five desires.

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001495"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001495 -->
For form: colors and shapes,
different crafts and illusory expressiveness,
and manifold optical illusions. [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138), [N-T78](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t78).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001496"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001496 -->
For sound: lute and booming pot,
many-stringed instrument, earthenware drum, and flute,
and sounds such as ‘phek’ and ‘har.’ [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001497"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001497 -->
For smell: combine fragrant substances,
and act through profound, agreeable dependent connections.

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001498"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001498 -->
For taste: jaggery endowed with six [unspecified aspects],
and various mixtures of grape wine are transformed.

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001499"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001499 -->
Join the branches of dependent connections. [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001500"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001500 -->
For touch, the skilled person sits on a seat.

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001501"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001501 -->
Through crossed bows, a combination of elements,
and certain leaves of trees,
through mere touch, in the nature of phenomena,
ordinary mind abides without separation. [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001502"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001502 -->
For ordinary mind: holding and abiding,
cutting the root and pursuing through entities,
searching between object and knowing,
and casting into distraction. [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

Earlier notes: [N-138](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001503"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001503 -->
[Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001504"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001504 -->
From taking [this] up in experience in this way,
the signs and measures are as follows.

Earlier notes: [N-139](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001505"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001505 -->
The body appears light, with unimpeded penetration.

Earlier notes: [N-139](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001506"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001506 -->
It does not touch the earth and is unobstructed.

Earlier notes: [N-139](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001507"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001507 -->
Speech's capacity is complete; its store of words is exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-139](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139), [N-T74](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t74).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001508"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001508 -->
Ordinary mind is capable of transference.

Earlier notes: [N-139](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001509"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001509 -->
Eye, ear, nose, tongue,
and all bodily faculties are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-139](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001510"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001510 -->
Because there is no intrinsic nature, their capacities are exhausted. [N-139](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139)

Earlier notes: [N-139](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001511"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001511 -->
[Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001512"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001512 -->
The appearances of the intermediate state:
the aggregates of abiding in intrinsic nature,
the elements, and afflictions appear as objects. [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140)

Earlier notes: [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001513"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001513 -->
At death there are mistiness and wavering,
and knowing does not remain in one [place].

Earlier notes: [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001514"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001514 -->
In the intermediate state of the nature of phenomena: embodiments,
primordial knowing, light, and colors.

Earlier notes: [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001515"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001515 -->
Spheres spread, and elemental appearances are pure.

Earlier notes: [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001516"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001516 -->
Whatever appears is the immeasurable palace of the nature of phenomena. [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140)

Earlier notes: [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001517"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001517 -->
The appearances of becoming are fitful,
fleeting, like dream appearances.

Earlier notes: [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001518"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001518 -->
Appearing true, though false, is the body of mental faculty.

Earlier notes: [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140), [N-T75](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t75).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001519"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001519 -->
White, gleg [unresolved], and so forth—the lights of the six classes—
are appearances of their respective objects of focus. [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140)

[^G-U03240]

Earlier notes: [N-140](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001520"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001520 -->
[Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001521"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001521 -->
What is called the liberation of one's own ordinary mind
is not a going from one [place] to another.

Earlier notes: [N-141](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-141).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001522"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001522 -->
Ordinary mind is not released by anyone.

Earlier notes: [N-141](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-141).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001523"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001523 -->
Complete in the Ground, there is no going or coming.

Earlier notes: [N-141](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-141).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001524"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001524 -->
It is not found by examination; there is no reason.

Earlier notes: [N-141](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-141).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001525"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001525 -->
Without Ground or root, it abides empty.

Earlier notes: [N-141](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-141).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001526"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001526 -->
That ordinary mind, abiding in its own clarity,
liberated through the crucial point, is not partial to any side. [N-141](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-141)

Earlier notes: [N-141](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-141), [N-T76](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t76).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001527"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001527 -->
Liberated through time, there is no basis of dependence.

Earlier notes: [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142), [N-T79](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t79).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001528"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001528 -->
Liberated without effort, there is no effortful accomplishment.

Earlier notes: [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001529"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001529 -->
Liberated through confidence, there is no endeavor or effort.

Earlier notes: [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001530"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001530 -->
This is explained as liberation through confidence.

Earlier notes: [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001531"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001531 -->
Liberated from the outset, there is nothing to repeat.

Earlier notes: [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142), [N-T79](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t79).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001532"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001532 -->
Liberated by itself, there is no antidote.

Earlier notes: [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001533"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001533 -->
Liberated nakedly, it vanishes where it is seen.

Earlier notes: [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142), [N-T79](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t79).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001534"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001534 -->
Completely liberated, it is effortless. [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142)

Earlier notes: [N-142](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001535"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001535 -->
[Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001536"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001536 -->
In the nature of ordinary mind liberated in this way,
it is not that compassionate responsiveness is absent.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001537"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001537 -->
Through primordial knowing that abides in the Ground,
intrinsic nature, by its own momentum, serves as a support for holding. [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143)

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143), [N-T79](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t79).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001538"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001538 -->
Through primordial knowing that holds characteristics,
purity is brought to maturity through conditions.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001539"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001539 -->
Through primordial knowing of knowing and what is to be known,
accomplishments are granted to those with aspiration and devotion.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001540"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001540 -->
Furthermore, when the nature of ordinary mind matures,
the Ground abides in the dharma embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001541"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001541 -->
The stains of what is pure from the outset are exhausted. [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143)

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001542"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001542 -->
Essence is undeluded from the outset.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001543"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001543 -->
Making appearances arise without cessation is spontaneous presence.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001544"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001544 -->
Intrinsic nature is not determined as ‘like this.’

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001545"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001545 -->
Because it pervades all, it is compassionate responsiveness.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001546"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001546 -->
Samsara and passing beyond are not cut off.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001547"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001547 -->
The primordial knowing of the complete enjoyment embodiment holds characteristics.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001548"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001548 -->
Through objects and entities being pure, there is the mirror-like [primordial knowing],
the clear reflection of samsara and nirvana.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001549"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001549 -->
There is evenness without partiality.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001550"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001550 -->
Relying on the objects of the faculties, there is discriminating [primordial knowing]. [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143) [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80)

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001551"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001551 -->
Not through effort, activity is accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001552"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001552 -->
Because there is no difference, there is the basic space of phenomena. [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143) [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80)

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001553"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001553 -->
In the emanation embodiment, there is what is to be known.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001554"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001554 -->
Through knowing how [things are], an entity's
natural state is known for one's own purpose.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143), [N-T81](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t81).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001555"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001555 -->
Through knowing the multiplicity, concerning those to be trained,
one knows how their intentions abide.

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143), [N-T81](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t81).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001556"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001556 -->
Thus, from the ways compassionate responsiveness arises,
for the different beings to be trained,
there are that many embodiments that train them. [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143)

Earlier notes: [N-143](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001557"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001557 -->
[Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001558"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001558 -->
In teaching that very crucial point of primordial knowing:
first, its manner of pervading the Ground;
in between, its completion on the path;
finally, it pervades the bodies of beings.

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001559"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001559 -->
At the time of the body, it is like this:
in the aggregates of gathered elements,
within the precious palace of the heart (citta),
the portion of essence, pure from the outset,
is the radiance of embodiment, empty and clear united.

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001560"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001560 -->
Faces and hands complete: the manner of the vase embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144), [N-T81](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t81).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001561"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001561 -->
It abides as an orb of spontaneously present light.

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001562"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001562 -->
Compassionate responsiveness is clear in distinct colors.

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001563"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001563 -->
Roma, Kyangma, and Kundarma;
the Kati crystal tube channel—
the mirror-like, evenness, and discriminating,
activity-accomplishing and basic-space-of-phenomena primordial knowings,
with moving and nonmoving spheres,
abide as supports of completion in their own channels. [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144)

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001564"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001564 -->
From the two eyes, where the refined parts gather,
through the distinction of methods and discerning knowing,
knowing how [things are] matures as the sphere [lamp] and
the lamp of pure basic space.

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144), [N-T81](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t81).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001565"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001565 -->
Knowing the multiplicity matures as the far-reaching-lasso [lamp] and
the discerning-knowing lamp.

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144), [N-T81](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t81).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001566"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001566 -->
Also concerning the karma of beings,
whatever the distinctions of their faculties,
through training in this, they become known. [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144)

Earlier notes: [N-144](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001567"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001567 -->
[Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001568"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001568 -->
The manner of abiding and the natural state have two aspects:
abiding in the initial Ground,
and abiding in the object of delusion.

Earlier notes: [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001569"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001569 -->
The initial [abiding] is as essence, intrinsic nature,
and compassionate responsiveness—the three.

Earlier notes: [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001570"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001570 -->
Furthermore, because essence abides as embodiment,
into portions of dharma, complete enjoyment, and emanation,
it is not divided: the manner of no division.

Earlier notes: [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001571"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001571 -->
Even in what is accomplished from its own condition,
the embodiment's color and so forth are not objects of conceptual mind. [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145)

Earlier notes: [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001572"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001572 -->
Intrinsic nature makes appearances arise in the manner of light:
white, red, yellow, green, and blue.

Earlier notes: [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001573"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001573 -->
Not as characteristics with delineations,
without delineation, it is accomplished from the condition of what is to be known. [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145)

Earlier notes: [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001574"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001574 -->
From compassionate responsiveness arising in manifold ways,
because it is not determined as one ‘like this,’
and manifold [appearances] arise, it is called the Ground.

Earlier notes: [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001575"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001575 -->
In the object of delusion, leave [it] of itself,
and seal leaving just as it is with self-liberation. [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145)

Earlier notes: [N-145](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001576"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001576 -->
[Source heading: Twentieth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001577"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001577 -->
Enlightened intent has not moved from one.

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001578"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001578 -->
Because appearance is clear right upon appearance,
as with a face and a mirror,
liberation—that and that itself are liberated. [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146)

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001579"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001579 -->
Because moving knowing's object is empty,
seeing, knowing, and awareness are not different.

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001580"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001580 -->
That itself is liberated right where it is.

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001581"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001581 -->
Knowing one liberates all.

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001582"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001582 -->
The numerical limits of two and three are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001583"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001583 -->
No object of conceptualizing and holding appears.

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001584"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001584 -->
Movement and holding are empty from the outset.

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001585"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001585 -->
Because empty enlightened intent is clear and complete,
the numerical limits of the many are exhausted. [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146)

Earlier notes: [N-146](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001586"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001586 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001587"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001587 -->
The crucial point of the all-basis and the dharma embodiment:
first, the all-basis will be explained.

Earlier notes: [N-147](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-147).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001588"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001588 -->
It is the basis of all phenomena and nonphenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-147](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-147), [N-T81](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t81).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001589"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001589 -->
Its essence, word explanation, and subdivisions are explained.

Earlier notes: [N-147](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-147).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001590"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001590 -->
