<!-- pair: DTG-001890 -->
Placing three in the Ground, through twenty-one,
the twenty-one appearances are complete. [N-169](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-169)

Earlier notes: [N-169](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-169).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001891"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001891 -->
[Source annotation: The ninth reply is taught in six inner points.]

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001892"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001892 -->
The entry of words is like this:
Brahmā, the All-Pervader, and the Hundred-Giver;
the Sky-Soarer, the Powerful Lord, and the Fierce One;
their respective sounds and the voices of gods.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001893"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001893 -->
By reckoning other places, one enters words. [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001894"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001894 -->
For Brahmā, the first verse,
half complete, becomes eight other places.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001895"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001895 -->
For the Powerful Lord, nine verses;
two halves of their places result.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001896"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001896 -->
For the All-Pervader, in two thousand,
through mastery of the places, one is complete. [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001897"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001897 -->
For the Hundred-Giver, five verses;
through reversing sound, three and two are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001898"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001898 -->
For the Sky-Soarer, through six verses,
distinguishing signs: seven and two.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001899"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001899 -->
For the Fierce One, entry is in three and two. [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

[^G-U03945]

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001900"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001900 -->
The activity of arranging signs: seven and four. [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

[^G-U03946]

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001901"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001901 -->
Their respective sounds, from abiding,
entering, arising, and reversing,
relying on time, are complete with five.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001902"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001902 -->
The voices of gods number six thousand.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001903"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001903 -->
Adorning them with branches doubles that.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001904"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001904 -->
From the root foundation of other places— [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)
examining words: one hundred and two places. [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

[^G-U03952][^G-U03953]

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170), [N-T88](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t88).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001905"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001905 -->
Extending from that: four hundred and four. [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

[^G-U03954]

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001906"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001906 -->
The calculation of definite particulars becomes ten. [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

[^G-U03955]

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001907"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001907 -->
By knowing how to combine these thoroughly,
one gains mastery through verses and becomes a king of speech.

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001908"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001908 -->
Through this, the supreme combination is definitely accomplished. [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

Earlier notes: [N-170](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001909"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001909 -->
[Source annotation: The tenth reply is taught in nine inner points.]

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001910"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001910 -->
What are called conceptual mind and knowing:
ordinary mind, mental faculty, movement,
proliferation, agitation, rigidity,
object, apprehending subject, and clinging.

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171), [N-T89](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t89).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001911"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001911 -->
Ordinary mind: the pure and impure
three realms, too, enter buddhahood. [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171)

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001912"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001912 -->
Mental faculty is known through essence, word explanation,
and subdivisions.

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001913"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001913 -->
Movement: portions of mindfulness and appearance,
through examination, become conceptual mind.

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001914"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001914 -->
Proliferation, from the culmination of activities toward objects,
appears as the expressiveness of conceptual mind and knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001915"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001915 -->
Agitation is the activity enacted by wind;
formations are a symbol of conceptual mind and knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001916"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001916 -->
Rigidity, from three afflictions,
changes into what is called conceptual mind and is cut by knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171), [N-T89](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t89).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001917"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001917 -->
From the two gathering, three [kinds of] knowing arise. [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171)

[^G-U03976]

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001918"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001918 -->
Objects: from the apparent aspects of the five doors,
the entry portions of conceptual mind and knowing arise.

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001919"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001919 -->
The apprehending subject: through eyes and so forth,
conceptual mind and knowing are actual in each respective [object].

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001920"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001920 -->
Clinging: conceptual mind has four measures of realization;
through the measures of knowing, these become eight.

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001921"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001921 -->
Arising from this, there are holding and attachment. [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171)

Earlier notes: [N-171](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001922"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001922 -->
[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001923"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001923 -->
Thus, from the agency of apparent aspects,
apprehended object and apprehending subject arise as two.

Earlier notes: [N-172](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001924"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001924 -->
The apprehended object is the object; the apprehending subject is ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-172](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001925"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001925 -->
Object and ordinary mind gathered together are the three poisons.

Earlier notes: [N-172](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001926"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001926 -->
The object's crucial point is in the faculties; the faculties' crucial point is ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-172](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001927"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001927 -->
Ordinary mind's crucial point is in the eyes; the eyes' crucial point is the channels.

Earlier notes: [N-172](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001928"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001928 -->
Therefore, through bodily posture,
the connection of habitual tendencies with the body is cut,
and the buddhas' enlightened intent is complete.

Earlier notes: [N-172](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001929"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001929 -->
The channels, through the wheel of dependent connections,
are the place of seeking, holding, and increasing—
the yogin's branches. [N-172](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172)

Earlier notes: [N-172](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001930"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001930 -->
[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001931"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001931 -->
From essence as primordial purity and primordial knowing,
there is no possible name ‘ignorance.’

Earlier notes: [N-173](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-173).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001932"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001932 -->
There is no count of one and two. [N-173](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-173)

Earlier notes: [N-173](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-173).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001933"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001933 -->
On examination, neither existence nor nonexistence is established.

Earlier notes: [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001934"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001934 -->
In the nature of phenomena, undivided anywhere,
not even a mere primordial knowing is established.

Earlier notes: [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001935"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001935 -->
Without phrases, nothing is established through expression. [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174)

Earlier notes: [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001936"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001936 -->
Pure self-awareness does not abide at an extreme;
the limits of the names ‘apprehended object and apprehending subject’ are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001937"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001937 -->
There is no cause, nor any number of conditions;
there is no dual appearance, object, or possessor of an object.

Earlier notes: [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001938"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001938 -->
No characteristic is differentiated anywhere;
coarse objects and conditions are self-exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001939"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001939 -->
Absent from the beginning, pure through purity,
with deluded conceptual thought ceased, it does not enact anything.

Earlier notes: [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001940"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001940 -->
Since it has not arisen, cessation is empty. [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174)

Earlier notes: [N-174](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001941"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001941 -->
[Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001942"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001942 -->
What is called primordial knowing of intrinsic nature's spontaneous presence
does not arise, does not cease, and directs enlightened intent toward nothing,
because no object is fixed. [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175)

Earlier notes: [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175), [N-T93](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t93).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001943"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001943 -->
Since expressiveness and qualities do not cease,
it appears merely as play:
the foundation in which diversity is complete. [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175)

Earlier notes: [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001944"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001944 -->
Because it does not exist, it appears; because it appears, it is empty.

Earlier notes: [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001945"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001945 -->
It has the subsidiary aspects of appearance and emptiness entering together;
it is the pure domain of buddhas and sentient beings.

Earlier notes: [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001946"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001946 -->
Thus it manifestly abides as what is called ‘the Ground.’

Earlier notes: [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001947"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001947 -->
Abiding merely as intrinsic nature,
it is empty when examined from the aspect of essence.

Earlier notes: [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001948"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001948 -->
Because its subsidiary aspects unfold, knowing is complete.

Earlier notes: [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001949"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001949 -->
Since it abides of itself in spontaneous presence,
rejecting and accepting, the two, are absent here. [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175)

Earlier notes: [N-175](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001950"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001950 -->
[Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001951"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001951 -->
From primordial knowing of all-pervading compassionate responsiveness,
the inexhaustible gates of diverse arising
are complete in essence, though appearing to be exhausted. [N-176](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176)

Earlier notes: [N-176](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001952"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001952 -->
From the empty intrinsic nature of the dharma embodiment,
the aspect of primordial knowing whose knowing is complete
arises for sentient beings through awakened mind.

Earlier notes: [N-176](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001953"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001953 -->
Without that, the central connection of samsara and nirvana would be severed;
through knowing, there is awareness and clarity. [N-176](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176)

Earlier notes: [N-176](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001954"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001954 -->
From the very identity of clear self-awareness,
through the force of intrinsic nature, compassionate responsiveness itself
has not ceased and does not cease.

Earlier notes: [N-176](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001955"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001955 -->
From the pure aspect of the elements,
the one that is not an activity is complete. [N-176](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176)

Earlier notes: [N-176](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001956"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001956 -->
Like light from the sun,
it is inherent and unobscured.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001957"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001957 -->
[Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001958"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001958 -->
Then I shall explain the characteristic-holding
primordial knowing of the complete enjoyment embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001959"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001959 -->
Mirror-like primordial knowing makes forms clear;
the appearance aspects of shape and color are complete. [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001960"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001960 -->
It is white because stains are purified.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001961"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001961 -->
Appearing inherently, it joins samsara and nirvana.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001962"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001962 -->
Because it sees the forms of all phenomena,
it is called ‘primordial knowing through itself.’ [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001963"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001963 -->
Clear and empty, it is liberated into emptiness;
conceptual thought at the limits of liberation and grasping is exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001964"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001964 -->
With no other, the Ground of liberation is complete.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001965"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001965 -->
[Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001966"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001966 -->
What is called ‘evenness’
is through two causes of evenness, three conditions of evenness,
and the time and measure of evenness.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001967"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001967 -->
Here there is no duality, and it is free from awareness. [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001968"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001968 -->
Neither divided nor dividing, it abides of itself.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001969"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001969 -->
What is called ‘even’ is, in the nature of phenomena,
freedom from the elaborations of pain.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001970"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001970 -->
‘-ness’ means there is no contrivance or accomplishment through effort.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001971"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001971 -->
Intrinsic nature abides; essence is empty.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001972"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001972 -->
In the nature of ordinary mind, all conceptual thought is exhausted.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001973"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001973 -->
What is called ‘primordial knowing’ abides;
through realizing its characteristics,
one does not abide in either samsara or nirvana. [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001974"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001974 -->
[Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001975"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001975 -->
Discriminating [primordial knowing]: the types of faculty,
whatever appears to them, and the phenomena of that appearance
are each clear in sequence. [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001976"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001976 -->
When the self-appearance of examination is purified,
awareness itself becomes the object.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001977"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001977 -->
What is called ‘individually’ arises from distinctions
in the manner of an antidote.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001978"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001978 -->
What is called ‘realization’ is seeing the characteristics,
together with the increase of self-appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001979"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001979 -->
Primordial knowing arises at the beginning;
through knowing, the faults of afflictions are purified. [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001980"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001980 -->
[Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001981"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001981 -->
What is called ‘doing accomplished’:
when striving and effort have self-ceased,
all phenomena rest of themselves and are self-liberated.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177), [N-T80](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001982"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001982 -->
From ground-appearance in which self-liberation is complete,
the subtle aspect attains freedom from particles and stains.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001983"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001983 -->
Beyond the object of conceptual thought and examination,
it is the nature of phenomena, divided by no one.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001984"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001984 -->
Intrinsic nature being complete, the limits of appearance are exhausted.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001985"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001985 -->
Simultaneous realization is ‘doing.’

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001986"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001986 -->
Through being ‘accomplished,’ desire is absent;
there is neither repetition nor reversal.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001987"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001987 -->
‘Primordial’ has the meaning of abiding;
through ‘knowing,’ it becomes manifest,
and one reaches the place where phenomena are exhausted. [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001988"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001988 -->
[Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001989"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001989 -->
The basic space of phenomena is vast;
no edge or center is an object of focus.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001990"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001990 -->
In the pure nature of phenomena, the Ground of liberation,
‘phenomena’ means engaging in activity:
doing the doings of the transcendent and the world.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001991"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001991 -->
Intrinsic nature is clear and limpid.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001992"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001992 -->
‘Basic space’ opens room;
it is great naturally arisen abiding.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001993"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001993 -->
‘Primordial’ is the very identity of abiding,
uncontrived from the beginning.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001994"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001994 -->
Through ‘knowing,’ one is liberated from both samsara and nirvana.

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001995"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001995 -->
This is enlightened intent in which self-appearance is complete. [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

Earlier notes: [N-177](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001996"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001996 -->
[Source heading: Twentieth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001997"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001997 -->
Primordial knowing encompassed by knowing
pervades all buddhas and sentient beings
indivisibly, without intrinsic nature.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001998"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001998 -->
In buddhas it is completely pure;
it abides unstained and free from particles.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-001999"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-001999 -->
In the appearance aspect of sentient beings of the six classes,
it abides in each one's continuum:
in gods, it is complete in its own clarity;
in asuras, subtle and flashing;
in humans, round and clear of itself;
in animals, clear within;
in hungry ghosts, subtle;
in hell, complete as self-appearance. [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002000"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002000 -->
Through ‘knowing,’ there is awareness, clear in its own place.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002001"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002001 -->
Through ‘encompassed,’ essence is self-awareness.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002002"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002002 -->
‘Primordial’ is the clear aspect of knowing and awareness;
‘knowing’ is self-liberation, a single mandala. [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002003"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002003 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002004"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002004 -->
Primordial knowing encompassed by objects of knowledge
is to be known in two aspects.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002005"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002005 -->
Of these, I shall explain objects of knowledge in their multiplicity.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002006"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002006 -->
Through this, the intentions of those to be trained are known.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002007"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002007 -->
Through understanding others' benefit,
one knows the intrinsic nature of phenomena.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002008"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002008 -->
‘Whatever’ refers to the natural state;
‘multiplicity’ completes everything without remainder.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002009"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002009 -->
‘Primordial’ concerns the benefit of all sentient beings;
through ‘knowing,’ the three realms are drawn out from their depths. [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002010"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002010 -->
The knowing aspect of the emanation embodiment
is complete in the identity of self-appearance.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002011"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002011 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-second reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002012"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002012 -->
I shall explain the primordial knowing that knows how [things are].

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002013"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002013 -->
The appearance aspect is free from a basis for expression;
the reflection of pure self-appearance is clear.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002014"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002014 -->
Through knowing the natural state of things,
and realizing one's own benefit, the flow of delusion is exhausted.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002015"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002015 -->
‘How’ refers to the natural state;
‘view’ engages without distraction. [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002016"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002016 -->
‘Knowing’ is complete because it does not cease.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002017"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002017 -->
‘Primordial’ has become the path;
‘knowing’ has reached its measure.

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002018"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002018 -->
There is no movement from the Ground
of great uncontrived self-liberation. [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

Earlier notes: [N-178](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002019"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002019 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-third reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002020"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002020 -->
The gathering of primordial knowings
has pervasive and clear characteristics;
intrinsic nature is clarity-emptiness, indivisible,
and essence is not differentiated anywhere.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002021"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002021 -->
Gathered into four divisions,
it shows distinctions in the methods by which conceptual thought enters.

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002022"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002022 -->
With Ground, core, and flowers,
it is beautiful and truly complete. [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179)

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002023"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002023 -->
Through the manner of pervasion and that which pervades,
it shows self-appearance in the Ground
and exhausts differentiating conceptualization.

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002024"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002024 -->
Distinguishing the gathered special features,
from the self-radiance of the nature of phenomena, free from conceptual thought,
there arises an appearance of primordial knowing without an object of focus.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002025"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002025 -->
There the yogin's ordinary mind is complete.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002026"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002026 -->
As a subsidiary aspect of the true gathering,
the crucial point of primordial knowing arises from the eyes.

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002027"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002027 -->
Through knowing the eyes' gaze,
one engages together with the basic space of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002028"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002028 -->
In order to exhaust the three realms,
the pulsating channel behind the eyes
is turned upward, bringing down primordial knowing. [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179)

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002029"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002029 -->
Whatever primordial knowing arises from the crucial point,
just as the nature of phenomena endowed with awareness,
is completely liberated from all conceptual thought:
it is primordial knowing arising from basic space.

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002030"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002030 -->
The crucial points of the eyes are above and below;
whichever avenue of effort the adept employs,
there is the appearance of all-gathering primordial knowing. [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179)

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002031"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002031 -->
Alternatively, in the primordial knowing of the eyes,
through clearly distinguishing the five colors,
there is a likeness of the form of a complete mandala.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002032"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002032 -->
The experiential acquaintance arising from this
cannot be expressed by anyone—
Ema! Such great wonder!—
an appearance complete in qualities.

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002033"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002033 -->
From pervasive primordial knowing, subtle in aspect,
it is through direct perception and on the path. [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179)

Earlier notes: [N-179](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002034"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002034 -->
Furthermore, this is to be explained:
from mindfulness and reflection in which differentiating conceptualization gathers,
even the dharma embodiment becomes form. [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180)

Earlier notes: [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002035"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002035 -->
One who wishes to go beyond the three realms,
through the great crucial point of sacred primordial knowing,
examines the respective winds as breaths.

Earlier notes: [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002036"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002036 -->
From the self-purification of stains in all phenomena,
the three embodiments are complete in the center of the body.

Earlier notes: [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002037"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002037 -->
Why is there conceptual thought concerning this?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002038"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002038 -->
From the intrinsic nature of gathered mindfulness and reflection,
even the dharma embodiment becomes samsara. [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180)

Earlier notes: [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002039"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002039 -->
When the limits of samsara are exhausted,
one is liberated in one's own place without anything being done.

Earlier notes: [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002040"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002040 -->
In this liberation there is nothing at all.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002041"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002041 -->
Because there is nothing, the nature of phenomena is not a thing.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002042"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002042 -->
Primordial knowing being complete, the flow of delusion is cut;
being cut, going and coming are exhausted.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002043"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002043 -->
Because they are exhausted, it is empty primordial knowing.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002044"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002044 -->
Since the primordial knowing of the three embodiments has not ceased,
the existence of the three realms is empty.

Earlier notes: [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002045"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002045 -->
By finding going and arising,
things being pure, form is exhausted. [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180)

Earlier notes: [N-180](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002046"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002046 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the primordial knowing of appearance.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002047"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002047 -->
From earth, water, fire, wind, and space,
through the wonder of primordial knowing's appearance,
the blessed protector is without awareness. [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

Earlier notes: [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002048"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002048 -->
Primordial knowing, not found on examination,
decisively resolves the three realms
and cuts the flow of clinging at the five gates.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002049"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002049 -->
The moon, a jewel, ‘mar gar,’ and light—
one who wishes to hold these
holds the appearance of embodiment arising from holding. [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

Earlier notes: [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181), [N-T92](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t92).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002050"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002050 -->
Then, through knowing the crucial point
of the Great Perfection, primordial knowing
is free from doing, just like space.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002051"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002051 -->
Through complete realization, it is free from blue. [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

Earlier notes: [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002052"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002052 -->
Wondrous, marvelous primordial knowing
was absent before, is absent afterward, and absent from the beginning.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002053"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002053 -->
Now it is beyond the object of differentiating conceptualization:
an empty intrinsic nature free from extremes.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002054"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002054 -->
Beyond phrases, free from the conceptual mind, empty in essence,
its intrinsic nature not differentiated anywhere,
the deeds of compassionate responsiveness do not appear. [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

Earlier notes: [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002055"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002055 -->
From the third appearance of primordial knowing,
discerning knowing arising from the mental faculty is held as an aspect of delusion. [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

Earlier notes: [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181), [N-T94](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t94).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002056"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002056 -->
Here there is no limit of differentiated focus.

Earlier notes: [N-181](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002057"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002057 -->
From the marvelous play of absence,
the result of the buddhas is found.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002058"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002058 -->
Outside and inside are all the basic space of phenomena;
here nothing whatsoever appears.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002059"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002059 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the primordial knowing of objects.

Earlier notes: [N-182](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-182).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002060"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002060 -->
Through distinctions of form and sound,
the experiences to be trained in become five.

Earlier notes: [N-182](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-182).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002061"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002061 -->
There is the appearance of the exhaustion of knowing's experience;
increase is shown like the waxing moon.

Earlier notes: [N-182](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-182).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002062"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002062 -->
With each appearance, an experience comes. [N-182](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-182)

Earlier notes: [N-182](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-182).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002063"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002063 -->
Through warmth, measure, and signs,
the two truths enter in union, and the limits of conceptual thought are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-183](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002064"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002064 -->
With no doing, characteristics are empty.

Earlier notes: [N-183](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002065"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002065 -->
The basis of expression having ceased, one does not abide at an extreme.

Earlier notes: [N-183](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002066"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002066 -->
Since outflows are exhausted, there is no material weight.

Earlier notes: [N-183](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002067"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002067 -->
The interior of mindfulness ceases; essence is clear. [N-183](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183)

Earlier notes: [N-183](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002068"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002068 -->
Clear awareness does not conceptualize the limits of objects.

Earlier notes: [N-183](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002069"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002069 -->
Free from words, truly without phrases,
all bases of dependence are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-183](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002070"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002070 -->
Furthermore, one directly engages the desirable qualities.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002071"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002071 -->
The two trainings through form
are a support for focus and freedom from focus.

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002072"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002072 -->
Through the special activity agreeable to the mental faculty,
first make offerings to the lama.

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002073"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002073 -->
Become accustomed to shapes and then to colors;
through this familiarity, delusory appearance ceases.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002074"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002074 -->
For sound, with a pure support for focus:
piwaṃ, earthenware drum, resonant pot,
draba, the many-stringed instrument, the three-junctioned instrument,
har, flute, cymbals,
pheg, rdob, small hand drum, bell, and so forth—
join wind to each respective sound. [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184)

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184), [N-T97](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t97).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002075"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002075 -->
For smell, through a condition for focus agreeable to the mental faculty,
faith and great faith,
the supreme and the greatly distinctive,
join the yogin to great bliss. [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184)

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002076"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002076 -->
For taste, an adept combines cane sugar, that endowed with six [tastes],
grape wine, and rice beer.

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002077"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002077 -->
Knowing the five stages endowed with restraint,
merely tasting makes primordial knowing blaze. [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184)

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002078"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002078 -->
For touch, with a support for focus agreeable to the mental faculty,
join each awareness to experiential acquaintance.

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002079"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002079 -->
When a tree leaf curls rightward and contracts,
raise it: through touch, buddhahood is realized. [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184)

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002080"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002080 -->
Concerning phenomena, accord with the lama's authoritative transmission.

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002081"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002081 -->
When excellent cultivation is realized,
practicing thus brings it onto the path,
and the flow of the machinery of delusion in the three realms is cut.

Earlier notes: [N-184](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002082"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002082 -->
Furthermore, the appearance of primordial knowing:
from the white of stainless purity,
because qualities are complete, the form of appearance
becomes the indivisible, complete form embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002083"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002083 -->
From the yellow that generates qualities,
in an activity in which the Ground and differentiation are simultaneous,
the nature of phenomena is shown. [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185)

Earlier notes: [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002084"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002084 -->
From the red of complete self-expressiveness,
the center and surrounding of pure intrinsic nature,
the encircling bands and enclosure, are shown in completeness.

Earlier notes: [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002085"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002085 -->
From the green free from doing-expressiveness,
emanations of pure enlightened activity arise.

Earlier notes: [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002086"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002086 -->
From the blue of unchanging completeness,
not abiding at edge or center, it is pervasively empty.

Earlier notes: [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002087"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002087 -->
Through pure intrinsic nature, the embodiments are ‘three, four.’ [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185)

Earlier notes: [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002088"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002088 -->
Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:
the third chapter, the Array of Primordial Knowing, in which the root of appearance definitely emerges.

[^G-U04305]

Earlier notes: [N-185](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002089"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002089 -->
[Unresolved source graphic at the Chapter 3/4 boundary; no transcription supplied.]

[^G-C3-TRANSITION]
<!-- /pair -->

## Chapter 4

<a id="dtg-002090"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002090 -->
Then the Lord of Gods, Maker of Joy,
asked the teacher, the great all-pervading lord,
about the intrinsic nature of the Array of the Nature of Phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002091"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002091 -->
‘Kye kye! Truly complete buddha,
through the Array of Primordial Knowing,
I have gained respite from the activities of samsara,
and been uplifted on the path of passing beyond sorrow. [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186)

[^G-U04312]

Earlier notes: [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002092"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002092 -->
I have also seen the object of intrinsic nature's appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002093"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002093 -->
Although I have gained confidence in the buddhas' enlightened intent,
for sentient beings who will enter in the future,
so that primordial knowing free from conceptual thought may be complete,
I seek the Array of the Nature of Phenomena in evenness. [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186)

Earlier notes: [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002094"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002094 -->
Where is the nature of phenomena of abiding to be applied?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002095"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002095 -->
What is the crucial point of the intervals of movement?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002096"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002096 -->
How is the nature of phenomena of time distinguished?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002097"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002097 -->
What is the nature of phenomena of buddhas like?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002098"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002098 -->
How does it abide at the time of sentient beings?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002099"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002099 -->
What are the crucial points of the body?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002100"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002100 -->
How does it emerge from the gates?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002101"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002101 -->
What are the characteristics of appearing in the object?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002102"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002102 -->
As what does it appear there? [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186)

Earlier notes: [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002103"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002103 -->
Having seen, by what is it decisively established?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002104"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002104 -->
Having arrived, what is the measure of completeness?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002105"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002105 -->
When the measure is reached, what becomes of the body?
What is ordinary mind like?
What is the nature of phenomena when connection is severed?

[^G-A2000-C04-S01]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002106"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002106 -->
By what is the nature of phenomena of cause and result accomplished?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002107"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002107 -->
What is the nature of phenomena that clears in its own place?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002108"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002108 -->
What are the definitive phrases of the nature of phenomena?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002109"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002109 -->
What are its crucial points?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002110"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002110 -->
How is this crucial point of the time of arising held?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002111"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002111 -->
What is the nature of phenomena of the intermediate state like?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002112"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002112 -->
How do its characteristics appear?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002113"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002113 -->
Speak of these for the benefit of sentient beings.’
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002114"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002114 -->
Then, [the words of] the teacher Vajradhara,
from the space of pure intrinsic nature—
these elaborations of words and phrases—
arose in the Ground free from expression. [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186)

Earlier notes: [N-186](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002115"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002115 -->
‘Ema! Marvelous wonder!
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002116"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002116 -->
I shall teach an expression of wonder
unheard of in other fields.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002117"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002117 -->
[Source heading: First subject of inquiry.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002118"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002118 -->
The nature of phenomena of abiding is as follows:
abiding in its fundamental disposition, it is free from virtue and wrongdoing;
abiding as cause and result, it is free from dependence;
abiding through conditions, the elements are pure—
it does not shift or change. [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187)

Earlier notes: [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002119"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002119 -->
[Source heading: Second subject of inquiry.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002120"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002120 -->
The nature of phenomena of the intervals of movement is like this:
I shall explain it fully as a great wonder.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002121"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002121 -->
Wind, according to the sequence of a day,
is reckoned as twenty thousand, one thousand,
and six hundred;
the winds of one day are thereby complete.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002122"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002122 -->
According to the particular distinctions,
for the young there are sixty extra;
for the old there is a shortfall.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002123"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002123 -->
This is through the activity of the elemental vessel. [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188)

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002124"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002124 -->
Among sixty movements of wind,
the subtle movement of the nature of phenomena is held through reckoning.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002125"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002125 -->
From two such groups it is coarse;
when these are gathered as three, it is gross.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002126"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002126 -->
Subtract three from that: it is even. [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188)

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002127"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002127 -->
In brief, within twenty movements,
there is one evenness of the nature of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002128"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002128 -->
[Source heading: Third subject of inquiry.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002129"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002129 -->
The nature of phenomena of time is as follows:
outer, inner, secret,
and suchness—four.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002130"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002130 -->
The outer is the second [month] of autumn,
showing the abiding of the nature of phenomena of time.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002131"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002131 -->
The inner is the time when dawn breaks.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002132"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002132 -->
The secret is sleep and dreams.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002133"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002133 -->
Suchness is held through wind.

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002134"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002134 -->
When the time is reached, it is complete. [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188)

Earlier notes: [N-188](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002135"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002135 -->
[Source heading: Fourth subject of inquiry.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002136"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002136 -->
The nature of phenomena of buddhas:
undivided, not dividing, even—
how could words, phrases, and names exist there?

Earlier notes: [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002137"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002137 -->
The basis of dependence is self-purified, and delusion is purified.

Earlier notes: [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002138"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002138 -->
It is not known by the names samsara and nirvana.

Earlier notes: [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002139"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002139 -->
Not having come about or arisen, it does not cease.

Earlier notes: [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002140"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002140 -->
All-arising primordial knowing is not any [thing]. [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187)

Earlier notes: [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002141"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002141 -->
There is no taking and no liberation.

Earlier notes: [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002142"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002142 -->
In a moment free from causes and conditions,
buddhas and sentient beings appear as a foundation,
free from all multiplicity and parts. [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187)

Earlier notes: [N-187](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002143"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002143 -->
[Source heading: Fifth subject of inquiry.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002144"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002144 -->
As for deluded sentient beings:
embodiments and primordial knowing are in their own manner,
genuine, fresh, and letting be—
abiding solely in ‘gyi nar’ [unresolved expression],
intrinsic nature at the faculties' objects. [N-189](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189)

Earlier notes: [N-189](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189), [N-T95](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t95).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002145"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002145 -->
Here their own object is shown;
without entering into surrounding conditions,
in unobstructedness and penetration,
from the appearance aspects of object and knowing,
each is directly liberated from its own Ground. [N-189](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189)

Earlier notes: [N-189](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002146"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002146 -->
The desirable qualities are self-complete.

Earlier notes: [N-189](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002147"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002147 -->
In the respective crucial points of diverse movement,
knowing one, one abides liberated.

Earlier notes: [N-189](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002148"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002148 -->
Thus the nature of phenomena is realized,
free from parts.

Earlier notes: [N-189](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002149"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002149 -->
[Source heading: Sixth subject of inquiry.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002150"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002150 -->
The crucial points of the body are as follows:
the general [body], the heart, and the channels—
the pure, uncontrived nature of phenomena abides there. [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190)

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002151"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002151 -->
Throughout the bodies of all sentient beings,
the nature of phenomena pervades in the manner of wind.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002152"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002152 -->
Subtle, light, moving, and separating:
through these four ways of functioning,
know the yoga of the four times.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002153"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002153 -->
Within the heart it abides as embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002154"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002154 -->
The embodiments of radiance have five aspects;
they hold the characteristics of their respective families.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190), [N-T98](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t98).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002155"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002155 -->
The six embodiments of rays
gather the phenomena of intrinsic nature's appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190), [N-T98](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t98).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002156"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002156 -->
Through the three aspects of pure embodiment,
the characteristics of passing beyond sorrow are held.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002157"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002157 -->
Through the eight aspect-embodiments,
the doing of completing grounds and paths is done. [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190)

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190), [N-T98](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t98).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002158"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002158 -->
Within the channels are spheres.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002159"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002159 -->
Through three aspects of entering spheres,
the seeds connecting samsara and nirvana are planted.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190), [N-T98](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t98).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002160"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002160 -->
Through the five arising spheres,
the mandala of yogic experience is established.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190), [N-T98](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t98).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002161"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002161 -->
Through the six arraying spheres,
the manifold nature of phenomena is gathered into one.

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190), [N-T98](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t98).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002162"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002162 -->
Through reliance on each of these,
all phenomena have the identity of evenness. [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190)

Earlier notes: [N-190](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002163"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002163 -->
[Source heading: Seventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002164"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002164 -->
As for how it emerges from the gates:
impelled by entering wind,
the four lamps become four activities.

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002165"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002165 -->
Moved by pervading wind,
vajra chains [arise] from awareness itself.

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002166"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002166 -->
Gathered by ripening wind,
they appear as embodiments in fivefold pairs. [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191)

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002167"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002167 -->
From the eyes, the two winds of apprehended object and apprehending subject:
through the right, the apprehended aspect, appearance increases;
through the left, the apprehending aspect, colors are complete. [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191)

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002168"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002168 -->
At the ears, from pervading and entering winds,
the right, through pervasion, displays sounds;
the left, through the activity of entering wind,
holds each respective [sound].

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002169"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002169 -->
The wind of complete pervasion at the crown,
subtle and without generation,
also holds the embodiments of awareness. [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191)

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002170"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002170 -->
[Source heading: Eighth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002171"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002171 -->
The characteristics of appearing in the object:
from space, clear blue and unstirred,
light, colors, and shapes
display the self-appearance of the five primordial knowings.

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002172"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002172 -->
From the lamp of pure basic space,
spheres, embodiments, and purified delusory appearance—
intrinsic nature itself and essence itself—
appear nondually, without joining or separating. [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191)

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002173"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002173 -->
At this time the characteristic of exhaustion
is just like space, free from doing.

Earlier notes: [N-191](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002174"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002174 -->
[Source heading: Ninth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002175"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002175 -->
As for by what it is seen:
it is seen through familiarity and the crucial points.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002176"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002176 -->
Familiarity starts with the preliminaries;
applying the crucial points of body and voice,
one sees the forms of pure self-appearance,
and all delusion subsides.

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002177"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002177 -->
Through the body at the crucial point, seeing occurs.

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002178"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002178 -->
Through the lion posture of the dharma embodiment,
free from all fear of delusion,
one sees with the vajra eye.

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002179"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002179 -->
Through the posture of the complete enjoyment embodiment,
relying on the reclining elephant,
one fully enjoys the nature of phenomena
and sees with the lotus eye.

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002180"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002180 -->
Through the posture of the emanation embodiment,
relying on the crouching sage,
the nature of phenomena emanates of itself,
and one sees with the dharma eye. [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192)

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002181"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002181 -->
Furthermore, through channels and bodily exercises,
one sees the five primordial knowings of intrinsic nature's appearance
with the water-bubble eyes. [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192)

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192), [N-T96](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t96).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002182"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002182 -->
[Source heading: Tenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002183"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002183 -->
Having seen, one applies the crucial point.

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002184"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002184 -->
Through methods of guiding appearance,
the appearances of the four elements reach the crucial point.

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002185"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002185 -->
By reckoning the connections of the eight winds' activities,
manifold differentiating conceptualization reaches the crucial point.

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002186"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002186 -->
For the body: compressing, shaking out expressiveness,
and the crucial points of striking, extending, and gathering the limbs;
through subtlety, straightening, and massage—
in flesh, blood, and bone. [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192)

Earlier notes: [N-192](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002187"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002187 -->
[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002188"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002188 -->
Having thus arrived, the measures of completeness are:
cessation of what appears directly before one;
introducing appearance into earth and stone;
one's own knowing entering anything;
through that entry, moving insentient things;
taking the measure of wind's movement; [N-193](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-193)
seeing the body's minute particles arises;
whatever appears is complete as form embodiment;
there, father and mother embrace;
fivefold fathers and mothers join;
all are within a round encircling band.

Earlier notes: [N-193](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-193), [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002189"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002189 -->
The yogin's body too is clear light;
going, coming, and abiding—
all are awareness reaching its measure. [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194)

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002190"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002190 -->
[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002191"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002191 -->
The manner of reaching the measure also becomes like this:
free from the particles of each body,
windows of light appear in the intervening space.

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002192"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002192 -->
From these, rays shaped like hooks,
subtle and entwining, hold appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002193"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002193 -->
The body's material weight self-ceases:
a stainless body of light with unimpeded penetration.

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002194"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002194 -->
At its center is the sign of A,
and rays from the brow curl measure one armspan.

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002195"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002195 -->
The topknot appears held back by wind. [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194)

[^G-U04519]

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002196"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002196 -->
From the fingers of the peaceful embodiments,
garlands of light gather densely.

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002197"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002197 -->
There is activity of ordinary mind without sound.

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002198"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002198 -->
From the palace of the skull in the head,
Herukas manifestly arise.

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002199"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002199 -->
At this time one's body is brought to its measure:
the three embodiments, with unimpeded penetration, gathered into one. [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194)

Earlier notes: [N-194](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002200"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002200 -->
[Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002201"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002201 -->
At this time ordinary mind is clear;
through the six higher knowings,
phenomena that are far away or measurable
are known in an instant.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002202"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002202 -->
Immeasurable buddha fields
and likewise embodiments appear to ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002203"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002203 -->
In empty ordinary mind, stains are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002204"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002204 -->
Intrinsic nature does not move; emptiness and clarity are even.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002205"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002205 -->
Unmixed, complete in evenness, it pervades basic space.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002206"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002206 -->
Appearance and existence are clear, free from multiplicity.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002207"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002207 -->
Without parts, there is no elaboration. [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195)

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002208"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002208 -->
[Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002209"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002209 -->
At the times of such appearances,
the connection between body and ordinary mind is severed.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002210"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002210 -->
Even in the body whose contaminated [elements] are exhausted,
ordinary mind, characterized by clarity, emerges outward.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002211"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002211 -->
In the manner of a shooting star passing across,
it is clearly seen in the element of space. [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195)
