Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002212"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002212 -->
At the initial times of this,
even in separation from wind,
through effort and the activity of pure wind,
it arises from being projected outside the body.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002213"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002213 -->
Through the two aspects of what sees,
the body is pure clear light;
ordinary mind is like proliferating sparks,
not abiding in one place, a Ground of its own clarity.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002214"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002214 -->
It arises in the manner of two appearance aspects.

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002215"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002215 -->
Through this, the connection between body and ordinary mind is severed,
and one does not return to the three realms. [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195)

Earlier notes: [N-195](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002216"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002216 -->
[Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002217"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002217 -->
The nature of phenomena of cause and result:
when the source of coarse conceptual thought has ceased,
in the continuum of self-purified mindfulness,
dependent connections and wind itself are completely pure.

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002218"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002218 -->
It is the nature of phenomena arising from wind. [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196)

[^G-U04562]

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196), [N-T99](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t99).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002219"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002219 -->
In intrinsic nature, pure from the beginning,
the numbers one and many are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002220"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002220 -->
There are no embodiments or primordial knowing;
having reached the limit where phenomena are exhausted,
this is held to be the nature of phenomena of the result. [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196)

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002221"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002221 -->
[Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002222"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002222 -->
The nature of phenomena that clears in its own place:
when the elements clear, material weight is purified;
particles, subtle and coarse, cease at their limit,
and not even a mere part abides.

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002223"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002223 -->
When afflictions clear, delusion clears.

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002224"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002224 -->
Free from conceptual thought, clinging does not abide;
neither dormancy nor manifest arising—
not even a particle's portion abides.

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002225"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002225 -->
Through the clearing of subtle and coarse in this way,
the five aggregates gradually become subtle.

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002226"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002226 -->
When the body of the four elements is exhausted,
all is embodiment complete in primordial knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002227"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002227 -->
Deep absorption free from conceptualization abides of itself.

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002228"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002228 -->
Beyond contrived phenomena,
it does not abide even in the phrase ‘phenomena.’ [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196)

Earlier notes: [N-196](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002229"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002229 -->
[Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002230"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002230 -->
The definitive phrase ‘nature of phenomena’ is thus:
‘phenomena’ means gathering and assembling,
doing doings and holding characteristics,
showing self-appearance
and holding the respective classes.

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002231"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002231 -->
‘Nature’ means no contrivance;
abiding in letting be, it genuinely pervades.

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002232"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002232 -->
Fresh, genuine, and in its original course,
the number of words, phrases, and names is exhausted. [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197)

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002233"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002233 -->
[Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002234"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002234 -->
The nature of phenomena of the crucial points is as follows:
the elements' abodes, one's body,
objects, gates, knowing, wind,
channels, spheres, and subsidiary aspects—
each respective entry abides pervasively.

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002235"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002235 -->
Through transforming their respective preliminaries
and the abiding of the main practice,
in the nature of phenomena free from conceptual thought,
intrinsic nature abides and movement is exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002236"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002236 -->
From the object in which exhaustion and appearance are nondual,
through intrinsic abiding and contrived [practice],
the basic space of phenomena also appears through purity.

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002237"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002237 -->
Through this, buddhahood comes through the crucial points. [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197)

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002238"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002238 -->
[Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002239"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002239 -->
The crucial point of the time of arising is like this:
Ema! I shall explain a great wonder.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002240"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002240 -->
This nature of phenomena has eight times of arising,
and just as many times of abiding.

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002241"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002241 -->
Also at four distinctive times,
when the yogin's ordinary mind abides,
there is evenness without joining or separating.

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002242"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002242 -->
Knowing the crucial point of pure intrinsic nature,
one knows phenomena without anything being done.

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002243"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002243 -->
Time is held through the number of transitions;
through holding it, there is purity without delusion. [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197)

Earlier notes: [N-197](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002244"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002244 -->
[Source heading: Twentieth reply, in full.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002245"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002245 -->
The nature of phenomena of the intermediate state is like this:
I shall explain the most secret nature of phenomena.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002246"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002246 -->
Distinguished, there are four aspects;
here I shall explain the intermediate state of the nature of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002247"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002247 -->
When released from the moment of death,
consciousness dissolves into space.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002248"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002248 -->
When space dissolves into clear light,
all subtle and coarse appearances cease.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002249"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002249 -->
A body of light endowed with all faculties
becomes like a shadow from the body. [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198)

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002250"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002250 -->
At that time, when light appears in fragments,
the adept holds the spreading of appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002251"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002251 -->
Through the spreading of moving wind,
light and rays of five colors
appear in uncertain rainbow patterns.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002252"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002252 -->
From entering and ripening winds,
the four directions, intermediate directions, above and below are established.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002253"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002253 -->
As the directions' lights gather there,
embodiments of the five families also manifest.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002254"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002254 -->
Blue, white, yellow, red, and green
appear with their respective objects.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002255"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002255 -->
Knowing how to spread and hold them,
one completes the buddhas' qualities
and does not enter the three realms. [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198)

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002256"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002256 -->
[Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002257"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002257 -->
Their characteristics are like this:
free from dependence on coarse elements,
spheres blaze as rays in the light.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002258"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002258 -->
Five connected spheres are single embodiments;
from half-embodiments, forms become complete.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002259"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002259 -->
From embodiments with half-forms, there are fivefold pairs.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002260"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002260 -->
As each cluster becomes complete,
there are groups of five, ten, and a hundred;
clusters of a thousand and a hundred thousand
appear pure in the intrinsic nature of what is envisaged. [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198)

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002261"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002261 -->
Innumerable, beyond what expression can encompass—
when one oneself knows their intrinsic nature,
one attains the three embodiments gathered into one.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002262"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002262 -->
From this attainment there is no return.

Earlier notes: [N-198](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002263"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002263 -->
It comes from making this clear. [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199)

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002264"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002264 -->
Furthermore, the essence of the nature of phenomena
is established as essence from intrinsic nature itself.

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002265"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002265 -->
Since its manner of appearance is not fixed as one,
the paths of entry also differ.

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002266"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002266 -->
Through identity, knowing, and seeing liberation,
through entering, seeing, and familiarity—
from recognition, there is one's own identity;
through trust, one reaches it;
through decision, one enters confidence.

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002267"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002267 -->
Through these three certainties,
essence being complete, the continuum ceases. [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199)

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002268"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002268 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the intrinsic nature of the nature of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002269"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002269 -->
Not fixed as one thing of this kind,
it appears however it is designated.

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002270"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002270 -->
From the basis for assigning diverse names,
it appears as elaborations of many words and phrases.

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002271"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002271 -->
Names are uncertain and diverse;
meaning too is empty through mere designation.

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002272"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002272 -->
Since emptiness is not established as anything,
its intrinsic nature appears pure. [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199)

Earlier notes: [N-199](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002273"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002273 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the compassionate responsiveness of the nature of phenomena.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002274"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002274 -->
Since the domains of those to be trained are diverse,
the embodiments that train them are likewise.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002275"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002275 -->
Through the transformations of classes and faculties,
conceptual thoughts of samsara and nirvana differ.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002276"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002276 -->
When each is left in its own object,
this is enlightened intent of the uncontrived meaning.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002277"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002277 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the appearance of the nature of phenomena.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002278"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002278 -->
In impure delusion,
without contriving the gates of the faculties,
the crucial point is leaving [them] as they are;
not altering that is the pith instruction. [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200)

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200), [N-T101](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t101).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002279"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002279 -->
In appearance without an apprehended object,
ordinary mind arises without an apprehending subject.

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002280"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002280 -->
It is seen by eyes without activity;
drawn along a path without a nature of phenomena;
carried to a Ground without view and cultivation;
gathered into a result with nothing to do, free from effort.

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002281"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002281 -->
Here the limits of appearance do not abide. [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200)

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002282"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002282 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of awareness.

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002283"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002283 -->
Through naked seeing in vajra chains,
the mindfulness of moving differentiating conceptualization ceases.

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002284"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002284 -->
Like going in space along the birds' path,
there is nowhere to go from the basic space of phenomena.

[^G-U04703]

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002285"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002285 -->
With no other, awareness-appearance being completely pure,
the causes and conditions of delusive appearance are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002286"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002286 -->
Since basic space and awareness are inseparable,
the connected appearance of vajra chains is shown.

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002287"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002287 -->
The essence of awareness is vajra chains:
in past, future, or present,
no one has made them.

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002288"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002288 -->
Thus being unconditioned
is the intrinsic nature of vajra chains.

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200), [N-T100](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t100).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002289"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002289 -->
All differentiating conceptualization being pure,
there is buddhahood without delusion. [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200)

Earlier notes: [N-200](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002290"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002290 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of the continuum.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002291"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002291 -->
The true nature of phenomena penetrates beyond words;
its own essence is without letters.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002292"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002292 -->
The lamp-light of self-aware primordial knowing blazes.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002293"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002293 -->
With the nature of phenomena pure, auspiciousness is beautiful.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002294"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002294 -->
The view, free from fear, completes the lion's expressiveness;
with self-awareness ripened, completeness arises naturally;
vajra chains connect, a garland of pearls;
appearances of experience form an array of jeweled inlay;
self-abiding emptiness is the mirror of the core;
deviations are cut of themselves: the mirror of awakened mind.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002295"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002295 -->
Not arising from another, it is self-arising itself.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002296"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002296 -->
Because it arises naturally, there is self-liberation.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002297"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002297 -->
One in basic space, there are also six expanses.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002298"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002298 -->
With qualities complete, precious things are heaped.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002299"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002299 -->
Self-awareness being taught, it is adorned with pointing out.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002300"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002300 -->
With appearance pure, sun and moon are joined.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002301"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002301 -->
Because awareness ripens, embodiment-relics blaze.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201), [N-T102](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t102).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002302"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002302 -->
All of these arise from the Ground. [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201)

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002303"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002303 -->
Thus, because the nature of phenomena is inexpressible,
it arises as the foundation of conventional phrases.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002304"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002304 -->
The sequences of expressed phrases being completely pure,
one thus abides in the inexpressible.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002305"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002305 -->
Therefore the limits of phrase-sequences are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-201](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002306"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002306 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of liberation.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002307"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002307 -->
Liberated through the crucial points, striving and effort are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002308"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002308 -->
Liberated from the beginning, there is no need to repeat.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002309"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002309 -->
Because it is self-liberation, there is no antidote.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002310"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002310 -->
Because it is naked liberation, it vanishes where it is seen.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002311"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002311 -->
Completely liberated, intrinsic nature is pure.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002312"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002312 -->
Liberated through time, there is no need for familiarity.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002313"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002313 -->
Liberated through intrinsic nature, it is uncontrived.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002314"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002314 -->
‘Liberated’ is merely a convention.

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002315"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002315 -->
Whose phenomena are realization and non-realization?

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002316"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002316 -->
For whom there is ‘liberated,’ what is the object of focus? [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202)

Earlier notes: [N-202](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002317"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002317 -->
For whom could entry into the three realms occur?

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002318"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002318 -->
This is the nature of phenomena liberated from the limits of existence.

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002319"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002319 -->
Furthermore, the nature of phenomena free from doing:
with no doing, there is liberation;
with no arising, there is no abiding.

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002320"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002320 -->
With no going, coming and circling are exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002321"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002321 -->
There is neither one nor two of what is done or arisen.

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002322"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002322 -->
There is not even mere conventional delusion.

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002323"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002323 -->
It is empty of entry into activities of existence and nonexistence.

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002324"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002324 -->
From the beginning, the result is free from the conceptual mind,
beyond all speaking, thinking, and expression. [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203)

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002325"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002325 -->
Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:
the fourth chapter, the Array of the Nature of Phenomena, teaching the root of ordinary mind's appearance.

[^G-U04763]

Earlier notes: [N-203](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002326"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002326 -->
[Unresolved source graphic at the Chapter 4/5 boundary; no transcription supplied.]

[^G-C4-TRANSITION]
<!-- /pair -->

## Chapter 5

<a id="dtg-002327"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002327 -->
Then, through completely pure intrinsic nature,
from the inexpressible dharma embodiment,
from the space of the nature of phenomena, free from conceptual elaborations,
from the inexpressible foundation,
phrases of meaning in a naturally arisen array
arose like light proliferating from the teacher's tongue,
without being made by anyone. [N-204](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-204)

Earlier notes: [N-204](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-204).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002328"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002328 -->
The Lord of Gods supplicated:
‘Kyema! Blessed one, lord without delusion,
by whom will the Array of View and Cultivation
be held?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002329"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002329 -->
What is the natural state established in its own disposition?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002330"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002330 -->
How should one view it?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002331"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002331 -->
Distinguish the boundary between viewing phrases and meaning.

Earlier notes: [N-204](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-204).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002332"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002332 -->
What are the essence and intrinsic nature of view?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002333"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002333 -->
What are the divisions of expressed phrases and names?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002334"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002334 -->
What is that called cultivation like?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002335"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002335 -->
What are its activity and understanding?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002336"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002336 -->
What cultivation abides without deviation?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002337"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002337 -->
How do cultivation's characteristics appear?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002338"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002338 -->
What are the faults of not cultivating?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002339"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002339 -->
I wish to hear these.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002340"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002340 -->
Vajra-holder, speak to me.’
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002341"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002341 -->
Then, from uncontrived space,
the one bearing thirty-two marks
spoke the Array of View and Cultivation thus:
‘Lord of Gods, listen well.

Earlier notes: [N-204](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-204).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002342"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002342 -->
Whoever doubts this
obscures the embodiments of the buddhas.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002343"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002343 -->
[Source heading: First reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002344"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002344 -->
Those who hold view and cultivation
have excellent faith and diligence,
are able to give and respect the lama,
possess disciplined observance and avoid wrongdoing;
unchanging and utterly stable, they hold it.

Earlier notes: [N-205](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-205).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002345"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002345 -->
Alternatively, one whose own impetus is quiet,
letting be, with a simple person's relaxation,
whose realization is small and whose intrinsic nature is quiet,
who is free from doings of body and voice,
and possesses discerning knowing, will hold it. [N-205](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-205)

Earlier notes: [N-205](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-205).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002346"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002346 -->
[Source heading: Second reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002347"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002347 -->
The natural state established in its own disposition
also has seven manners of abiding:
spontaneous presence from the side of diversity;
indeterminacy from the aspect of the extent of change;
certainty through not changing;
appearance's expressiveness transforming into anything;
all arising, together with affirmation;
primordial purity because delusion is purified;
and the variegation held to be diverse manners of appearance. [N-206](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-206)

Earlier notes: [N-206](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-206).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002348"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002348 -->
Through such distinctions in what is asserted,
because the Great Perfection appears as anything,
although differing assertions are numerous,
in the view of abiding in its own disposition
there is no one, no two, and freedom from anything.

Earlier notes: [N-206](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-206).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002349"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002349 -->
Even taking life and taking what is not given
arise from me and are my phenomena. [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207)

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002350"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002350 -->
View, cultivation, and activity too
are beyond the extremes of existence, nonexistence, and emptiness.

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002351"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002351 -->
In brief, they are distinguished through characteristics.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002352"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002352 -->
[Source heading: Third reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002353"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002353 -->
There are two ways of viewing this:
viewing phrases and viewing meaning.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002354"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002354 -->
Viewing phrases has seven stages;
their respective assertions are constructed by the conceptual mind:
freedom from doing and absence of striving;
gradual entry and drawing out distinctions of self and other;
taking life, and freedom from virtue and wrongdoing;
nothing done and nothing arisen;
unaltered appearance, self-purified;
and afflictions held to be liberated in intrinsic nature. [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207)

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002355"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002355 -->
Meaning is viewed in direct perception of the nature of phenomena,
not through discerning knowing,
but as the very domain of the faculties.

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002356"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002356 -->
It is not decisively reached through phrases.

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002357"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002357 -->
In the identity of seeing the three crucial points,
because the three phrases are explained as crucial points,
there are no sharp or dull faculties.

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002358"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002358 -->
Because seeing the nature of phenomena purifies,
there is no good or bad in activities.

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002359"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002359 -->
The nature of phenomena is empty and without divisions;
virtue and wrongdoing, both, are removed. [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207)

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002360"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002360 -->
Without seeing self-awareness directly,
the assertions of the nine stages do not liberate.

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002361"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002361 -->
When the nature of phenomena is exhausted and its limit reached,
the three embodiments are the appearance of the path.

Earlier notes: [N-207](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002362"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002362 -->
[Source heading: Fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002363"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002363 -->
The boundary between the views of phrases and meaning:
phrases are causes of expression;
whatever expresses or produces an emanation
is mental examination that thinks about it.

Earlier notes: [N-208](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002364"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002364 -->
Meaning is oneself viewing oneself.

Earlier notes: [N-208](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002365"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002365 -->
Through the three measures of direct perception,
one engages directly with vajra chains.

Earlier notes: [N-208](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002366"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002366 -->
Through naked seeing and naked liberation,
whatever one sees is meaning. [N-208](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208)

Earlier notes: [N-208](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002367"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002367 -->
[Source heading: Fifth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002368"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002368 -->
The essence of view is seeing,
unchanged by phrases of mental examination.

Earlier notes: [N-208](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002369"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002369 -->
Neither authoritative transmission nor the lama's pith instructions
shows it by any [means]. [N-208](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208)

Earlier notes: [N-208](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002370"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002370 -->
[Source heading: Sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002371"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002371 -->
The intrinsic nature of view pervades environment and inhabitants;
nothing is unpervaded, so anything is liberated.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002372"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002372 -->
In liberated phenomena, differentiated grasping is absent.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002373"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002373 -->
Whatever appears is one's own nature of phenomena;
there is no separate contrived nature of phenomena.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002374"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002374 -->
However one acts, non-conceptuality arises.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002375"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002375 -->
There is self-appearance without rejecting and accepting.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002376"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002376 -->
[Source heading: Seventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002377"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002377 -->
The divisions of expressed phrases and names
are outer, inner, secret,
and unsurpassed—the four views.

Earlier notes: [N-209](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002378"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002378 -->
The outer is viewing bearers of properties:
in the respective assertions of the eight stages,
mental examination and grasping are maintained. [N-209](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209)

Earlier notes: [N-209](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209), [N-T103](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t103).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002379"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002379 -->
The inner: viewing the nature of phenomena,
one sees essence and intrinsic nature as illusion.

Earlier notes: [N-209](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002380"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002380 -->
The secret: viewing self-awareness,
one sees its uninterrupted connection with vajra chains.

Earlier notes: [N-209](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002381"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002381 -->
Suchness is basic space and awareness. [N-209](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209)

Earlier notes: [N-209](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002382"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002382 -->
[Source heading: Eighth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002383"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002383 -->
What is called cultivation is ordinary mind abiding,
cutting outer and inner movement,
and ceasing apprehended object and apprehending subject.

Earlier notes: [N-210](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-210).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002384"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002384 -->
What arises from intrinsic nature's own disposition
is to be called meditative stabilization.

Earlier notes: [N-210](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-210).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002385"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002385 -->
Wind, channels, spheres,
the body's crucial points, the eyes, and emptiness—
these are held to be cultivation.

Earlier notes: [N-210](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-210).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002386"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002386 -->
The light and colors of letters,
and the emanations' proliferating and gathering,
are explained as deep absorption.

Earlier notes: [N-210](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-210), [N-T106](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t106).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002387"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002387 -->
Uncontrived, resting of itself, is the natural state.

Earlier notes: [N-210](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-210).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002388"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002388 -->
Because there is no change from that, however [it occurs],
it is the buddhas' enlightened intent. [N-210](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-210)

Earlier notes: [N-210](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-210).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002389"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002389 -->
[Source heading: Ninth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002390"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002390 -->
Its activity and understanding:
through activity, clinging to objects is cut,
afflictions and the like are rejected,
and the root of samsara is drawn out.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002391"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002391 -->
Through understanding, the nature of ordinary mind is held,
made to abide, and wind is exhausted;
the path of the empty nature of phenomena is found,
and there is ground-appearance of pure intrinsic nature.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002392"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002392 -->
[Source heading: Tenth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002393"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002393 -->
Cultivation that abides without deviation:
the faculties' gates are clear,
and the mental faculty's conceptual thought does not move.

Earlier notes: [N-211](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-211).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002394"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002394 -->
The mental faculty that examines knowing itself
does not escape outward.

Earlier notes: [N-211](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-211).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002395"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002395 -->
It is not a clarity marked by divisions;
it has not gone into an emptiness cut off separately;
it is not a continuous succession of bliss;
it has not become a stagnant pool of equanimity. [N-211](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-211)

Earlier notes: [N-211](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-211).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002396"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002396 -->
Resting in clear mindfulness and awareness;
training's contrary aspects having become the path;
an apprehending aspect with a continuous inward ease;
an apprehending aspect clear in its manner of parts;
and casting out colors and syllables—
when one has not fallen to the side of any of these,
this is cultivation abiding without deviation. [N-211](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-211)

[^G-U04923]

Earlier notes: [N-211](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-211).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002397"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002397 -->
[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002398"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002398 -->
The characteristics and manner of appearance of cultivation:
its characteristic is empty, clear awareness;
its intrinsic nature is mixed with nothing;
the limits of apprehended object and apprehending subject are exhausted;
and the nature of phenomena is pure in its own identity.

Earlier notes: [N-212](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002399"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002399 -->
Its manner of appearance is not delusion.

Earlier notes: [N-212](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002400"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002400 -->
In the intrinsic nature of pure causes and conditions,
things and absence of things are self-purified;
clinging to the objects of the five gates is purified;
the order of the elements is purified;
and the increasing appearance of pure primordial knowing is shown.

Earlier notes: [N-212](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212), [N-T104](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t104).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002401"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002401 -->
There is the nature of phenomena of exhaustion. [N-212](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212)

Earlier notes: [N-212](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002402"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002402 -->
[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002403"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002403 -->
The faults of not cultivating are:
the characteristics of samsara manifest;
self and other, objects and knowing,
a view bound to phrases,
and objects bound to focus—
afflictions bind [one] as a self.

Earlier notes: [N-212](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002404"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002404 -->
The path of buddhahood is also lost;
the intrinsic nature of the result is not known;
there is no Ground of the evenness of all phenomena;
self-awareness is bound in the three realms;
and one falls into what is accompanied by conceptual thought. [N-212](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212)

Earlier notes: [N-212](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002405"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002405 -->
[Source heading: Below this, the condensed meaning is explained.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002406"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002406 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the wheel of the nature of phenomena.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002407"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002407 -->
Through the view of abiding, all phenomena are realized.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002408"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002408 -->
With intrinsic nature pure, samsara and nirvana are distinguished.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002409"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002409 -->
With nothing done, there is arising.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002410"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002410 -->
Brought onto the true path, it pervades.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002411"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002411 -->
Completely arisen, the nature of phenomena is empty.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002412"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002412 -->
With no doing, non-arising is complete.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002413"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002413 -->
Samsara being purified, there is passing beyond sorrow.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002414"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002414 -->
Furthermore, the view of pure seeing:
the great completeness of self-purified absence of things.

Earlier notes: [N-213](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002415"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002415 -->
There is no need to make offerings to deities;
there is no mantra recitation or austerity;
no incense, lamps, or food offerings to deities;
no gathering offerings or offerings to the lama;
no praise or burnt offerings;
no scope of activity of the six perfections.

Earlier notes: [N-213](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002416"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002416 -->
Thus, with no Ground or root whatsoever:
taking life, taking what is not given, and unchaste activity— [N-213](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213)
there is no view, no cultivation, and no activity.

Earlier notes: [N-213](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002417"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002417 -->
With nothing to view or enact, what assertion of cultivation is there?

Earlier notes: [N-213](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002418"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002418 -->
Ordinary mind's assertions of emptiness and clarity cease.

Earlier notes: [N-213](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002419"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002419 -->
Likewise, phenomena and the phenomena of buddhahood are absent. [N-213](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213)

Earlier notes: [N-213](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002420"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002420 -->
Furthermore, the view of intrinsic nature:
the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature, without things.

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002421"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002421 -->
Earth, water, fire, and wind—the elements—are manifestly buddha.

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002422"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002422 -->
Without contrivance, there is great self-liberation itself.

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002423"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002423 -->
Desire, hatred, dull confusion, and awakening—four. [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214)

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002424"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002424 -->
The activity of differentiating conceptualization and karma is confidence in realization.

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002425"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002425 -->
Without rejecting samsara, there is the buddhas' enlightened intent.

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002426"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002426 -->
Body, voice, and mental faculty being refined, the three embodiments are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002427"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002427 -->
Ordinary mind, ignorance, and delusion are the Ground of the nature of phenomena.

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002428"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002428 -->
Karma, habitual tendencies, and clinging are crucial points of view. [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214)

Earlier notes: [N-214](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002429"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002429 -->
Furthermore, the contrived view:
for a buddha that has not arisen from phenomena,
a result complete without being generated by a cause,
one takes a body not destroyed by conditions.

Earlier notes: [N-215](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-215).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002430"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002430 -->
With tenets not arising from authoritative transmission,
and pith instructions not relying on the continuum,
one cuts the root of passing beyond sorrow,
and makes the result of samsara ripen.

Earlier notes: [N-215](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-215).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002431"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002431 -->
An untrue, impure Ground—
in the foundation of the six afflictions,
the seed of awakening is emptied out.

Earlier notes: [N-215](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-215).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002432"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002432 -->
Forms of the three lower destinies ripen,
and the three embodiments too are ‘lag bcangs’ [unresolved]. [N-215](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-215)

Earlier notes: [N-215](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-215), [N-T105](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t105).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002433"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002433 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the view of what is expressed.

Earlier notes: [N-216](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-216).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002434"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002434 -->
If life is not cut, one does not become buddha. [N-216](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-216)

Earlier notes: [N-216](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-216).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002435"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002435 -->
If the windows are not closed,
the darkness itself is not cleared. [N-216](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-216)

Earlier notes: [N-216](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-216).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002436"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002436 -->
The teacher of the buddhas,
at the king of Dharma in hell,
leads through Dharma to the three lower destinies.

Earlier notes: [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002437"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002437 -->
Through practicing wrongdoing, virtue is attained. [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217)

Earlier notes: [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002438"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002438 -->
Through viewing, [one enters] samsara in the three realms;
through cultivating, one gathers all the hosts of afflictions;
through enacting activity, [one enters] deviation and obscuration;
the result is the very cause of samsara. [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217)

Earlier notes: [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002439"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002439 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the connection of symbolic meaning and phrases.

Earlier notes: [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002440"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002440 -->
In the midst of an expanse of the conflagration of an age,
a person, without being burned,
expounds the Dharma of the scriptural collections from his mouth,
while in action cutting another's life—
and his virtuous practice increases. How strange! [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217)

Earlier notes: [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002441"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002441 -->
Inside a spacious iron house without a door,
in the midst of an expanse of lightless darkness,
although sun, moon, and clear light are there,
no one sees them.

Earlier notes: [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002442"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002442 -->
The objects of each one's faculties are clear,
yet there is no seeing. How strange! [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217)

Earlier notes: [N-217](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002443"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002443 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the view that apprehends objects.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002444"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002444 -->
Emptying the Ground of samsara in the three realms,
one sweeps away passing beyond sorrow.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002445"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002445 -->
Cutting the root of ignorance and samsara,
one uproots the result of the three embodiments.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002446"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002446 -->
Putting on the armor of darkness, one extinguishes the sun;
the clear means of seeing is covered by darkness.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002447"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002447 -->
Giving an edge its sharp temper, not even one [thing] is cut.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002448"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002448 -->
With great breadth and an empty center, not even one [thing] fits.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002449"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002449 -->
Gathering diversity, one is not found.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002450"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002450 -->
Conceptual thought at the extremes of existence and nonexistence is cleared. [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218)

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002451"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002451 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the view of desiring ordinary mind.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002452"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002452 -->
Striking all, not even one is cut;
striking one, all are to be killed.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002453"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002453 -->
A hundred people in iron fetters—not one is freed;
holding one, all are freed.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002454"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002454 -->
Through freedom, the path of liberation above is exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002455"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002455 -->
The cause of entering activity ceases. [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218)

Earlier notes: [N-218](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002456"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002456 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the wheel of activity.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002457"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002457 -->
Since there is no arising, there is no self;
neither taking nor casting away is the seal of embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002458"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002458 -->
Do not search for elements; do not reject what exists.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002459"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002459 -->
Arouse desire, but do not become attached.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002460"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002460 -->
Having become attached, let go.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002461"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002461 -->
Do not disparage another's body or set [your own] above it.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002462"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002462 -->
Do not interrupt the flow of mantra and gestures.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002463"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002463 -->
Do not reject tenets; do not reject Dharma.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002464"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002464 -->
Do not disparage companions or the lama. [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219)

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002465"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002465 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain pledges and vows.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002466"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002466 -->
Lamas and buddhas are supports for offerings;
to Dharma, the community, chosen deities,
and vajra ḍākinīs,
do not cut the flow of gathering offerings and other offerings.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002467"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002467 -->
On seeing a lama endowed with the characteristics,
make praise and offerings complete.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002468"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002468 -->
Do not disparage women; do not become angry.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002469"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002469 -->
Do not disparage the aggregates; rely on the chosen deity.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002470"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002470 -->
Relying on mandalas, mantras, deities,
and gestures is authentic.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002471"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002471 -->
Do not break up gatherings of the faithful;
do not disturb the ordinary minds of those possessing virtue.

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002472"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002472 -->
Possessing vows, abide in the pledges. [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219)

Earlier notes: [N-219](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002473"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002473 -->
Furthermore, the stages of training:
complete the body's activity,
and thoroughly cut speech.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002474"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002474 -->
Let ordinary mind abide, and examine and investigate it.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002475"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002475 -->
In the stages of progressively entering activities,
know the measures and characteristics.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002476"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002476 -->
Constantly, through ointments and pills,
introduce the channels into a net of spheres. [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220)

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002477"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002477 -->
Garlands, necklaces, and half-[necklaces] too,
beautiful and encircling, are to be connected.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002478"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002478 -->
The doing of activity is clear and without things.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002479"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002479 -->
Take the foremost of supreme accomplishments;
make the wheel of adornments at the six places. [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220)

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002480"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002480 -->
Furthermore, reliance and entry:
relying on the lama ceases the flow of samsara;
relying on empowerment ripens body and voice;
relying on pledges brings accomplishments;
through reliance on the view, the three realms are cut off;
through reliance on cultivation, the flow of delusion stops;
relying on activity trains the body.

[^G-U05079][^G-A2000-C05-S01]

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002481"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002481 -->
Whatever arises from these objects of reliance
is itself pledges and vows.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002482"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002482 -->
Activities of entry have eight aspects;
bodily entry is fivefold;
voice has eight aspects;
ordinary mind's entry is thirty-twofold.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002483"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002483 -->
The special functioning of body and ordinary mind
is the distinction between karma and primordial knowing. [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220)

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002484"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002484 -->
Furthermore, the abiding-wheel of cultivation:
through training the body with diverse bodily exercises,
ordinary mind comes to abide.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002485"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002485 -->
Through three applications, soothe the channels;
draw ordinary mind in gently.

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002486"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002486 -->
Through applications of signs and skillful craft—
through the distinct methods for body and ordinary mind,
make the yogin's ordinary mind abide. [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220)

[^G-U05094]

Earlier notes: [N-220](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002487"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002487 -->
Furthermore, cut the flow of deluded clinging.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002488"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002488 -->
Gather the light of sun and moon; draw in the ocean.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002489"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002489 -->
Weigh the Mount Merus of a three-thousandfold world on a balance.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002490"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002490 -->
Reverse the Ground of ignorance: self-awareness. [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221)

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002491"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002491 -->
Draw samsara out entirely; block the path of passing beyond sorrow.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002492"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002492 -->
Sweep away phenomena and persons.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002493"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002493 -->
The result is self-complete, the Ground of the nature of phenomena;
doing and acting self-cease.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002494"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002494 -->
Draw out from the root craving that desires another.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002495"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002495 -->
Delusion concerning phenomena makes an upper basis (yas gzhi). [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221)

[^G-U05106]

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002496"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002496 -->
That itself is the object liberated in its own place.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002497"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002497 -->
The three realms made the path, there is no buddha.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002498"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002498 -->
For whom could the teacher's teaching exist? It does not.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002499"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002499 -->
All phenomena are without a Ground; for whom could a root exist?

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002500"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002500 -->
Ordinary mind free from existence and without desire,
uncontrived in intrinsic nature, is beyond expression.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002501"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002501 -->
Deluded conceptual thought of doing and acting is exhausted.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002502"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002502 -->
There are no phenomena, no buddhas, and no sentient beings.

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002503"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002503 -->
There is no I, no self, no apprehended object and no apprehending subject. [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221)

Earlier notes: [N-221](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002504"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002504 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the one taste of the dharma embodiment.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002505"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002505 -->
It is not expressed in phrases;
there is not even mere abiding called ordinary mind.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002506"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002506 -->
Because it is absent, in the appearance of emptiness
the source of mindfulness and knowing is exhausted.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002507"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002507 -->
In the nature of phenomena in which nothing is done,
there is no arising. Such great wonder!
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002508"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002508 -->
In the dharma embodiment without arising,
there is no going. Such great wonder!
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002509"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002509 -->
In awareness with nowhere to circle,
there is the nature of phenomena without arising.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002510"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002510 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain gathering and evenness.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002511"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002511 -->
Rejecting multiplicity, one is free from convention.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002512"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002512 -->
One is not established; intrinsic nature is pure.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002513"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002513 -->
Intrinsic nature being clear, meaning is accomplished.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002514"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002514 -->
Self-purity and the foundation,
and the foundation mixed with samsara—
hollow and without a core:
called ‘ya ma rla,’ beyond indication. [N-222](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-222)

[^G-U05132]

Earlier notes: [N-222](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-222), [N-T107](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t107).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002515"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002515 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the delusion of intrinsic nature.

Earlier notes: [N-222](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-222).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002516"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002516 -->
Time and faculties,
distinctions, entry, and respective activities;
aggregates, elements, and sense bases—
in an instant when causes and conditions coincide,
like jewels and masses of cloud,
they circle like a body and its shadow.

Earlier notes: [N-222](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-222).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002517"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002517 -->
Through different dependencies upon the basis of delusion,
the activities of sentient beings are diverse. [N-222](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-222)

Earlier notes: [N-222](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-222).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002518"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002518 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain pure appearance.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002519"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002519 -->
The vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena
definitely emerges through the faculties' gates
and is clear in cloudless space.

Earlier notes: [N-223](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-223).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002520"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002520 -->
The vision of increasing experience:
the colors of primordial knowing come forth,
standing upright and arising as spokes,
as diverse spheres and embodiments,
clear in the domain of coarse appearance.

Earlier notes: [N-223](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-223).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002521"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002521 -->
The vision of awareness reaching its limit:
the complete enjoyment embodiment's marks and signs are clear;
from rainbows of indeterminate colors,
the five families appear as fathers and mothers.

Earlier notes: [N-223](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-223).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002522"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002522 -->
The vision of the exhaustion of nature of phenomena:
appearances of experience are emptied out,
the body is exhausted, and the objects of the faculties too are exhausted;
the assemblage of realizations and delusion is self-liberated, [N-223](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-223)
and there is freedom from expressed phrases.

Earlier notes: [N-223](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-223).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002523"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002523 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the sounds and signs of the elements.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002524"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002524 -->
When fire's sound crackles,
and sparks wave and proliferate,
the yogin does not accomplish the aim.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002525"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002525 -->
When it rises openly, blazing without interruption,
slow and long, with a sharp tip,
the yogin accomplishes a great aim. [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224)

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002526"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002526 -->
I shall also explain the colors.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002527"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002527 -->
When it is red with a black tip,
and pieces break off toward oneself,
obstacles occur.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002528"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002528 -->
White and clear, like a lotus;
sharp and of diverse colors;
wheels, parasols, weapon-points;
red, clear, limpid rays proliferating;
vajras, victory banners, bells,
checkerboard patterns, tents and houses,
tiered stupas, and so forth:
for the yogin, there is great completeness. [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224)

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002529"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002529 -->
Having directly perceived the meaning,
one passes beyond the three realms.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002530"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002530 -->
Furthermore, the subsidiary aspects of the elements:
water resounds with diverse sounds
and shows many white designs;
the yogin excellently accomplishes the aim.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002531"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002531 -->
Earth, through bouncing and ringing,
leaping at the edges and rolling, brings accomplishment.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002532"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002532 -->
Wind, through whistling and pressing,
and through being blue-green and billowing, does likewise. [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224)

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->
