<a id="dtg-002533"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002533 -->
If one has not met with such signs,
the adept repeats the rite and undertakes it again.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002534"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002534 -->
Through three, five, or seven times,
it will be accomplished; there is no doubt about this.

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002535"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002535 -->
Accord definitely with the respective signs. [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224)

Earlier notes: [N-224](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002536"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002536 -->
Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:
the fifth chapter, the Array of View and Cultivation, teaching the root of abiding.

[^G-U05197]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002537"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002537 -->
[Unresolved source graphic at the Chapter 5/6 boundary; no transcription supplied.]

[^G-C5-TRANSITION]
<!-- /pair -->

## Chapter 6

<a id="dtg-002538"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002538 -->
Then, although all phenomena are inexpressible,
from the many deluded conceptual thoughts of sentient beings,
also as a foundation for words, phrases, and conventions,
from uncontrived space,
phrases arise: the teacher abides in distinction. [N-225](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225)

Earlier notes: [N-225](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002539"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002539 -->
To the buddha arisen from the lotus,
Maker of Joy supplicated thus:
‘Kye kye! You who abide in the lotus's center,
here in the mandala of space,
sixty-four groups of a hundred thousand
drops of light appear.

Earlier notes: [N-225](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002540"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002540 -->
What is the cause of the seventeen utmost refinements?

Earlier notes: [N-225](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002541"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002541 -->
What is the dharma embodiment like?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002542"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002542 -->
To what is complete enjoyment embodiment applied?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002543"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002543 -->
How many emanation embodiments are there?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002544"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002544 -->
What are the characteristics of embodiment?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002545"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002545 -->
How does it abide?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002546"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002546 -->
What are the embodiments of intrinsic nature and the nature of phenomena?
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002547"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002547 -->
Blessed one, speak to me.’
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002548"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002548 -->
Then the blessed Vajradhara
rose from the lotus's center
and made the gesture of the sky-soarer.

Earlier notes: [N-225](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002549"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002549 -->
Merely by doing this,
from the space of the nature of phenomena's self-appearance,
from inexpressible intrinsic nature,
as expressed phrases, these words
arose naturally, without being made. [N-225](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225)

Earlier notes: [N-225](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002550"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002550 -->
[Source heading: First reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002551"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002551 -->
‘Kye kye! Lord of Gods, listen.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002552"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002552 -->
I shall truly explain the sixty-four
groups of a hundred thousand white lights.

Earlier notes: [N-226](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002553"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002553 -->
From the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature,
distinctions of phrases proliferate;
from three divisions, there are nine expanses.

Earlier notes: [N-226](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002554"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002554 -->
When phrases are gathered, there are ślokas:
sixty-four groups of a hundred thousand,
with thirty-five thousand chapters
and twenty-one thousand textual sections.

Earlier notes: [N-226](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226), [N-T108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002555"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002555 -->
What is expressed equals the bounds of space
and cuts the conceptual thoughts of beings to be trained. [N-226](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226)

Earlier notes: [N-226](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002556"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002556 -->
Here, when the subject matter is closely condensed,
there are a hundred and eighty fixings,
a thousand and a half crucial junctures,
three thousand gatherings of great crucial points,
four hundred thousand deviations and obscurations,
and twenty thousand named continua.

Earlier notes: [N-226](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226), [N-T108](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t108).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002557"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002557 -->
The sequences of expressed phrases are clearly arrayed. [N-226](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226)

Earlier notes: [N-226](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002558"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002558 -->
[Source heading: Second reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002559"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002559 -->
I shall explain the seventeen utmost refinements of light:
Self-Arising and Self-Liberation,
Naturally Arisen Itself and Complete at the Root,
Endowed with Beauty and Jeweled Array,
Garland of Pearls and Without Letters— [N-227](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-227)
Mirror of the Core and Mirror of Awakened Mind,
Union of Sun and Moon, Adorned with Pointing Out,
Blazing Embodiment-Relics, Heap of Jewels,
Blazing Lamp, and Six Great Expanses.

[^G-U05248]

Earlier notes: [N-227](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-227), [N-228](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-228).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002560"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002560 -->
Thus these sixteen sequences
definitely proliferate from Thal Gyur
and appear to sentient beings to be trained.

Earlier notes: [N-228](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-228).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002561"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002561 -->
By holding the teaching in which vital content is gathered,
one liberates from samsara for as long as ages exist,
and complete buddhahood manifests. [N-228](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-228)

Earlier notes: [N-228](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-228).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002562"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002562 -->
The dharma embodiment: speech and awakened mind,
qualities, and enlightened activity.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002563"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002563 -->
Embodiment is emptiness and clarity,
an intrinsic nature without signs.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002564"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002564 -->
Speech is free from words, phrases, and names,
free from expression and discussion.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002565"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002565 -->
Awakened mind, without thinking or movement,
is beyond proliferation and activity.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002566"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002566 -->
Its quality is neither coming back nor returning;
basic space and primordial knowing pervade,
and intrinsic nature is uncontrived.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002567"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002567 -->
Enlightened activity has not come about, arisen, or been done;
it comes about of its own force and does not cease.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002568"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002568 -->
Not done, there is no doing. [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229)

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002569"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002569 -->
[Source heading: Fourth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002570"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002570 -->
The complete enjoyment embodiment too has embodiment, speech, awakened mind,
qualities, and enlightened activity—five.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002571"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002571 -->
Embodiment appears but has no intrinsic nature.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002572"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002572 -->
Clarity and that which makes clear are pervaded by awareness.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002573"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002573 -->
Speech is naturally arisen and self-appearance,
a wheel of the aspects of proliferating and gathering.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002574"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002574 -->
Awakened mind is seeing without a flow,
the expressiveness of knowing, and enlightened intent. [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229)

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002575"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002575 -->
Qualities: the marks and signs are complete;
clear enlightened intent and higher knowing are complete.

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002576"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002576 -->
Enlightened activity: from the wheel of Dharma,
outer, inner, and secret [teachings] unfold,
and for the buddhas of the respective families
and for bodhisattvas too,
they unfold from the naturally arisen tongue,
showing the retinue through their own essence. [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229)

Earlier notes: [N-229](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002577"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002577 -->
[Source heading: Fifth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002578"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002578 -->
The emanation embodiment too has embodiment, speech, awakened mind,
qualities, and enlightened activity—five in each case.

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002579"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002579 -->
Embodiment, with marks and signs,
emanates bodies for whatever beings to be trained appear.

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002580"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002580 -->
Speech has excellent phrase-composition:
the six aspects of Brahmā's voice.

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002581"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002581 -->
Awakened mind, through primordial knowing of objects of knowledge,
abides in enlightened intent for the two benefits, one's own and others'.

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002582"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002582 -->
Qualities are completeness of knowing.

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002583"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002583 -->
Enlightened activity completes without remainder
the outer, inner, and secret deeds,
abiding in five perfections. [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230)

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002584"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002584 -->
[Source heading: Sixth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002585"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002585 -->
Embodiment's characteristics, essence,
definitive wording, and divisions;
the respective fields and beings to be trained;
the activities of differentiation and the measure of arising—
through distinctions in characteristics and understanding:
characteristics are established from phenomena,
established from desirable qualities,
and established from diverse methods.

[^G-U05309]

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002586"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002586 -->
Understanding: intrinsic nature is pure;
the respective domains of appearance are shown;
and, endowed with form, the faculties are complete. [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230)

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002587"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002587 -->
[Source heading: Seventh reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002588"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002588 -->
Embodiment's manner of abiding has three aspects:
because it is liberated from phenomena of signs,
it is not a form marked by divisions;
because the intrinsic nature of clarity is complete,
it appears but has no intrinsic nature;
to train the continua of those to be trained,
it abides unmoving in great equipoise.

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002589"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002589 -->
Unmoving and clear,
it appears as the aspect of awareness and knowing:
even, without partiality, and undivided. [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230)

Earlier notes: [N-230](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002590"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002590 -->
[Source heading: Eighth reply.]
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002591"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002591 -->
The intrinsic nature and nature of phenomena of embodiment:
intrinsic nature is emptiness and clarity,
the unceasing manifestation of knowing and awareness.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002592"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002592 -->
The nature of phenomena of embodiment is complete;
there is no return, going, or coming,
and no speaking, thinking, or expression.
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002593"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002593 -->
Furthermore, accomplishing embodiments:
through the distinctions taught above,
in one's own ordinary mind, the seed of the dharma embodiment,
apply the crucial point of direct perception,
and become constantly familiar with it.

Earlier notes: [N-231](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-231).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002594"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002594 -->
This is accomplishing the dharma embodiment.

Earlier notes: [N-231](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-231).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002595"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002595 -->
Train in desirable qualities, the seed of the complete enjoyment embodiment,
and become familiar with them:
this is accomplishing complete enjoyment.

Earlier notes: [N-231](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-231).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002596"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002596 -->
As for the seed of the emanation embodiment, the four elements:
train in their word and meaning;
through familiarity, the emanation embodiment is accomplished. [N-231](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-231)

Earlier notes: [N-231](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-231).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002597"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002597 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of fire's activities.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002598"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002598 -->
Distinguished through colors:
white, clear, and gentle accomplishes pacifying;
yellow, subtle, and long accomplishes increasing;
red, limpid, and proliferating is the activity of power;
blue-green, black, and short accomplishes the fierce.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002599"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002599 -->
When it is variegated, broken, and whirling,
distinctions of various activities are accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002600"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002600 -->
When it is extremely subtle, there is falling.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002601"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002601 -->
When broken and fierce, obstacles arise.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002602"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002602 -->
When spreading tongues of fire have smoke,
pledges deteriorate. [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232)

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002603"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002603 -->
Distinctions through sound:
three successive lhab lhab sounds and many spar spar sounds
accomplish diverse [activities].

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232), [N-T109](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t109).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002604"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002604 -->
Fierceness and crackling [accomplish] the fierce;
roaring and dense [sound accomplish] pacifying and increasing, the two;
resounding and ringing accomplish the supreme. [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232)

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002605"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002605 -->
Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of water's activities.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002606"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002606 -->
The definitive signs of colors:
through the four beginning with white,
pacifying and the rest are definitely accomplished.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002607"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002607 -->
When brown-black, rough, and whirling,
the yogin does not accomplish the aim.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002608"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002608 -->
When white and clear, rising and billowing,
the supreme accomplishment is definitely attained.

Earlier notes: [N-232](../translations/2026-10-01-golden-aligned/LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232).
<!-- /pair -->

<a id="dtg-002609"></a>

<!-- pair: DTG-002609 -->

In space in which nothing is done,

[^G-A2000-C01-S01]: **G-A2000-C01-S01 — A2000-C01-S01**

    Three scan-restored main verses after U01274. Penetration, dispersal, and equalizing continue the terminology already used in U01272–U01280. The source annotation at U01286 is an inherited omission query, not proof that this released passage is still missing.

    **Golden selected text / layer:** `"ཆུ་ཡི་ཟུག་པས་དབང་པོ་སྡུད།\nབྱེར་བ་ཡིས་ནི་འཁྲུགས་པར་བྱེད།\nསྙོམས་པ་ཡིས་ནི་འབྲས་བུ་འབྱིན།"`
